     8	--- a/xla/backends/gpu/runtime/ragged_all_to_all_thunk.h

    11	+#include <cstdlib>

    13	@@
    14	   // barrier/signal slots reserved when creating the device communicator (the
    15	   // kernel indexes its cooperative barrier by blockIdx.x, so registration
    16	   // must cover the launched grid). All ranks launch the same grid, which the
    17	   // cross-rank cooperative barriers require: every CTA must be resident
    18	   // simultaneously, so the size is kGridSmMultiplier CTAs of 128 threads per
    19	   // SM. Callers pass the SM count from
    20	   // se::DeviceDescription::core_count(); all participating ranks are expected
    21	   // to be homogeneous so every rank arrives at the same value.
    22	+  //
    23	+  // XLA_RAGGED_A2A_DK_CTAS_PER_SM (1..kGridSmMultiplier, default
    24	+  // kGridSmMultiplier) caps the per-SM CTA count so the kernel's resident
    25	+  // register/thread footprint can be traded against copy bandwidth: the
    26	+  // balanced copy is grid-size-agnostic (per-CTA element ranges and the GIN
    27	+  // put loop both derive from the launched grid), a regular (non-cooperative)
    28	+  // launch keeps a smaller grid trivially resident, and every barrier/signal
    29	+  // registration below routes through this same function, so launch and
    30	+  // registration stay consistent. The value must be identical on all ranks.
    31	   static int32_t DeviceKernelCtaCount(int core_count) {
    32	-    return std::max<int32_t>(kGridSmMultiplier * core_count,
    33	+    static const int32_t multiplier = [] {
    34	+      const char* env = std::getenv("XLA_RAGGED_A2A_DK_CTAS_PER_SM");
    35	+      const int parsed = env == nullptr ? 0 : std::atoi(env);
    36	+      return (parsed >= 1 && parsed <= kGridSmMultiplier)
    37	+                 ? parsed
    38	+                 : kGridSmMultiplier;
    39	+    }();
    40	+    return std::max<int32_t>(multiplier * core_count,
    41	                              kMinDeviceKernelCtaCount);
    42	   }

     4	"""Sideload a self-built jax-cuda13-pjrt wheel into the worker env at task start.
     5	
     6	The campaign env installs the locked PJRT wheel from pyproject; measurement arms that test an
     7	adhoc PJRT build replace exactly that one package before the task command runs. Everything else
     8	in the env stays as locked, so the arm differs from its control by the single wheel.
     9	"""
    10	
    11	
    12	def pjrt_wheel_install_script(wheel_url: str) -> str:
    13	    """Worker setup script fetching ``wheel_url`` (a ``.whl`` or a directory of one) and
    14	    reinstalling jax-cuda13-pjrt from it.
    15	
    16	    The URL is any fsspec-readable location; workers read object storage with their own
    17	    credentials, so an s3 research prefix works without presigning.
    18	    """
    19	    return f"""set -e
    20	: "${{IRIS_WORKDIR:?}}"
    21	: "${{IRIS_VENV:?}}"
    22	wheel_dir="$IRIS_WORKDIR/.marin-adhoc-pjrt"
    23	rm -rf "$wheel_dir"
    24	mkdir -p "$wheel_dir"
    25	echo 'downloading adhoc PJRT wheel'
    26	"$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" - <<'PY'
    27	import os
    28	from pathlib import Path
    29	
    30	import fsspec
    31	
    32	wheel_url = {wheel_url!r}
    33	wheel_dir = Path(os.environ["IRIS_WORKDIR"]) / ".marin-adhoc-pjrt"
    34	filesystem, remote_path = fsspec.core.url_to_fs(wheel_url)
    35	if remote_path.endswith(".whl"):
    36	    matches = [remote_path]
    37	else:
    38	    matches = filesystem.glob(remote_path.rstrip("/") + "/*.whl")
    39	if not matches:
    40	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"no .whl under {{wheel_url}}")
    41	for match in matches:
    42	    filesystem.get(match, str(wheel_dir / match.rsplit("/", 1)[1]))
    43	    print("fetched", match)
    44	PY
    45	echo 'reinstalling jax-cuda13-pjrt from the adhoc wheel'
    46	uv pip install --python "$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" --no-deps --reinstall "$wheel_dir"/*.whl
    47	"$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" -c \\
    48	  "from importlib.metadata import version; print('sideloaded jax-cuda13-pjrt', version('jax-cuda13-pjrt'))"

## 2026-08-29 ~18:40Z: lever #3 investigation — a2a dk SM footprint (H11)

Mechanism fully identified (agent, all file:line at the pinned fork rev):
DeviceKernelCtaCount = 8 x core_count (ragged_all_to_all_thunk.h:198-201,
min 8, threads 128) -> 1184 CTAs on GB200 = 8 resident CTAs x 148 SMs,
64 regs/thread via __launch_bounds__(128,8) = the ENTIRE sm_100 register
file, held for the whole op INCLUDING barrier spins. This is the +550
ms/step GEMM contention. No env var, no debug option reaches the grid
(exhaustively enumerated); TF_CPP_VMODULE=ragged_all_to_all_thunk=3
prints the live cta_count.

H11 = one-header patch: env-gated multiplier XLA_RAGGED_A2A_DK_CTAS_PER_SM
(1..8, DEFAULT 8 = today's behavior) in DeviceKernelCtaCount; all
consumers (launch, barrier/signal registration) route through the one
function so consistency is automatic; kernel is grid-size-agnostic
(balanced ranges + strided GIN loop, regular launch, no cooperative
assumptions). Default-identity means the rebuilt wheel is bit-comparable
for every existing arm — only env-carrying arms diverge. BDP estimate:
4/SM marginal-OK for the copy phase; the barrier-spin share shrinks
linearly at zero bandwidth cost. Requires one jax-cuda13-pjrt rebuild
(~3.5h) on the marin-community/xla fork + release + pin swap.
No-rebuild alternatives all dominated: one-shot and NCCL-fallback paths
lose ~0.5 MFU on prior same-night data (host marshalling per chunk-op);
stream-priority flip risks re-exposing the a2a; MPS/green contexts
inapplicable.

GATE: pushing a branch/release to marin-community/xla is an
external-repo write -> per standing instruction, needs user approval.
Patch + review will be prepared so the build can start on a word.

   583	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   584	class EnvironmentConfig:
   585	    """Job environment configuration.
   586	
   587	    Args:
   588	        workspace: Path to workspace root for uv-based execution
   589	        docker_image: Docker image for containerized execution
   590	        pip_packages: Additional pip packages to install
   591	        env_vars: Environment variables to set
   592	        extras: Extra dependency groups for uv (e.g., ["tpu", "eval"])
   593	        setup_scripts: Setup scripts run in order before the command. ``None``
   594	            builds the default uv-sync script; a list runs verbatim (``[]`` => no
   595	            setup, image used as-is). Either way iris appends its own runtime-deps
   596	            script. Build the default and tweak it via ``fray.default_setup_script``.
   597	        sync_packages: Workspace members the default sync targets (default: all).
   598	    """
   599	
   600	    workspace: str | None = None
   601	    docker_image: str | None = None
   602	    pip_packages: Sequence[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   603	    env_vars: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   604	    extras: Sequence[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   605	    setup_scripts: Sequence[str] | None = None
   606	    sync_packages: Sequence[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   607	
   608	    def __post_init__(self):
   609	        if self.workspace and self.docker_image:
   610	            raise ValueError("Cannot specify both workspace and docker_image")
   611	        if not self.workspace and not self.docker_image:
   612	            raise ValueError("Must specify either workspace or docker_image")
   613	
   614	
   615	def create_environment(
   616	    workspace: str | None = None,

   619	    env_vars: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   620	    extras: Sequence[str] | None = None,
   621	    setup_scripts: Sequence[str] | None = None,
   622	    sync_packages: Sequence[str] | None = None,
   623	) -> EnvironmentConfig:
   624	    """Create an EnvironmentConfig with sensible defaults.
   625	
   209	def wrap_multiprocess(entrypoint: IrisEntrypoint, resources: ResourceSpec, processes_per_task: int) -> IrisEntrypoint:
   210	    """Prepend the multigpu supervisor so each task runs ``processes_per_task`` GPU processes.
   211	
   212	    iris runs the entrypoint verbatim, so fray applies the hook here. Requires a GPU
   213	    device whose count is divisible by ``processes_per_task``.
   214	    """
   215	    hook = build_multigpu_hook(resources, processes_per_task)
   216	    return IrisEntrypoint(
   217	        command=hook.wrap(entrypoint.command),
   218	        workdir_files=entrypoint.workdir_files,
   219	        workdir_file_refs=entrypoint.workdir_file_refs,
   220	    )
   221	
   222	
   223	def convert_environment(env: EnvironmentConfig | None, device: DeviceConfig | None = None) -> EnvironmentSpec | None:
   224	    """Convert fray EnvironmentConfig to Iris EnvironmentSpec."""
   225	    env_vars = dict(env.env_vars) if env is not None else {}
   226	    if device is not None:
   227	        for key, value in device.default_env_vars().items():
   228	            env_vars.setdefault(key, value)
   229	    if env is None and not env_vars:
   230	        return None
   231	    return EnvironmentSpec(
   232	        pip_packages=list(env.pip_packages) if env is not None else [],
   233	        env_vars=env_vars,
   234	        extras=list(env.extras) if env is not None else [],
   235	        setup_scripts=list(env.setup_scripts) if env is not None and env.setup_scripts is not None else None,
   236	        sync_packages=list(env.sync_packages) if env is not None else [],
   237	    )

   391	    def __getattr__(self, method_name: str) -> "_IrisActorMethod":
   392	        if method_name.startswith("_"):
   393	            raise AttributeError(method_name)
   394	        return _IrisActorMethod(self, method_name)

   396	
   397	class OperationFuture:
   398	    """Polling-based future backed by an Iris long-running operation.
   399	
   400	    Satisfies the ``ActorFuture`` protocol. Each call to ``result()`` polls
   401	    the server via short ``GetOperation`` RPCs until the operation completes,
   402	    fails, or the caller's timeout expires.
   403	    """
   404	
   405	    def __init__(self, client: ActorClient, operation_id: str, poll_interval: float = 1.0):
   406	        self._client = client
   407	        self._op_id = operation_id
   408	        self._poll_interval = poll_interval
   409	
   410	    def result(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> Any:
   411	        deadline = None if timeout is None else time.monotonic() + timeout
   412	        while True:
   413	            op = self._client.poll_operation_status(self._op_id)
   414	
   415	            if op.state == actor_pb2.Operation.SUCCEEDED:
   416	                return cloudpickle.loads(op.serialized_result)
   417	
   418	            if op.state == actor_pb2.Operation.FAILED:
   419	                if op.error.serialized_exception:
   420	                    raise cloudpickle.loads(op.error.serialized_exception)
   421	                raise RuntimeError(f"{op.error.error_type}: {op.error.message}")
   422	
   423	            if op.state == actor_pb2.Operation.CANCELLED:
   424	                raise RuntimeError(f"Operation {self._op_id} was cancelled")
   425	
   426	            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
   427	                self._client.cancel_operation(self._op_id)
   428	                raise TimeoutError(f"Operation {self._op_id} timed out after {timeout}s")
   429	
   430	            time.sleep(self._poll_interval)
   431	
   432	
   433	class _ThreadFuture:
   434	    """Future backed by a daemon thread running a direct Call RPC.
   435	
   436	    Unlike OperationFuture, this holds a single HTTP connection for the
   437	    call duration and does not poll. Suitable for short RPCs where the
   438	    overhead of StartOperation + GetOperation polling is unnecessary.
   439	    """
   440	
   441	    def __init__(self, fn: Any, args: tuple, kwargs: dict):
   442	        self._future: Future[Any] = Future()
   443	
   444	        def run() -> None:
   445	            try:
   446	                self._future.set_result(fn(*args, **kwargs))
   447	            except Exception as e:
   448	                self._future.set_exception(e)
   449	
   450	        threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()
   451	
   452	    def result(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> Any:
   453	        return self._future.result(timeout=timeout)
   454	
   455	
   456	def _call_actor_method(method: Any, args: tuple, kwargs: dict) -> Any:
   457	    try:
   458	        return method(*args, **kwargs)
   459	    except ConnectError as exc:
   460	        if is_retryable_error(exc):
   461	            raise ActorUnavailableError(str(exc)) from exc
   462	        raise
   463	
   464	
   465	class _IrisActorMethod:
   466	    """Wraps a method on an Iris actor.
   467	
   468	    ``remote()`` spawns a thread running a direct ``Call`` RPC — fast
   469	    (single RPC) and suitable for short-lived methods.
   470	
   471	    ``submit()`` uses long-running operations: a fast ``StartOperation``
   472	    RPC returns an operation ID, and ``OperationFuture.result()`` polls
   473	    via ``GetOperation``. Use for methods that run for minutes/hours.
   474	
   475	    ``__call__()`` uses the blocking ``Call`` RPC synchronously.
   476	    """
   477	
   478	    def __init__(self, handle: IrisActorHandle, method_name: str):
   479	        self._handle = handle
   480	        self._method = method_name
   481	
   482	    def remote(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> ActorFuture:
   483	        client = self._handle._resolve()
   484	        method = getattr(client, self._method)
   485	        return _ThreadFuture(_call_actor_method, (method, args, kwargs), {})
   486	
   487	    def submit(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> ActorFuture:
   488	        client = self._handle._resolve()
   489	        op_id = _call_actor_method(client.start_operation, (self._method, *args), kwargs)
   490	        return OperationFuture(client, op_id)
   491	
   492	    def __call__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   493	        client = self._handle._resolve()
   494	        return _call_actor_method(getattr(client, self._method), args, kwargs)
   495	
   496	
   497	class IrisActorGroup:
   498	    """ActorGroup that polls the Iris resolver to discover actors as they start."""
   499	
   500	    def __init__(self, name: str, count: int, job_id: Any, client: IrisClientLib | None = None):
   501	        """Args:
   502	        name: Actor name prefix
   503	        count: Number of actors to discover
   504	        job_id: JobId/JobName for the actor job
   505	        client: Client for driver-side discovery. Serialized groups use the Iris job context.
   506	        """
   507	        self._name = name
   508	        self._count = count
   509	        self._job_id = job_id
   510	        self._client = client
   511	        self._handles: list[ActorHandle] = []
   512	        self._discovered_names: set[str] = set()
   513	
   514	    def __getstate__(self) -> dict:
   515	        # Serialize only the discovery parameters - discovery state resets on deserialize
   516	        return {
   517	            "name": self._name,
   518	            "count": self._count,
   519	            "job_id": self._job_id,
   520	        }
    20	
    21	# `JobRequest.priority` is the Iris priority band as a bare int. INHERIT is Iris's own default.
    22	INHERIT_PRIORITY = priority_band_value("inherit")
    23	
    24	# Runtime-tuning env vars forwarded from the dispatcher to the train tasks.
    25	# Iris tasks don't inherit the submitter's shell, so anything the launcher was
    26	# given (e.g. `iris job run -e XLA_FLAGS ...`) must be re-exported explicitly.
    27	# JAX_PLATFORMS is excluded: the dispatcher runs CPU-only and its value must
    28	# not leak onto accelerator tasks.
    29	_FORWARDED_ENV_PREFIXES = ("XLA_", "LIBTPU_INIT_ARGS", "NCCL_", "JAX_", "MALLOC_")
    30	# TF_CPP_* control XLA's C++ logging; measurement harnesses forward them to read compile-time
    31	# scheduler/remat evidence (memory limits, remat counts) out of the task logs.
    32	_FORWARDED_ENV_NAMES = ("LD_PRELOAD", "TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL", "TF_CPP_VMODULE")
    33	_FORWARDED_ENV_EXCLUDE = ("JAX_PLATFORMS",)
    34	
    35	
    36	def _forwarded_env_vars() -> dict[str, str]:
    37	    return {
    38	        k: v
    39	        for k, v in os.environ.items()
    40	        if (k in _FORWARDED_ENV_NAMES or k.startswith(_FORWARDED_ENV_PREFIXES)) and k not in _FORWARDED_ENV_EXCLUDE
    41	    }
    42	
    43	
    44	def _safe_job_suffix(run_id: str) -> str:
    45	    """Sanitize run IDs into Fray/Iris-safe job-name suffixes."""
    46	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "-", run_id)
    47	
    48	
    49	def dispatch_grug_training_run(
    50	    *,
    51	    run_id: str,
    52	    config: ConfigT,
    53	    local_entrypoint: Callable[[ConfigT], None],
    54	    resources: ResourceConfig,
    55	    max_retries_failure: int = 3,
    56	    max_task_failures: int = 10,
    57	    processes_per_task: int = 1,
    58	    priority: int = INHERIT_PRIORITY,
    59	    setup_scripts: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    60	) -> None:
    61	    """Submit a grug train entrypoint through Fray and wait for completion.
    62	
    63	    ``INHERIT_PRIORITY`` takes the submitting job's band, or ``interactive`` when the submitter is
    64	    not itself an Iris job -- which is the case for a launcher run from a dev box.
    65	
    66	    ``max_retries_failure`` is the per-task retry budget and ``max_task_failures`` is the
    67	    cumulative one. The job fails when either is exhausted, so raise the two together: a large
    68	    per-task budget under a small cumulative one still ends the job at the cumulative limit.
    69	    """
    70	    safe_run_id = _safe_job_suffix(run_id)
    71	    env_vars = resolve_training_env(base_env=_forwarded_env_vars(), resources=resources)
    72	    request = JobRequest(
    73	        name=f"grug-train-{safe_run_id}",
    74	        entrypoint=Entrypoint.from_callable(local_entrypoint, args=[config]),
    75	        resources=resources,
    76	        environment=create_environment(
    77	            env_vars=env_vars,
    78	            extras=extras_for_resources(resources),
    79	            setup_scripts=list(setup_scripts) if setup_scripts else None,
    80	        ),
    81	        max_retries_failure=max_retries_failure,
    82	        max_task_failures=max_task_failures,
    83	        processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
    84	        priority=priority,
    85	    )
    86	    logger.info("Dispatching grug training via Fray: %s", request.name)
    87	    job = current_client().submit(request)
    88	    job.wait(raise_on_failure=True)
   330	
   331	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   332	class GrugRunConfig:
   333	    """Top-level config for grug training."""
   334	
   335	    model: GrugModelConfig
   336	    data: LmDataConfig
   337	    resources: ResourceConfig
   338	    tensorstore_cache_bytes: int | None = None
   339	    optimizer: OptimizerConfig = field(default_factory=AdamConfig)
   340	    trainer: GrugTrainerConfig = field(default_factory=GrugTrainerConfig)
   341	    eval: GrugEvalConfig | None = field(default_factory=GrugEvalConfig)
   342	    # Stop after this many steps while `trainer.num_train_steps` still sizes the learning-rate
   343	    # schedule. Warmup and decay are fractions of `num_train_steps`, so training the head of a
   344	    # long schedule requires the two to differ. None runs the whole schedule.
   345	    stop_after_steps: int | None = None
   346	    # GPU processes per task: > 1 runs one JAX process per GPU (multi-controller)
   347	    # via the iris.hooks.multigpu_main supervisor instead of one process per node.
   348	    processes_per_task: int = 1
   349	    # Retry budgets for the training job. The two are separate gates and the job fails when either
   350	    # one trips, thus raise them together. The defaults make a failure terminal, which is what a run
   351	    # that cannot resume wants: a retry would repeat it from step 0. Only a run that both saves and
   352	    # restores checkpoints benefits from a deep budget.
   353	    max_retries_failure: int = 0
   354	    max_task_failures: int = 10
   355	    # Shell scripts run on each worker before the task command, in order. Measurement harnesses
   356	    # use this to sideload a self-built jax-cuda13-pjrt wheel; empty means the bundled env runs
   357	    # exactly as locked.
   358	    worker_setup_scripts: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   359	
   360	
   361	def build_train_dataset(
   362	    data_config: LmDataConfig,
   900	
   901	    trainer = config.trainer.trainer
   902	    trainer.initialize()
   903	    levanter.tracker.log_configuration(config)
   904	
   905	    run_id = trainer.id
   906	    if run_id is None:
   907	        raise ValueError("trainer.id was not initialized")
   908	
   909	    optimizer = config.optimizer.build(trainer.num_train_steps)
   910	    watch_config = trainer.watch
   911	    diagnostic_watch_step = None
   912	    inline_watch_config = watch_config if watch_config.is_enabled else None
   913	    if watch_config.is_enabled and config.trainer.watch_mode == WatchMode.DIAGNOSTIC:
   914	        diagnostic_watch_step = _make_diagnostic_watch_step(
   915	            trainer.mp,
   916	            z_loss_weight=config.trainer.z_loss_weight,
   917	            watch_config=watch_config,
   918	        )
   919	        inline_watch_config = None
   920	    train_step = _make_train_step(
   921	        optimizer,
   922	        trainer.mp,
   923	        z_loss_weight=config.trainer.z_loss_weight,
   924	        ema_beta=config.trainer.ema_beta,
   925	        watch_config=inline_watch_config,
   926	        offload_opt_state=config.trainer.offload_opt_state,
   927	        master_param_mode=config.trainer.master_param_mode,
   928	    )
   929	
   930	    data_key, model_key = jax.random.split(jax.random.PRNGKey(trainer.seed), 2)
   931	    if config.trainer.data_seed is not None:
   932	        data_key = jax.random.PRNGKey(config.trainer.data_seed)
   933	
   934	    # Grug uses raw PartitionSpecs rather than Trainer's logical axis mapping.
   935	    # Keep the mesh compact so the batch pspec derived by `_batch_spec(mesh)` spans slices directly.
   936	    # replica_axis_size=None lets compact_grug_mesh default to jax.process_count() (full
   937	    # cross-slice replication); set it to 1 on GrugTrainerConfig for cross-slice FSDP.
   938	    mesh = compact_grug_mesh(
   939	        expert_axis_size=config.trainer.expert_axis_size,
   940	        replica_axis_size=config.trainer.replica_axis_size,
   941	    )
   942	    # Armed before the state is built or restored. The watchdog's step and process deadlines only
   943	    # arm once a step reports progress, so its startup deadline is the only thing bounding a stall
   944	    # in initialization, checkpoint restore, cache construction or compilation.
   945	    progress_watchdog = trainer.progress_watchdog.create(process_index=jax.process_index())
   946	
   947	    checkpointer = trainer.checkpointer.create(run_id) if config.trainer.save_checkpoints else None
   948	    dashboard = (
   949	        TrainingDashboard(config, checkpointer.request_checkpoint, run_id) if checkpointer is not None else nullcontext()
   950	    )
   951	    with set_mesh(mesh), dashboard:
   952	        batch_schedule = trainer.batch_schedule
   953	
   954	        @jax.jit
   955	        def _init_state(model_rng):
   956	            return initial_state(
   957	                config.model,
   958	                optimizer=optimizer,
   959	                mp=trainer.mp,
   960	                key=model_rng,
   961	                ema_beta=config.trainer.ema_beta,
   962	                offload_opt_state=config.trainer.offload_opt_state,
   963	                master_param_mode=config.trainer.master_param_mode,
   964	            )
   965	
   966	        state = _init_state(model_key)
   967	        released_initial_state = trainer.load_checkpoint is not False and not trainer.allow_partial_checkpoint
   968	        if released_initial_state:
   969	            state = restore_template_from(state)
   970	
  1040	                        max_seq_len=config.model.max_seq_len,
  1041	                        mesh=dropless_eval_mesh,
  1042	                        eval_cfg=eval_cfg,
  1043	                        mp=trainer.mp,
  1044	                        model_transform=functools.partial(
  1045	                            _to_dropless_local,
  1046	                            implementation=eval_cfg.dropless_eval_moe_implementation,
  1047	                        ),
  1048	                    )
  1049	
  1050	        # `trainer.num_train_steps` sizes the schedule; this bounds the run. Progress and the loop
  1051	        # both use it so a head-of-schedule run reports against the steps it will actually take.
  1052	        requested_stop_step = trainer.num_train_steps if config.stop_after_steps is None else config.stop_after_steps
  1053	        stop_step = min(requested_stop_step, trainer.num_train_steps)
  1054	
  1055	        profiler_cfg = trainer.profiler
  1056	        profiler_num_steps = profiler_cfg.resolve_num_profile_steps(num_train_steps=stop_step)
  1057	        profiler_enabled = profiler_cfg.is_enabled and profiler_num_steps > 0
  1058	
  1059	        log_every = max(1, config.trainer.log_every)
  1060	        if config.trainer.training_data_mode == TrainingDataMode.SYNTHETIC:
  1061	            synthetic_batch = _make_synthetic_batch(
  1062	                batch_size=batch_schedule.batch_size_at_step(int(state.step)),
  1063	                max_seq_len=config.model.max_seq_len,
  1064	                vocab_size=config.model.vocab_size,
  1065	                seed=trainer.seed + 1 if config.trainer.data_seed is None else config.trainer.data_seed,
  1066	                mesh=mesh,
  1067	            )
  1068	            batch_source = itertools.repeat(synthetic_batch)
  1069	        else:
  1070	            assert train_loader is not None
  1071	            batch_source = train_loader.iter_from_step(int(state.step))
  1072	        iterator = LoadingTimeTrackerIterator(batch_source)
  1073	
  1074	        state_callbacks = StateCallbackRunner[GrugTrainState](
  1075	            step_getter=lambda s: s.step,
  1076	            model_getter=lambda s: s.params,
  1077	            eval_model_getter=lambda s: s.ema_params if s.ema_params is not None else s.params,
  1078	            opt_state_getter=lambda s: s.opt_state,
  1079	        )
  1080	        if progress_watchdog is not None:
  1081	            state_callbacks.add_hook(progress_watchdog, every=1)
  1082	        state_callbacks.add_hook(
  1083	            callbacks.log_performance_stats(config.model.max_seq_len, batch_schedule, flops_per_example),
  1084	            every=log_every,
  1085	        )
  1086	        state_callbacks.add_hook(callbacks.pbar_logger(total=stop_step), every=log_every)
  1087	        state_callbacks.add_hook(callbacks.log_step_info(stop_step), every=log_every)
  1088	        if profiler_enabled:
  1089	            state_callbacks.add_hook(
  1090	                profiler_cfg.build(
  1091	                    str(trainer.log_dir / run_id / "profiler"),
  1092	                    run_id=run_id,
  1093	                    num_steps=profiler_num_steps,
  1094	                ),
  1095	                every=1,
  1096	            )
  1097	        if train_dataset is not None:
  1098	            state_callbacks.add_hook(_make_mixture_stage_callback(train_dataset, batch_schedule), every=1)
  1099	        state_callbacks.add_hook(log_device_memory, every=1)
  1100	        if evaluator is not None and eval_cfg is not None:
  1101	            interval = eval_cfg.steps_per_eval
  1102	            eval_ema = eval_cfg.eval_ema and config.trainer.ema_beta is not None
  1103	            if eval_cfg.eval_current or eval_ema:
  1104	                tagged_eval_hook = cb_tagged_evaluate(
  1105	                    evaluator,
  1106	                    prefix=eval_cfg.prefix,
  1107	                    eval_current=eval_cfg.eval_current,
  1108	                    eval_ema=eval_ema,
  1109	                )
  1110	                eval_hooks: list[Callable[..., None]] = [tagged_eval_hook]
  1240	                    checkpointer.on_step(tree=state, step=int(state.step), force=True)
  1241	                    checkpointer.wait_until_finished()
  1242	        finally:
  1243	            state_callbacks.emit_event(callbacks.ProgressEvent.TRAINING_FINISHED)
  1244	
  1245	    levanter.tracker.current_tracker().finish()
  1246	
  1247	
  1248	def run_grug(config: GrugRunConfig) -> None:
  1249	    """Dispatch grug training through Fray jobs."""
  1250	    trainer = config.trainer.trainer
  1251	    if trainer.id is None:
  1252	        raise ValueError("trainer.id must be set before dispatching grug training.")
  1253	
  1254	    # Dispatch snapshots os.environ for the child task, so apply the hero defaults first.
  1255	    inline_watch_enabled = trainer.watch.is_enabled and config.trainer.watch_mode == WatchMode.INLINE
  1256	    _apply_hero_ep_runtime_defaults(
  1257	        inline_watch_enabled=inline_watch_enabled,
  1258	        processes_per_task=config.processes_per_task,
  1259	        moe_implementation=config.model.moe_implementation,
  1260	        remat_mode=config.model.remat_mode,
  1261	    )
  1262	    dispatch_grug_training_run(
  1263	        run_id=trainer.id,
  1264	        config=config,
  1265	        local_entrypoint=_run_grug_local,
  1266	        resources=config.resources,
  1267	        processes_per_task=config.processes_per_task,
  1268	        max_retries_failure=config.max_retries_failure,
  1269	        max_task_failures=config.max_task_failures,
  1270	        setup_scripts=config.worker_setup_scripts,
  1271	    )
  1272	
  1273	
  1274	__all__ = [
  1275	    "GrugEvalConfig",
    60	DEFAULT_HERO_STEPS = 25
    61	HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL = timedelta(minutes=15)

    63	
    64	def build_diagnostic_run(
    65	    *,
    66	    run_id: str,
    67	    restore_from: str | None = None,
    68	    pjrt_wheel: str | None = None,
    69	    dp_racks: int,
    70	    num_steps: int,
    71	    schedule_steps: int | None = None,
    72	    seed: int = 0,
    73	    batch_size: int = HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
    74	    num_experts: int | None = None,
    75	    num_experts_per_token: int | None = None,
    76	    intermediate_dim: int | None = None,
    77	    capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    78	    latent_dim: int | None = None,
    79	    moe_implementation: str | None = None,
    80	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
    81	    processes_per_task: int = HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
    82	    eval_every: int = 0,
    83	    save_checkpoints: bool = False,
    84	    checkpoint_interval: timedelta = HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL,
    85	    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,
    86	    checkpoint_debug: CheckpointDebugConfig | None = None,
    87	    watch_interval: int = HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
    88	    watch_mode: WatchMode = WatchMode.INLINE,
    89	    profile_steps: int = 0,
    90	    profile_start_step: int = 5,
    91	    training_data_mode: TrainingDataMode = TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE,
    92	    version: str | None = None,
    93	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
    94	    """Build a bounded diagnostic run for the production EP64 hero recipe.
    95	
    96	    The overrides sweep expert count, expert width, routed top-k, and routing capacity from the
    97	    hero spec. They keep the hidden dimension, so the compute-scaled optimizer values stay
    98	    comparable across a sweep. ``None`` keeps the hero value.
    99	
   100	    ``batch_size`` is the global batch across all data-parallel racks. It does not scale with
   101	    ``dp_racks``. ``batch_size`` and ``schedule_steps`` change the token budget for the heuristic.
   102	    """
   103	    if not run_id.strip():
   104	        raise ValueError("run_id must not be empty")
   105	    if dp_racks <= 0:
   270	            resources=ctx.runtime_arg("train_resources"),
   271	            tensorstore_cache_bytes=HERO_TENSORSTORE_CACHE_BYTES,
   272	            optimizer=optimizer,
   273	            trainer=dataclasses.replace(grug_trainer, trainer=trainer),
   274	            # Off by default so a throughput run stays a throughput run. Turn it on to make a
   275	            # run scoreable: comparing configs needs held-out loss, not train loss.
   276	            eval=(
   277	                GrugEvalConfig(
   278	                    steps_per_eval=eval_every,
   279	                    eval_batch_size=HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE * dp_racks,
   280	                    eval_ema=False,
   281	                    compute_bpb=True,
   282	                    dropless_eval=True,
   283	                )
   284	                if eval_every > 0
   285	                else None
   286	            ),
   287	            stop_after_steps=num_steps,
   288	            processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
   289	            worker_setup_scripts=((pjrt_wheel_install_script(pjrt_wheel),) if pjrt_wheel is not None else ()),
   290	        )
   291	
   292	    return ArtifactStep(
   293	        name=user_namespaced_name(name, version),
   294	        version=version,
   295	        artifact_type=HeroThroughputResult,
   296	        run=run_grug,
   297	        build_config=build_config,
   298	        deps=(HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_STORE, *validation),
   299	        runtime_args={"train_resources": train_resources},
   300	    )
   430	    show_default=True,
   431	    help="Steps between gradient and parameter norm logs. 0 disables norm logging.",
   432	)
   433	@click.option(
   434	    "--watch-mode",
   435	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in WatchMode]),
   436	    default=WatchMode.INLINE.value,
   437	    show_default=True,
   438	    help="Compute norms in the training step or in a separate forward and backward diagnostic step.",
   439	)
   440	@click.option(
   441	    "--profile-steps",
   442	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   443	    default=0,
   444	    show_default=True,
   445	    help="Steps to trace with XProf on rank 0. 0 disables the profiler.",
   446	)
   447	@click.option(
   448	    "--profile-start-step",
   449	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   450	    default=5,
   451	    show_default=True,
   452	    help="First traced step. Keep it past compile and warmup.",
   453	)
   454	@click.option(
   455	    "--training-data",
   456	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in TrainingDataMode]),
   457	    default=TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE.value,
   458	    show_default=True,
   459	    help="Use the configured mixture or reuse a deterministic synthetic batch without opening TensorStore.",
   460	)
   461	@click.option(
   462	    "--capacity-factor",
   463	    type=click.FloatRange(min=0, min_open=True),
   464	    default=HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.capacity_factor,
   465	    show_default=True,
   466	    help="Override the pooled receiver capacity factor.",
   467	)
   468	@click.option(
   469	    "--restore-from",
   470	    default=None,
   471	    help="Initialize from another run's checkpoint directory, read-only.",
   472	)
   473	@click.option(
   474	    "--pjrt-wheel",
   475	    default=None,
   476	    help="fsspec URL of a self-built jax-cuda13-pjrt wheel (or a directory holding one); workers "
   477	    "reinstall exactly that package before the task starts.",
   478	)
   479	@build_options
   480	def main(
   481	    run_id: str,
   482	    restore_from: str | None,
   483	    pjrt_wheel: str | None,
   484	    dp_racks: int,
   485	    num_steps: int,
   486	    schedule_steps: int | None,
   487	    seed: int,
   488	    batch_size: int,
   489	    num_experts: int | None,
   490	    num_experts_per_token: int | None,
   491	    intermediate_dim: int | None,
   492	    capacity_factor: float | None,
   493	    latent_dim: int | None,
   494	    moe_implementation: str | None,
   495	    master_params: str,
   496	    processes_per_task: int,
   497	    save_checkpoints: bool,
   498	    checkpoint_minutes: float,
   499	    checkpoint_path: str | None,
   500	    checkpoint_debug: bool,
   501	    eval_every: int,
   502	    watch_interval: int,
   503	    watch_mode: str,
   504	    profile_steps: int,
   505	    profile_start_step: int,
   506	    training_data: str,
   507	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
   508	    return build_diagnostic_run(
   509	        run_id=run_id,
   510	        restore_from=restore_from,
   511	        pjrt_wheel=pjrt_wheel,
   512	        dp_racks=dp_racks,
   513	        num_steps=num_steps,
   514	        schedule_steps=schedule_steps,
   515	        seed=seed,
   516	        batch_size=batch_size,
   517	        num_experts=num_experts,

The Fray boundary already exposes the key ordering hazard: providing any non-empty worker script replaces Fray’s default uv-sync script, while Iris still appends its runtime-dependencies step. I’m now checking the Iris worker implementation to determine whether that appended step can reinstall the locked PJRT package, and the multigpu/watchdog paths for mismatch behavior.

     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Build the per-task setup scripts that prepare a worker's environment.
     5	
     6	Client-side helpers: the submitter resolves the scripts and ships them to the
     7	worker, which runs them in order before the command. The default environment is
     8	two distinct scripts so iris's requirements stay separate from the user's project:
     9	
    10	- ``default_setup_script`` syncs the user's workspace (``uv sync`` + extras + pip).
    11	- ``iris_runtime_setup_script`` installs iris's runtime deps (cloudpickle for
    12	  callable entrypoints, py-spy/memray for the profiler) into the same venv.
    13	
    14	A caller can pass its own scripts to bypass both; an empty list means no setup at
    15	all (bring-your-own image).
    16	
    17	The scripts run with the task's ``IRIS_*`` environment available and populate the
    18	venv at ``$IRIS_VENV`` without activating it. The run phase activates ``$IRIS_VENV``
    19	if it exists, so a setup that leaves no venv runs in the image's own environment.
    20	"""
    21	
    22	import shlex
    23	from collections.abc import Sequence
    24	
    25	# cloudpickle for callable entrypoints, py-spy/memray for the profiler attach paths.
    26	_IRIS_RUNTIME_DEPS = ("cloudpickle", "py-spy", "memray")
    27	
    28	
    29	def _uv_sync_target(packages: Sequence[str] | None) -> str:
    30	    """Return the uv-sync package selector: every member, or a scoped subset."""
    31	    if not packages:
    32	        return "--all-packages"
    33	    return " ".join(f"--package {shlex.quote(p)}" for p in packages)
    34	
    35	
    36	def _extra_flags(extras: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    37	    """Render ``--extra`` flags. Accepts ``extra`` or ``package:extra`` syntax.
    38	
    39	    The package prefix is dropped; ``--extra`` applies to whichever member
    40	    defines that extra name.
    41	    """
    42	    flags: list[str] = []
    43	    for e in extras:
    44	        extra = e.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in e else e
    45	        flags.extend(["--extra", shlex.quote(extra)])
    46	    return " ".join(flags)
    47	
    48	
    49	def default_setup_script(
    50	    *,
    51	    extras: Sequence[str] = (),
    52	    pip_packages: Sequence[str] = (),
    53	    python_version: str | None = None,
    54	    packages: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    55	) -> str:
    56	    """Render the standard uv-based setup script as a bash string.
    57	
    58	    uv runs at its default verbosity so its progress (``Resolved``,
    59	    ``Downloading <pkg>``, ``Installed``) streams into the task logs; this is the
    60	    only signal a live setup gives, so it is never suppressed.
    61	
    62	    Args:
    63	        extras: uv extras to enable (``extra`` or ``package:extra``).
    64	        pip_packages: extra packages to ``uv pip install`` after the sync.
    65	        python_version: pin the interpreter (matches the client for cloudpickle
    66	            compatibility); omitted when empty.
    67	        packages: workspace members to sync. ``None`` syncs every member
    68	            (``--all-packages``); a list scopes the sync to those members so an
    69	            unrelated member that fails to resolve cannot fail the job. The
    70	            development dependency group is omitted in both cases.
    71	
    72	    Returns:
    73	        A bash snippet that creates and populates the venv at ``$IRIS_VENV``.
    74	    """
    75	    python_flag = f"--python {shlex.quote(python_version)}" if python_version else ""
    76	    # --frozen when a lockfile is present skips resolution; ConfigMap-based
    77	    # workdirs may drop uv.lock (>1MB limit), so fall back to a normal resolve.
    78	    frozen_flag = "$([ -f uv.lock ] && echo '--frozen' || echo '')"
    79	    # Symlink wheels from the uv cache into the venv instead of copying; works
    80	    # across bind mounts.
    81	    link_mode_flag = "--link-mode symlink"
    82	    target = _uv_sync_target(packages)
    83	    extra_flags = _extra_flags(extras)
    84	
    85	    sync_cmd = " ".join(
    86	        part
    87	        for part in [
    88	            "uv sync",
    89	            frozen_flag,
    90	            link_mode_flag,
    91	            python_flag,
    92	            target,
    93	            "--no-dev",
    94	            extra_flags,
    95	        ]
    96	        if part
    97	    )
    98	
    99	    lines = [
   100	        "set -e",
   101	        'cd "$IRIS_WORKDIR"',
   102	        "echo 'syncing deps'",
   103	        sync_cmd,
   104	        # uv sync writes .pth links for editable path sources but does not invoke
   105	        # the build backend, so rust-dev mode (editable = true) leaves native
   106	        # extensions unbuilt. Build every maturin member explicitly.
   107	        "if grep -q 'editable = true' pyproject.toml 2>/dev/null; then"
   108	        " echo 'rust-dev mode: building native extensions';"
   109	        " for crate in lib/*/pyproject.toml; do"
   110	        ' grep -q \'build-backend = "maturin"\' "$crate" 2>/dev/null &&'
   111	        ' uv pip install -e "$(dirname "$crate")";'
   112	        " done;"
   113	        " fi",
   114	    ]
   115	    if pip_packages:
   116	        pip_args = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in pip_packages)
   117	        pip_cmd = " ".join(["uv pip install", link_mode_flag, pip_args])
   118	        lines += ["echo 'installing pip deps'", pip_cmd]
   119	    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   120	
   121	
   122	def wants_gpu_extra(extras: Sequence[str]) -> bool:
   123	    """Whether any requested extra is the ``gpu`` extra (``extra`` or ``package:extra``)."""
   124	    return any((e.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in e else e) == "gpu" for e in extras)
   125	
   126	
   127	# The NVVM bitcode library JAX/XLA load to compile GPU kernels.
   128	_LIBDEVICE_FILE = "libdevice.10.bc"
   129	# XLA's built-in default --xla_gpu_cuda_data_dir, resolved relative to the workdir.
   130	_XLA_CUDA_DATA_DIR = "cuda_sdk_lib"
   131	# These are the only CUDA 12/13 distributions in the resolved GPU environment
   132	# that both install files under the same nvidia namespace.  Reinstalling them
   133	# last makes the requested CUDA 13 wheel own its shared-library paths again.
   134	CUDA_13_LIBRARY_PACKAGES = ("nvidia-cudnn-cu13", "nvidia-nccl-cu13")
   135	
   136	
   137	def cuda_toolchain_setup_script() -> str:
   138	    """Return a setup script that exposes the venv's CUDA toolchain to JAX/Pallas.
   139	
   140	    Appended to a GPU job's setup so Mosaic GPU kernels compile and JAX sees the
   141	    CUDA 13 shared libraries after mixed CUDA package installs. It puts the
   142	    ``jax[cuda13]`` toolchain (``ptxas``/``nvlink``) on ``PATH``, stages
   143	    ``libdevice.10.bc`` where XLA looks, and restores CUDA 13 cuDNN and NCCL
   144	    precedence when those packages are installed. A no-op when the venv carries
   145	    no CUDA toolchain.
   146	    """
   147	    cuda_13_library_packages = " ".join(CUDA_13_LIBRARY_PACKAGES)
   148	    return rf"""set -e
   149	cuda_bin=""
   150	for _d in "$IRIS_VENV"/lib/python*/site-packages/nvidia/cu*/bin; do
   151	  if [ -x "$_d/ptxas" ]; then cuda_bin="$_d"; break; fi
   152	done
   153	if [ -z "$cuda_bin" ]; then echo 'no CUDA toolchain to stage'; exit 0; fi
   154	echo 'staging CUDA toolchain'
   155	ln -sf "$cuda_bin"/* "$IRIS_VENV/bin/"
   156	_libdevice="$(dirname "$cuda_bin")/nvvm/libdevice/{_LIBDEVICE_FILE}"
   157	if [ -f "$_libdevice" ]; then
   158	  mkdir -p "$IRIS_WORKDIR/{_XLA_CUDA_DATA_DIR}/nvvm/libdevice"
   159	  cp -f "$_libdevice" "$IRIS_WORKDIR/{_XLA_CUDA_DATA_DIR}/nvvm/libdevice/{_LIBDEVICE_FILE}"
   160	  cp -f "$_libdevice" "$IRIS_WORKDIR/{_LIBDEVICE_FILE}"
   161	fi
   162	for _cuda13_package in {cuda_13_library_packages}; do
   163	  _cuda13_version=""
   164	  if [ -x "$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" ]; then
   165	    _cuda13_version="$(
   166	      "$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" - "$_cuda13_package" <<'PY'
   167	import importlib.metadata as md
   168	import sys
   169	
   170	try:
   171	    print(md.version(sys.argv[1]))
   172	except md.PackageNotFoundError:
   173	    pass
   174	PY
   175	    )"
   176	  fi
   177	  if [ -n "$_cuda13_version" ]; then
   178	    echo "restoring CUDA 13 library precedence for $_cuda13_package"
   179	    uv pip install --python "$IRIS_VENV/bin/python" \
   180	      --link-mode symlink \
   181	      --reinstall-package "$_cuda13_package" \
   182	      "$_cuda13_package==$_cuda13_version"
   183	  fi
   184	done
   185	"""
   186	
   187	
   188	def iris_runtime_setup_script() -> str:
   189	    """Render the script that installs iris's own runtime deps into ``$IRIS_VENV``.
   190	
   191	    Installs cloudpickle (callable entrypoints) and py-spy/memray (the profiler)
   192	    so iris features work without the user declaring them. Best-effort: skipped
   193	    unless a venv exists (a bring-your-own image is left untouched) and a failed
   194	    install only warns, so it never fails the job.
   195	    """
   196	    pkgs = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in _IRIS_RUNTIME_DEPS)
   197	    pip_cmd = " ".join(["uv pip install", "--link-mode symlink", pkgs])
   198	    return (
   199	        'cd "$IRIS_WORKDIR" 2>/dev/null || true\n'
   200	        'if [ -d "$IRIS_VENV" ]; then\n'
   201	        "  echo 'installing iris runtime deps'\n"
   202	        f"  {pip_cmd} || echo '[iris setup] runtime deps install failed; continuing'\n"
   203	        "fi\n"
   204	    )
   600	        """
   601	        # Allocate requested ports
   602	        port_names = list(self.request.ports)
   603	        allocated_ports = self._port_allocator.allocate(len(port_names)) if port_names else []
   604	        self.ports = dict(zip(port_names, allocated_ports, strict=True))
   605	
   606	        # Create task working directory with attempt isolation
   607	        safe_task_id = self.task_id.to_safe_token()
   608	        self.workdir = self._cache_dir / "workdirs" / f"{safe_task_id}_attempt_{self.attempt_id}"
   609	        self.workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   610	        self.output_dir = self.workdir / _OUTPUT_HOST_DIRNAME
   611	        self.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   612	
   613	        # Mount tmpfs on workdir for quota enforcement (Docker only; no-op for process/k8s).
   614	        # Must happen before _download_bundle() so staged files land on the tmpfs.
   615	        disk_bytes = self.request.resources.disk_bytes if self.request.HasField("resources") else 0
   616	        self._runtime.prepare_workdir(self.workdir, disk_bytes)
   617	
   618	    def run(self) -> None:
   619	        """Execute the full task lifecycle. Intended to run in a background thread.
   620	
   621	        The lifecycle is:
   622	        0. Setup: allocate ports, create workdir
   623	        1. Download bundle by bundle ID
   624	        2. Resolve base image
   625	        3. Create container handle
   626	        4. Build phase: run setup_commands (uv sync) - BUILDING state
   627	        5. Run phase: start main command - RUNNING state
   628	        6. Monitor until the command exits
   629	        7. Capture task outputs, then publish the terminal state
   630	
   631	        Not valid for adopted tasks — use resume_monitoring() instead.
   632	        """
   633	        if self._bundle_store is None or self._runtime is None:
   634	            raise RuntimeError("Cannot run() an adopted TaskAttempt — use resume_monitoring()")
   635	        logger.info(
   636	            "TaskAttempt starting: task_id=%s attempt=%s num_tasks=%s",
   637	            self.task_id,
   638	            self.attempt_id,
   639	            self.num_tasks,
   640	        )
   641	        try:
   642	            self._check_cancelled()
   643	            self._setup()
   644	            self._check_cancelled()
   645	            self._download_bundle()
   646	            self._check_cancelled()
   647	            self._resolve_image()
   648	            self._check_cancelled()
   649	            self._create_container()
   650	            self._check_cancelled()
   651	            self._build_container()
   652	            self._check_cancelled()
   653	            self._run_container()
   654	            outcome = self._monitor()
   655	            self._capture_outputs()
   656	            self.transition_to(outcome.state, error=outcome.error, exit_code=outcome.exit_code)
   657	        except TaskCancelled:
   658	            self.transition_to(job_pb2.TASK_STATE_KILLED)
   659	        except ContainerInfraError as e:
   660	            self._fail_from_exception(job_pb2.TASK_STATE_WORKER_FAILED, "Infrastructure error", e)
   661	        except Exception as e:
   662	            self._fail_from_exception(job_pb2.TASK_STATE_FAILED, "Task failed", e)
   663	        finally:
   664	            self._cleanup()
   665	            logger.info(
   666	                "TaskAttempt finished: task_id=%s attempt=%s state=%s exit_code=%s",
   667	                self.task_id,
   668	                self.attempt_id,
   669	                self.status,
   670	                self.exit_code,
   671	            )
   672	
   673	    def _download_bundle(self) -> None:
   674	        """Stage the code bundle from the configured bundle ID.
   675	
   676	        Transitions task to BUILDING state and performs chaos injection checks
   677	        for testing delayed builds.
   678	        """
   679	        self.transition_to(job_pb2.TASK_STATE_BUILDING, message="downloading bundle")
   680	        self.started_at = Timestamp.now()
   681	        self._build_phase_start = time.monotonic()
   682	
   683	        download_start = time.monotonic()
   684	
   685	        # Chaos injection for testing failures during download
   686	        chaos_raise("worker.bundle_download")
   687	
   688	        # Chaos injection for testing delayed builds (for screenshot tests)
   689	        if rule := chaos("worker.building_delay"):
   690	            time.sleep(rule.delay_seconds)
   691	
   692	        # Periodically check should_stop during download to support kill during BUILDING
   693	        # (RF-3: For now, we defer kill handling until container starts, as bundle
   694	        # downloads are typically fast. Future work could add cancellation support
   695	        # to BundleStore.extract_bundle_to if long downloads become a problem.)
   696	
   697	        assert self.workdir is not None
   698	        workdir_files = dict(self.request.entrypoint.workdir_files)
   699	        for name, blob_id in self.request.entrypoint.workdir_file_refs.items():
   700	            workdir_files[name] = self._bundle_store.get(blob_id)
   701	        self._runtime.stage_bundle(
   702	            bundle_id=self.request.bundle_id,
   703	            workdir=self.workdir,
   704	            workdir_files=workdir_files,
   705	            bundle_store=self._bundle_store,
   706	        )
   707	
   708	        logger.info(
   709	            "Bundle staged for task %s in %.2fs",
   710	            self.task_id,
   711	            time.monotonic() - download_start,
   712	        )
   713	
   714	    def _resolve_image(self) -> None:
   715	        """Resolve the task image from the request override or cluster config.
   716	
   717	        Per-task ``task_image`` on the RunTaskRequest takes precedence over the
   718	        worker's cluster-configured ``default_task_image``. This lets jobs that
   719	        need a custom runtime (e.g. runsc/skopeo for sandboxing untrusted child
   720	        workloads) supply their own image without reconfiguring the cluster.
   721	
   722	        No per-job Docker build — the chosen image must already exist in the
   723	        registry. The remote client wraps the entrypoint with uv sync.
   724	        """
   725	        requested = self.request.task_image or self._default_task_image
   726	        if not requested:
   727	            raise ValueError(
   728	                "No task image configured. Pass task_image to submit() or set "
   729	                "defaults.default_task_image in cluster config."
   730	            )
   731	        self.image_tag = self._resolve_image_fn(requested)
   732	
   733	        logger.info("Using task image %s for task %s", self.image_tag, self.task_id)
   734	
   735	    def _create_container(self) -> None:
   736	        """Create container handle from config.
   737	
   738	        Prepares the container configuration including environment variables,
   739	        mounts, and workdir setup. The actual container is not started yet.
   740	        """
   741	        iris_env = build_iris_env(
   742	            self,
   743	            self._worker_id,
   744	            self._controller_address,
   745	        )
   746	        env = dict(iris_env)
   747	
   748	        env.update(self._task_env)
   749	        env.update(dict(self.request.environment.env_vars))
   750	        # The controller owns the process-incarnation identity. User and cluster
   751	        # env cannot replace it with a value shared by two attempts.
   752	        env.pop(IRIS_ATTEMPT_UID_ENV, None)
   753	        if attempt_uid := iris_env.get(IRIS_ATTEMPT_UID_ENV):
   754	            env[IRIS_ATTEMPT_UID_ENV] = attempt_uid
   755	
   756	        # The worker's physical location is authoritative over task-provided env.
   757	        region = self._worker_metadata.attributes.get(WellKnownAttribute.REGION)
   758	        if region and region.string_value:
   759	            env[IRIS_WORKER_REGION_ENV] = region.string_value
   760	
   761	        # Get RuntimeEntrypoint proto directly
   762	        rt_ep = self.request.entrypoint
   763	
   764	        # Extract timeout from proto (0 or unset means no timeout)
   765	        timeout_seconds = None
   766	        if self.request.HasField("timeout") and self.request.timeout.milliseconds > 0:
   767	            timeout_seconds = self.request.timeout.milliseconds / 1000
   768	
   769	        assert self.workdir is not None
   770	        job_id, _ = self.task_id.require_task()
   771	
   772	        config = ContainerConfig(
   773	            image=self.image_tag,
   774	            entrypoint=rt_ep,
   775	            env=env,
   776	            resources=self.request.resources if self.request.HasField("resources") else None,
   777	            container_profile=self.request.container_profile,
   778	            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   779	            mounts=list(STANDARD_MOUNTS),
   780	            workdir_host_path=self.workdir,
   781	            output_host_path=self.output_dir,
   782	            task_id=self.task_id.to_wire(),
   783	            attempt_id=self.attempt_id,
   784	            attempt_uid=self.attempt_uid,
   785	            job_id=job_id.to_wire(),
   786	            worker_id=self._worker_id,
   787	            worker_metadata=self._worker_metadata,
   788	            ports=self.ports,
   789	        )
   790	
   791	        chaos_raise("worker.create_container")
   792	        self._container_handle = self._runtime.create_container(config)
   793	        logger.info("Container handle created for task %s", self.task_id)
   794	
   795	    def _capture_outputs(self) -> None:
   796	        if self._task_outputs is None:
   797	            return
   798	        if self.output_dir is None or not self.output_dir.exists():
   799	            self.output_archive = job_pb2.TaskOutputArchive(
   800	                state=job_pb2.TaskOutputArchive.TASK_OUTPUT_ARCHIVE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE,
   801	                error="output_directory_unavailable",
   802	            )
   803	            return
   804	        if self.should_stop:
   805	            self.output_archive = job_pb2.TaskOutputArchive(
   806	                state=job_pb2.TaskOutputArchive.TASK_OUTPUT_ARCHIVE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE,
   807	                error="cancelled",
   808	            )
   809	            return
   810	        self.status_message = TASK_OUTPUT_FINALIZING_STATUS
   811	        try:
   812	            self.output_archive = capture_task_outputs_for_attempt(
   813	                self.output_dir,
   814	                self._task_outputs,
   815	                self.task_id,
   816	                self.attempt_uid,
   817	                local_root=self._cache_dir / "task-output-archives",
   818	                source_prefix=self._task_env.get("MARIN_PREFIX"),
   819	                stop=self._output_stop,
   820	            )
   821	        finally:
   822	            self.status_message = ""
   823	
   824	    def _build_container(self) -> None:
   825	        """Run setup commands (uv sync, pip install, etc) during BUILDING state.
   826	
   827	        This is the build phase where dependencies are synced. The container
   828	        handle runs setup_commands in a blocking fashion. If there are no
   829	        setup_commands, this is a no-op.
   830	        """
   831	        assert self._container_handle is not None
   832	
   833	        if self.request.entrypoint.setup_commands:
   834	            self.transition_to(job_pb2.TASK_STATE_BUILDING, message="syncing dependencies")
   835	            self.build_started = Timestamp.now()
   836	
   837	        def on_build_logs(lines: list[LogLine]) -> None:
   838	            entries = [self._make_log_entry(source=line.source, data=line.data) for line in lines]
   839	            self._push_logs(entries)
   840	
   841	        self._container_handle.build(on_logs=on_build_logs)
   842	
   843	        self.build_finished = Timestamp.now()
   844	        if self.request.entrypoint.setup_commands:
   845	            logger.info("Build phase completed for task %s", self.task_id)
   846	
   847	    def _run_container(self) -> None:
   848	        """Start the container. Task stays in BUILDING until _monitor() confirms readiness."""
   849	        assert self._container_handle is not None
   850	
   851	        self._container_handle.run()
   852	        logger.info(
   853	            "Container started for task %s (container_id=%s, ports=%s)",
   854	            self.task_id,
   855	            self.container_id,
   856	            self.ports,
   857	        )
   858	
   859	    def _monitor(self) -> _TaskOutcome:
   860	        """Monitor task execution: check status, collect stats, stream logs.
   861	
   862	        Polls container status at regular intervals until the container stops.
   863	        Streams logs incrementally into task.logs (single source of truth).
   864	        Collects runtime statistics (CPU, memory, disk).
   865	        Returns the terminal outcome without mutating the attempt to a terminal state.
   866	
   867	        Execution timeouts are enforced by the controller, not the worker.
   868	        Profiling is handled centrally by the controller's profile loop thread.
   869	        """
   870	        assert self._container_handle is not None
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Multi-process GPU supervisor hook.
     5	
     6	``MultiGpuHook`` wraps a command in :mod:`iris.hooks.multigpu_main`, which
     7	spawns one process per GPU group inside a single task. ``build_multigpu_hook``
     8	derives it from a job's GPU count.
     9	
    10	This module also defines the ``IRIS_MULTIGPU_*`` rank-env contract the supervisor
    11	stamps on each child; :mod:`iris.runtime.jax_init` and
    12	:mod:`iris.hooks.nsys_main` read it.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from collections.abc import Sequence
    16	from dataclasses import dataclass
    17	
    18	from iris.cluster.types import ResourceSpec, get_gpu_count
    19	
    20	# Rank env the supervisor stamps on each child.
    21	IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT_ENV = "IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT"
    22	IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV = "IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX"
    23	IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV = "IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS"
    24	
    25	_MULTIGPU_MAIN_MODULE = "iris.hooks.multigpu_main"
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29	class MultiGpuHook:
    30	    """Run the command under the multi-process GPU supervisor.
    31	
    32	    The supervisor spawns ``nproc`` children, each pinned to a contiguous group of
    33	    ``devices_per_proc`` of the task's GPUs, and supervises their lifecycle.
    34	
    35	    Attributes:
    36	        nproc: Processes to launch on this host.
    37	        devices_per_proc: Local devices assigned to each process.
    38	        wrap_child: Wrapper prepended to *each child* (e.g. ``NsysHook(...).wrap``
    39	            rendered as a string), for per-process profiling — the wrapper then sees
    40	            each child's own ``IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX``. A whole-task wrapper is
    41	            composed around this hook instead.
    42	    """
    43	
    44	    nproc: int
    45	    devices_per_proc: int = 1
    46	    wrap_child: str | None = None
    47	
    48	    def wrap(self, command: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]:
    49	        argv = [
    50	            "python",
    51	            "-m",
    52	            _MULTIGPU_MAIN_MODULE,
    53	            "--nproc",
    54	            str(self.nproc),
    55	            "--devices-per-proc",
    56	            str(self.devices_per_proc),
    57	        ]
    58	        if self.wrap_child:
    59	            argv += ["--wrap", self.wrap_child]
    60	        argv.append("--")
    61	        return [*argv, *command]
    62	
    63	
    64	def build_multigpu_hook(resources: ResourceSpec, processes_per_task: int) -> MultiGpuHook:
    65	    """Build the hook for *processes_per_task* processes over the job's GPUs.
    66	
    67	    Requires a GPU device whose count is divisible by ``processes_per_task``; each
    68	    child then gets ``gpu_count // processes_per_task`` devices.
    69	    """
    70	    device = resources.device
    71	    gpu_count = get_gpu_count(device) if device is not None and device.HasField("gpu") else 0
    72	    if gpu_count <= 0:
    73	        raise ValueError("processes_per_task > 1 requires a GPU device")
    74	    if gpu_count % processes_per_task != 0:
    75	        raise ValueError(f"processes_per_task ({processes_per_task}) must divide the GPU count ({gpu_count})")
    76	    return MultiGpuHook(nproc=processes_per_task, devices_per_proc=gpu_count // processes_per_task)
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Per-node multi-process supervisor: run one JAX process per device group.
     5	
     6	``python -m iris.hooks.multigpu_main --nproc N [--devices-per-proc D] [--wrap 'CMD'] -- <argv>``
     7	spawns N copies of ``<argv>`` inside a single Iris task, each pinned to a
     8	contiguous group of D local accelerator devices. It is the GPU analogue of
     9	``srun``/``torchrun`` scoped to one host: the children share the pod's IPC
    10	namespace and ``/dev/shm``, so NCCL keeps the intra-node NVLink P2P that
    11	separate single-GPU pods cannot use.
    12	
    13	Invoked by the caller (or a launcher) as part of the command — iris is a dumb
    14	scheduler and does not inject it. ``--wrap 'CMD'`` prefixes each child with a
    15	wrapper (e.g. ``python -m iris.hooks.nsys_main --tasks first``) so a per-process
    16	profiler sees each child's own ``IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX``; a whole-task
    17	wrapper is composed outside this process instead.
    18	
    19	Each child inherits the supervisor's environment plus its rank::
    20	
    21	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT    = num_tasks * nproc           (global world size)
    22	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX    = task_index * nproc + local  (global rank)
    23	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS = the child's D device ids     ("0", or "2,3")
    24	
    25	``iris.runtime.jax_init.initialize_jax`` reads these (their names are the contract
    26	this module defines) and joins the JAX mesh. The names are iris-private (not the
    27	JAX_*/framework namespace) so a job that already sets JAX rank vars never trips
    28	the supervised path.
    29	
    30	The supervisor owns child lifecycle: it forwards SIGINT/SIGTERM to every child,
    31	tears the group down and exits non-zero if any child fails, and tags each
    32	child's output with its local rank, keeping stdout and stderr apart so iris
    33	still classifies each line by its stream. It deliberately does not import jax — the
    34	children initialize CUDA only after the supervisor has already spawned them, so
    35	no CUDA context exists in the supervisor's address space.
    36	"""
    37	
    38	import argparse
    39	import logging
    40	import os
    41	import shlex
    42	import signal
    43	import subprocess
    44	import sys
    45	import threading
    46	from types import FrameType
    47	from typing import IO
    48	
    49	from rigging.timing import Deadline, Duration
    50	
    51	from iris.cluster.client.job_info import get_job_info
    52	from iris.cluster.log_highlights import rank_log_tag
    53	from iris.hooks.multigpu import (
    54	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV,
    55	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT_ENV,
    56	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV,
    57	)
    58	
    59	logger = logging.getLogger("iris.multigpu")
    60	
    61	_SHUTDOWN_SIGNALS = (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM)
    62	# How often to re-poll child liveness while waiting for the group (seconds).
    63	_REAP_POLL_INTERVAL = 1.0
    64	# Grace period after a SIGTERM before escalating to SIGKILL, so a child that
    65	# traps or ignores SIGTERM cannot wedge the supervisor (and hence the task).
    66	_TERMINATE_GRACE = Duration.from_seconds(10.0)
    67	
    68	
    69	def _child_rank_env(
    70	    local_rank: int, nproc: int, devices_per_proc: int, task_index: int, num_tasks: int
    71	) -> dict[str, str]:
    72	    """Compute the rank env vars handed to one child process.
    73	
    74	    Device ids are a contiguous slice so rank ``i`` owns devices
    75	    ``[i*D, (i+1)*D)``; the common case ``D == 1`` gives one device per process.
    76	    """
    77	    begin = local_rank * devices_per_proc
    78	    device_ids = ",".join(str(d) for d in range(begin, begin + devices_per_proc))
    79	    return {
    80	        IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT_ENV: str(num_tasks * nproc),
    81	        IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV: str(task_index * nproc + local_rank),
    82	        IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV: device_ids,
    83	    }
    84	
    85	
    86	def _pump_output(tag: str, source: IO[str], sink: IO[str], write_lock: threading.Lock) -> None:
    87	    """Forward one of a child's streams to the matching supervisor stream, rank-tagged.
    88	
    89	    stdout and stderr stay separate: iris classifies an unprefixed line by the
    90	    stream it arrived on, so folding a child's stderr into stdout would demote
    91	    its tracebacks to ``INFO``.
    92	    """
    93	    for line in iter(source.readline, ""):
    94	        with write_lock:
    95	            sink.write(tag + line)
    96	            sink.flush()
    97	    source.close()
    98	
    99	
   100	def _signal_children(children: list[subprocess.Popen], ranks, sig: int) -> None:
   101	    """Send ``sig`` to every still-running child in ``ranks``."""
   102	    for rank in ranks:
   103	        child = children[rank]
   104	        if child.poll() is None:
   105	            child.send_signal(sig)
   106	
   107	
   108	def _spawn_children(
   109	    nproc: int, devices_per_proc: int, task_index: int, num_tasks: int, child_argv: list[str]
   110	) -> tuple[list[subprocess.Popen], list[threading.Thread]]:
   111	    """Spawn all children, or SIGKILL any that started and re-raise on failure.
   112	
   113	    A partial spawn (rank 0 up, rank 3 fails on ``EAGAIN`` / a missing binary /
   114	    thread-creation failure) would otherwise leave the started ranks orphaned,
   115	    holding GPUs after the supervisor exits.
   116	    """
   117	    children: list[subprocess.Popen] = []
   118	    pumps: list[threading.Thread] = []
   119	    write_lock = threading.Lock()
   120	    try:
   121	        for local_rank in range(nproc):
   122	            rank_env = _child_rank_env(local_rank, nproc, devices_per_proc, task_index, num_tasks)
   123	            child_env = {**os.environ, **rank_env}
   124	            logger.info(
   125	                "launch local rank %d: %s=%s %s=%s",
   126	                local_rank,
   127	                IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV,
   128	                rank_env[IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV],
   129	                IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV,
   130	                rank_env[IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV],
   131	            )
   132	            child = subprocess.Popen(
   133	                child_argv,
   134	                env=child_env,
   135	                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   136	                stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   137	                text=True,
   138	                bufsize=1,
   139	            )
   140	            children.append(child)
   141	            tag = rank_log_tag(local_rank)
   142	            for source, sink in ((child.stdout, sys.stdout), (child.stderr, sys.stderr)):
   143	                pump = threading.Thread(target=_pump_output, args=(tag, source, sink, write_lock), daemon=True)
   144	                pump.start()
   145	                pumps.append(pump)
   146	    except BaseException:
   147	        logger.error("spawn failed after starting %d/%d child(ren); killing them", len(children), nproc)
   148	        _signal_children(children, range(len(children)), signal.SIGKILL)
   149	        for child in children:
   150	            try:
   151	                child.wait(timeout=5.0)
   152	            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   153	                pass
   154	        raise
   155	    return children, pumps
   156	
   157	
   158	def run(nproc: int, devices_per_proc: int, child_argv: list[str]) -> int:
   159	    """Spawn ``nproc`` children, supervise them, return the process exit code.
   160	
   161	    ``128 + signum`` if a SIGINT/SIGTERM was delivered to the supervisor (an
   162	    external termination/preemption); this takes precedence because the children
   163	    it forwards the signal to report their own (negative) signal codes, which
   164	    must not be surfaced as a task failure. Otherwise the first non-zero child
   165	    exit code (after tearing the rest down), or 0 once every child exits cleanly.
   166	    """
   167	    if nproc < 1:
   168	        raise ValueError(f"--nproc must be >= 1, got {nproc}")
   169	    if devices_per_proc < 1:
   170	        raise ValueError(f"--devices-per-proc must be >= 1, got {devices_per_proc}")
   171	    if not child_argv:
   172	        raise ValueError("no child command given after '--'")
   173	
   174	    job_info = get_job_info()
   175	    num_tasks = job_info.num_tasks if job_info else 1
   176	    task_index = job_info.task_index if job_info else 0
   177	    logger.info(
   178	        "supervising %d process(es) x %d device(s) each; task_index=%d num_tasks=%d; command=%s",
   179	        nproc,
   180	        devices_per_proc,
   181	        task_index,
   182	        num_tasks,
   183	        " ".join(child_argv),
   184	    )
   185	
   186	    children, pumps = _spawn_children(nproc, devices_per_proc, task_index, num_tasks, child_argv)
   187	
   188	    terminating = threading.Event()
   189	    shutdown_signum: int | None = None
   190	    # Deadline after which surviving children are SIGKILLed; ``None`` until a
   191	    # teardown begins. Shared between the signal handler and the reap loop, both
   192	    # of which run in the main thread (no lock needed).
   193	    kill_deadline: Deadline | None = None
   194	
   195	    def _forward_signal(signum: int, _frame: FrameType | None) -> None:
   196	        nonlocal shutdown_signum, kill_deadline
   197	        if shutdown_signum is None:
   198	            shutdown_signum = signum
   199	        terminating.set()
   200	        logger.warning("received signal %d; forwarding to children", signum)
   201	        _signal_children(children, range(nproc), signum)
   202	        if kill_deadline is None:
   203	            kill_deadline = Deadline.from_now(_TERMINATE_GRACE)
   204	
   205	    for sig in _SHUTDOWN_SIGNALS:
   206	        signal.signal(sig, _forward_signal)
   207	
   208	    first_failure: int | None = None
   209	    pending = set(range(nproc))
   210	    while pending:
   211	        # Escalate to SIGKILL if a requested teardown overran its grace period,
   212	        # so a child that traps or ignores SIGTERM cannot wedge the supervisor.
   213	        if kill_deadline is not None and kill_deadline.expired():
   214	            survivors = [r for r in pending if children[r].poll() is None]
   215	            if survivors:
   216	                logger.error("SIGTERM grace expired; SIGKILL %d straggler(s): %s", len(survivors), survivors)
   217	                _signal_children(children, survivors, signal.SIGKILL)
   218	            kill_deadline = None  # SIGKILL is final; do not re-fire each tick
   219	        for local_rank in list(pending):
   220	            child = children[local_rank]
   221	            try:
   222	                code = child.wait(timeout=_REAP_POLL_INTERVAL)
   223	            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   224	                continue
   225	            pending.discard(local_rank)
   226	            logger.info("local rank %d exited with code %d", local_rank, code)
   227	            if code != 0 and first_failure is None:
   228	                first_failure = code
   229	            # A failure tears the whole group down: a collective job cannot make
   230	            # progress once one rank is gone. A clean exit does not — peers run to
   231	            # their own completion.
   232	            if code != 0 and not terminating.is_set():
   233	                terminating.set()
   234	                logger.error("local rank %d failed (code %d); terminating %d peer(s)", local_rank, code, len(pending))
   235	                _signal_children(children, pending, signal.SIGTERM)
   236	                kill_deadline = Deadline.from_now(_TERMINATE_GRACE)
   237	
   238	    for pump in pumps:
   239	        pump.join(timeout=5.0)
   240	
   241	    # An external signal wins over child exit codes: the children we forwarded it
   242	    # to report negative (signal) codes that are an artifact of the teardown, not
   243	    # a task failure. A child failing on its own never sets shutdown_signum, so
   244	    # its genuine non-zero code surfaces.
   245	    if shutdown_signum is not None:
   246	        return 128 + shutdown_signum
   247	    if first_failure is not None:
   248	        return first_failure
   249	    return 0
   250	
   251	
   252	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   253	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s")
   254	    raw = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
   255	    if "--" not in raw:
   256	        raise SystemExit(
   257	            "usage: python -m iris.hooks.multigpu_main --nproc N [--devices-per-proc D] "
   258	            "[--wrap 'CMD'] -- <command...>"
   259	        )
   260	    split = raw.index("--")
   261	    own_args, child_argv = raw[:split], raw[split + 1 :]
   262	
   263	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m iris.hooks.multigpu_main")
   264	    parser.add_argument("--nproc", type=int, required=True, help="number of processes to launch on this host")
   265	    parser.add_argument(
   266	        "--devices-per-proc", type=int, default=1, help="local accelerator devices assigned to each process"
   267	    )
   268	    parser.add_argument(
   269	        "--wrap",
   270	        default=None,
   271	        help="wrap each child in this command (e.g. 'python -m iris.hooks.nsys_main --tasks first') — for "
   272	        "per-process profiling, where the wrapper sees each child's own IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX",
   273	    )
   274	    args = parser.parse_args(own_args)
   275	    # A per-child wrapper (process-scope) goes *inside* the supervisor: each child runs
   276	    # `<wrap> -- <command>`. A whole-task wrapper (node-scope) is composed the other way,
   277	    # outside this process, so it needs nothing here.
   278	    if args.wrap:
   279	        child_argv = [*shlex.split(args.wrap), "--", *child_argv]
   280	    return run(args.nproc, args.devices_per_proc, child_argv)
   281	
   282	
   283	if __name__ == "__main__":
   284	    sys.exit(main())
