   719	        sys.last_exc = exc
   720	    raise
   721	"""
   722	
   723	
   724	@dataclass
   725	class EnvironmentSpec:
   726	    """Environment specification for jobs.
   727	
   728	    Default environment variables (automatically set if not overridden):
   729	    - HF_DATASETS_TRUST_REMOTE_CODE: "1" (allows custom dataset code)
   730	    - TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM: "false" (avoids tokenizer deadlocks)
   731	    - HF_TOKEN: from os.environ (if set)
   732	    - WANDB_API_KEY: from os.environ (if set)
   733	    - MARIN_PROVENANCE: the launch's ``rigging.provenance.Provenance`` as JSON, captured
   734	      at submission (or forwarded from this process's own env when re-submitting inside
   735	      a task), so tasks stamp artifacts with the submitter's git identity
   736	
   737	    Setup:
   738	    - ``setup_scripts=None`` builds the default uv-sync script. ``sync_packages``
   739	      scopes that sync to specific workspace members (default: all members).
   740	    - ``setup_scripts`` set to a list runs those scripts verbatim before the
   741	      command, with the task's ``IRIS_*`` env available; ``[]`` means no setup (the
   742	      image is used as-is). Build the default and tweak it via
   743	      ``iris.cluster.setup_scripts.default_setup_script``.
   744	
   745	    Whenever any setup runs (default or custom), iris appends its own
   746	    ``iris_runtime_setup_script`` so cloudpickle/profiler support is always
   747	    present; it is skipped only for the no-setup (``[]``) case.
   748	
   749	    Note: To specify workspace for bundle creation, use IrisClient.remote(workspace=...).
   750	    """
   751	
   752	    pip_packages: Sequence[str] | None = None
   753	    env_vars: dict[str, str] | None = None
   754	    extras: Sequence[str] | None = None
   755	    setup_scripts: Sequence[str] | None = None
   756	    sync_packages: Sequence[str] | None = None
   757	
   758	    def to_proto(self) -> job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig:
   759	        """Convert to wire format, resolving the user setup scripts.
   760	
   761	        ``setup_scripts=None`` builds the default uv-sync script from
   762	        extras/pip/sync_packages; a list is used verbatim; ``[]`` is no setup. The
   763	        wire carries only this user list.
   764	        """
   765	        default_env_vars = {
   766	            "HF_DATASETS_TRUST_REMOTE_CODE": "1",
   767	            "TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM": "false",
   768	            "HF_TOKEN": os.getenv("HF_TOKEN"),
   769	            "WANDB_API_KEY": os.getenv("WANDB_API_KEY"),
   770	            # Launch provenance: a task running from a git-less bundle inherits the
   771	            # submitter's identity via Provenance.capture(); transitive, since a task
   772	            # re-submitting captures this same env value.
   773	            LAUNCH_PROVENANCE_ENV: launch_provenance().to_json(),
   774	        }
   775	        if wants_gpu_extra(self.extras or ()):
   776	            default_env_vars.update(
   777	                {
   778	                    NCCL_RAS_ENABLE_ENV: "1",
   779	                    "NCCL_DEBUG": "INFO",
   780	                    "NCCL_DEBUG_SUBSYS": "INIT,BOOTSTRAP,ENV,NET,GRAPH,TUNING,RAS",
   781	                    "NCCL_DEBUG_TIMESTAMP": "[%F %T.%3f]",
   782	                }
   783	            )
   784	
   785	        merged_env_vars = {k: v for k, v in {**default_env_vars, **(self.env_vars or {})}.items() if v is not None}
   786	
   787	        if self.setup_scripts is None:
   788	            py_version = f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}"
   789	            extras = list(self.extras or [])
   790	            setup_scripts = [
   791	                default_setup_script(
   792	                    extras=extras,
   793	                    pip_packages=list(self.pip_packages or []),
   794	                    python_version=py_version,
   795	                    packages=list(self.sync_packages or []) or None,
   796	                )
   797	            ]
   798	            # GPU jobs need the venv's CUDA toolchain (ptxas/nvlink/libdevice)
   799	            # exposed for JAX/Pallas Mosaic; the script no-ops without it.
   800	            if wants_gpu_extra(extras):
   801	                setup_scripts.append(cuda_toolchain_setup_script())
   802	        else:
   803	            setup_scripts = [s for s in self.setup_scripts if s.strip()]
   804	
   805	        return job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig(env_vars=merged_env_vars, setup_scripts=setup_scripts)
   806	
   807	
   808	class Namespace(str):
   809	    """Namespace for actor isolation.
   810	
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Assemble a user-facing Entrypoint + EnvironmentConfig into a RuntimeEntrypoint.
     5	
     6	The RuntimeEntrypoint keeps setup separate from the user's command so each runtime
     7	can handle it as needed (DockerRuntime runs setup in a build container;
     8	ProcessRuntime skips it). Setup is the user's client-resolved
     9	``EnvironmentConfig.setup_scripts`` followed by iris's own runtime-deps script.
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from iris.cluster.setup_scripts import iris_runtime_setup_script
    13	from iris.cluster.types import Entrypoint
    14	from iris.rpc import job_pb2
    15	
    16	
    17	def build_runtime_entrypoint(
    18	    entrypoint: Entrypoint,
    19	    env_config: job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig,
    20	) -> job_pb2.RuntimeEntrypoint:
    21	    """Build a RuntimeEntrypoint from a user Entrypoint + env config.
    22	
    23	    Assembles ``setup_commands`` as the user's resolved scripts followed by iris's
    24	    runtime-deps script. The run_command is the user's original command, kept
    25	    separate so runtimes that don't need setup can skip it cleanly.
    26	    """
    27	    rt = job_pb2.RuntimeEntrypoint()
    28	    # Drop whitespace-only scripts; an empty user list means no setup at all, so the
    29	    # build phase (iris script included) is skipped and the command runs as-is.
    30	    user_scripts = [s for s in env_config.setup_scripts if s.strip()]
    31	    if user_scripts:
    32	        iris_script = iris_runtime_setup_script()
    33	        rt.setup_commands[:] = [*user_scripts, iris_script]
    34	    rt.run_command.argv[:] = entrypoint.command
    35	    for k, v in entrypoint.workdir_files.items():
    36	        rt.workdir_files[k] = v
    37	    for k, v in entrypoint.workdir_file_refs.items():
    38	        rt.workdir_file_refs[k] = v
    39	    return rt

   503	    # that only gains the iris package after the task's own `uv sync`, so the
   504	    # sidecar instead runs the iris controller image (iris + finelog installed),
   505	    # which can launch `python -m iris.cluster.backends.k8s.logship` directly.
   506	    logship_image: str = ""
   507	    cache_dir: str = DEFAULT_TASK_CACHE_DIR
   508	    service_account: str = ""
   509	    host_network: bool = False
   510	    controller_address: str | None = None
   511	    managed_label: str = ""
   512	    task_env: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   513	    task_outputs: TaskOutputPolicy | None = None
   514	    # Name of a Secret whose keys are projected into every task container via
   515	    # envFrom (operator-injected env, defaults.inject_env). Empty disables it.
   516	    env_secret_name: str = ""
   517	    # Kueue LocalQueue for coscheduled gang admission. Coscheduled jobs REQUIRE
   518	    # this: dispatching one with no LocalQueue configured raises (Kueue or
   519	    # nothing — there is no non-Kueue colocation fallback).
   520	    local_queue: str = ""
   521	    # coscheduling group_by -> KueueTopologyBinding. Defaults to CoreWeave
   522	    # conventions; a group_by with no entry carries no topology annotation.
   523	    kueue_topologies: dict[str, KueueTopologyBinding] = field(default_factory=lambda: dict(_CW_DEFAULT_TOPOLOGIES))
   524	    # PriorityBand -> Kubernetes PriorityClass name. Sets spec.priorityClassName.
   525	    # UNSPECIFIED is treated as INTERACTIVE. Defaults to the iris-{band} classes
   526	    # Iris creates at startup; override via kubernetes_provider.priority_classes.
   527	    priority_class_names: dict[int, str] = field(default_factory=lambda: dict(_DEFAULT_PRIORITY_CLASS_NAMES))
   528	
   529	
   530	def _build_task_script(run_req: job_pb2.RunTaskRequest) -> str:
   531	    """Build a shell script that runs the setup steps then the run_command."""
   532	    lines = ["set -e", "ulimit -c 0", "mkdir -p /app", "cd /app"]
   533	    lines.extend(render_setup_steps(run_req.entrypoint.setup_commands))
   534	    # Activate the venv the setup script populated. Conditional on it existing so
   535	    # a custom or no-setup script that brings its own environment runs as-is.
   536	    lines.append('[ -f "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate" ] && source "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate"')
   537	    if run_req.entrypoint.run_command.argv:
   538	        lines.append("exec " + shlex.join(run_req.entrypoint.run_command.argv))
   539	    return "\n".join(lines)
   540	
   541	
   542	def _build_init_container_spec(
   543	    run_req: job_pb2.RunTaskRequest,
   544	    pod_name: str,
   545	    default_image: str,
   546	    controller_address: str | None,
   547	) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict], str | None]:
   548	    """Build init containers for bundle fetch and workdir file staging.
   549	
   550	    Returns (init_containers, extra_volumes, configmap_name_or_None).
   551	    The init container runs a standalone Python script that downloads the
   552	    bundle zip from the controller and copies workdir files from a ConfigMap.
   553	    """
   554	    has_bundle = bool(run_req.bundle_id) and bool(controller_address)
   555	    workdir_files = dict(run_req.entrypoint.workdir_files)
   556	    workdir_file_refs = dict(run_req.entrypoint.workdir_file_refs)
   557	    has_blob_refs = bool(workdir_file_refs) and bool(controller_address)
   558	    if not has_bundle and not workdir_files and not has_blob_refs:
   559	        return [], [], None
   560	
   561	    script_path = Path(__file__).parent / "bundle_fetch.py"
   562	    bundle_script = script_path.read_text()
   563	
   564	    # The init container stages the bundle into the same volume the task reads it
   565	    # from, so both sides take the path and volume name from the one mount spec.
   566	    init_env: list[dict] = [{"name": "IRIS_WORKDIR", "value": WORKDIR_MOUNT.container_path}]
   567	    init_mounts: list[dict] = [{"name": WORKDIR_MOUNT.name, "mountPath": WORKDIR_MOUNT.container_path}]
   568	    extra_volumes: list[dict] = []
   569	    configmap_name: str | None = None
   570	
   571	    if has_bundle or has_blob_refs:
   572	        init_env.append({"name": "IRIS_CONTROLLER_URL", "value": controller_address})
   573	
   574	    if has_bundle:
   575	        init_env.append({"name": "IRIS_BUNDLE_ID", "value": run_req.bundle_id})
   576	
   577	    if has_blob_refs:
   578	        init_env.append({"name": "IRIS_WORKDIR_BLOB_REFS", "value": json.dumps(workdir_file_refs)})
   579	
   580	    if workdir_files:
   581	        configmap_name = f"{pod_name}-wf"
   582	        extra_volumes.append(
   583	            {
   584	                "name": "workdir-files",
   585	                "configMap": {
   586	                    "name": configmap_name,
   587	                    "items": [
   588	                        {"key": f"f{i:04d}", "path": normalize_workdir_relative_path(name)}
   589	                        for i, name in enumerate(workdir_files)
   590	                    ],
   591	                },
   592	            }
   593	        )
   594	        init_mounts.append(
   595	            {
   596	                "name": "workdir-files",
   597	                "mountPath": "/iris/staged-workdir-files",
   598	                "readOnly": True,
   599	            }
   600	        )
   601	        init_env.append({"name": "IRIS_WORKDIR_FILES_SRC", "value": "/iris/staged-workdir-files"})
   602	
   603	    init_containers = [
   604	        {
   605	            "name": "stage-workdir",
   606	            "image": default_image,
   607	            "imagePullPolicy": "IfNotPresent",
   608	            "command": ["python", "-c", bundle_script],
   609	            "env": init_env,
   610	            "volumeMounts": init_mounts,
   430	        # which don't reference config fields.
   431	        config = ContainerConfig(
   432	            image="",
   433	            entrypoint=job_pb2.RuntimeEntrypoint(),
   434	            env={},
   435	        )
   436	        handle = cls(config=config, runtime=runtime, _run_container_id=container_id)
   437	        runtime.track_container(container_id)
   438	        return handle
   439	
   440	    @property
   441	    def container_id(self) -> str | None:
   442	        """Return the Docker container ID (hash), if any."""
   443	        return self._run_container_id
   444	
   445	    def build(self, on_logs: Callable[[list[LogLine]], None] | None = None) -> list[LogLine]:
   446	        """Run setup_commands (uv sync, pip install, etc) in a temporary container.
   447	
   448	        Creates a temporary container that runs setup_commands, waits for completion,
   449	        and removes the container. The .venv is created in the shared workdir mount
   450	        and will be available to the run container.
   451	
   452	        If there are no setup_commands, this is a no-op.
   453	
   454	        Args:
   455	            on_logs: Optional callback invoked with each incremental batch of
   456	                log lines during the build. Enables streaming to LogService.
   457	
   458	        Returns:
   459	            List of log lines captured during the build phase.
   460	
   461	        Raises:
   462	            RuntimeError: If setup fails (non-zero exit code)
   463	        """
   464	        if not self.config.entrypoint.setup_commands:
   465	            logger.debug("No setup_commands, skipping build phase")
   466	            return []
   467	
   468	        # Build a bash script that runs all setup commands
   469	        setup_script = self._generate_setup_script()
   470	        self._write_setup_script(setup_script)
   471	
   472	        # Build containers get max(32 GB, task request) memory — uv sync on a large
   473	        # workspace OOMed at the old 8 GB ceiling on a host with 1.4 TB free.
   474	        task_memory_bytes = self.config.resources.memory_bytes if self.config.resources else 0
   475	        build_memory_bytes = (
   476	            max(self._BUILD_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES, task_memory_bytes)
   477	            if task_memory_bytes
   478	            else self._BUILD_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES
   479	        )
   480	        build_memory_mb = build_memory_bytes // (1024 * 1024)
   481	
   482	        build_container_id = self._docker_create(
   483	            command=["bash", "/app/_setup_env.sh"],
   484	            label_suffix="_build",
   485	            memory_limit_mb=build_memory_mb,
   486	        )
   487	
   488	        build_logs: list[LogLine] = []
   489	        try:
   490	            self._docker_start(build_container_id)
   491	
   492	            # Wait for build to complete (blocking), streaming logs incrementally
   493	            last_log_time: Timestamp | None = None
   494	            while True:
   495	                status = self._docker_inspect(build_container_id)
   496	
   497	                # Capture logs incrementally during build
   498	                new_logs = _docker_logs(build_container_id, since=last_log_time)
   499	                if new_logs:
   500	                    build_logs.extend(new_logs)
   501	                    last_log_time = Timestamp.from_seconds(new_logs[-1].timestamp.timestamp()).add_ms(1)
   502	                    if on_logs:
   503	                        on_logs(new_logs)
   504	
   505	                if status.phase == ContainerPhase.STOPPED:
   506	                    break
   507	                time.sleep(0.5)
   508	
   509	            # Final log fetch after container stops
   510	            final_logs = _docker_logs(build_container_id, since=last_log_time)
   511	            if final_logs:
   512	                build_logs.extend(final_logs)
   513	                if on_logs:
   514	                    on_logs(final_logs)
   515	
   516	            if status.exit_code != 0:
   517	                log_text = "\n".join(f"[{entry.source}] {entry.data}" for entry in build_logs[-50:])
   518	                raise RuntimeError(f"Build failed with exit_code={status.exit_code}\nLast 50 log lines:\n{log_text}")
   519	
   520	            logger.info("Build phase completed successfully for task %s", self.config.task_id)
   521	            return build_logs
   522	
   523	        finally:
   524	            # Always clean up the build container
   525	            self._docker_remove(build_container_id)
   526	
   527	    def _generate_setup_script(self) -> str:
   528	        """Generate a bash script that runs each setup command as its own step."""
   529	        lines = ["#!/bin/bash", "set -e"]
   530	        lines.extend(render_setup_steps(self.config.entrypoint.setup_commands))
   531	        return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   532	
   533	    def _write_setup_script(self, script: str) -> None:
   534	        """Write the setup script to the workdir mount."""
   535	        for rm in self._resolved_mounts:
   536	            if rm.container_path == "/app":
   537	                (Path(rm.host_path) / "_setup_env.sh").write_text(script)
   538	                return
   539	        raise RuntimeError("No /app mount found in config")
   540	
   541	    def run(self) -> None:
   542	        """Start the main command container.
   543	
   544	        Non-blocking - returns immediately after starting the container.
   545	        Use status() to monitor execution progress.
   546	        """
   547	        quoted_cmd = " ".join(shlex.quote(arg) for arg in self.config.entrypoint.run_command.argv)
   548	
   549	        # Run from the workdir (matching the k8s task script) and activate the venv
   550	        # only when a setup script left one, so a bring-your-own-env command runs in
   551	        # the image as-is instead of failing on a missing .venv.
   552	        run_script = f"""#!/bin/bash
   553	set -e
   554	cd "$IRIS_WORKDIR"
   555	[ -f "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate" ] && source "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate"
   556	exec {quoted_cmd}
   557	"""
   558	        self._write_run_script(run_script)
   559	        command = ["bash", "/app/_run.sh"]
   560	
   561	        self._run_container_id = self._docker_create(
   562	            command=command,
   563	            include_devices=True,
   564	        )
   565	        self.runtime.track_container(self._run_container_id)
   566	        self._docker_start(self._run_container_id)
   567	
   568	        logger.info(
   569	            "Run phase started for task %s (container_id=%s)",
   570	            self.config.task_id,
   571	            self._run_container_id,
   572	        )
   573	
   574	    def _write_run_script(self, script: str) -> None:
   575	        """Write the run script to the workdir mount."""

    81	    # pins 153.0 GiB in the pool and leaves under 3 GiB free on the device, so the arena
    82	    # allocation below has no room to remap into.
    83	    "XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_MEM_FRACTION": "0.75",
    84	}
    85	XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG = "--xla_gpu_experimental_parallel_collective_overlap_limit"
    86	DEFAULT_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 4
    87	DEFAULT_DROPLESS_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION: MoeImplementation = "sonic_cute"
    88	# Full inline norm watch failed with overlap 4. Overlap 1 completed the selected full-watch gate.
    89	INLINE_WATCH_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1
    90	# The ragged transport wants the opposite scheduling posture from the fixed and pooled ones. Its
    91	# dispatch and combine form one long dependent chain, so admitting several concurrent collectives
    92	# only contends for the SMs the transport itself needs.
    93	RAGGED_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1
    94	# The layer-carry offload, the latency-hiding scheduler, and an overlap limit above 1 corrupt
    95	# training together, and no pair of the three does: the reloaded residual races its consumer in
    96	# backward, so a restored run reports a first-step loss near 9.5 instead of its checkpoint's
    97	# (#8317). Overlap 4 gained 0.07 MFU, so the offload forces this value rather than defaulting it.
    98	OFFLOAD_CARRY_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1
    99	RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION = "ragged_all_to_all"
   100	# TODO(https://github.com/marin-community/marin/issues/5675): Re-enable XLA GPU
   101	# command buffers after the CUDA graph failure is fixed.
   102	XLA_DISABLE_GPU_COMMAND_BUFFER_FLAG = "--xla_gpu_enable_command_buffer="
   103	_RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAG_NAMES = frozenset(flag.partition("=")[0] for flag in RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAGS)
   104	PJRT_DISTRIBUTION = "jax-cuda13-pjrt"
   105	_FP32_POLICY = jmp.get_policy("params=float32,compute=float32,output=float32")

   108	class WatchMode(StrEnum):
   109	    """Where a watched training step computes gradient and parameter statistics."""
   110	
   111	    INLINE = "inline"
   112	    DIAGNOSTIC = "diagnostic"
   113	
   114	
   115	class MasterParamMode(StrEnum):
   116	    """Where the authoritative fp32 weights live.
   117	
   118	    DEVICE keeps them as the device params themselves, with no separate master copy;
   119	    FP32_PINNED_HOST keeps a pinned-host fp32 master while the device params are its bf16 cast.
   120	    """
   121	
   122	    DEVICE = "device"
   123	    FP32_PINNED_HOST = "fp32_pinned_host"
   124	
   125	
   126	class TrainingDataMode(StrEnum):
   127	    """Source of training batches."""
   128	
   129	    MIXTURE = "mixture"
   130	    SYNTHETIC = "synthetic"
   131	
   132	
   133	def restore_template_from(state):
   134	    """ShapeDtypeStructs carrying each leaf's concrete sharding, releasing the leaves.
   135	
   136	    `jax.eval_shape` drops the `pinned_host` memory kind that `initial_state` puts on
   137	    offloaded optimizer state, which is most of a hero checkpoint. Reading the sharding off a
   138	    built state keeps it.
   139	    """
   140	    template = jax.tree.map(
   141	        lambda leaf: (
   142	            jax.ShapeDtypeStruct(leaf.shape, leaf.dtype, sharding=leaf.sharding) if isinstance(leaf, jax.Array) else leaf
   143	        ),
   144	        state,
   145	    )
   146	    jax.tree.map(lambda leaf: leaf.delete() if isinstance(leaf, jax.Array) else None, state)
   147	    gc.collect()
   148	    return template
   149	
   150	
   151	def checkpoint_stores_master(candidate: str) -> bool:
   152	    """Whether checkpoint ``candidate``'s manifest lists fp32 master parameters.
   153	
   154	    A missing manifest raises ``FileNotFoundError``, which restore treats like any other
   155	    unreadable candidate.
   156	    """
   157	    manifest = read_manifest(candidate)
   158	    if manifest is None:
   159	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{candidate} has no manifest.json, so its layout cannot be read")
   160	    markers = (MASTER_PARAMS_KEY, f"{LEGACY_STATE_KEY}/{MASTER_PARAMS_KEY}")
   161	    return any(path == marker or path.startswith(marker + "/") for path in manifest.array_paths for marker in markers)
   162	
   163	
   164	def template_for_candidate_layout(
   165	    state: "GrugTrainState", candidate: str, run_mode: MasterParamMode
   166	) -> "GrugTrainState":
   167	    """Pick the template restore reads checkpoint ``candidate`` with.
   168	
   169	    Restore reads only the leaves the template names and takes each dtype from storage, checking
   170	    neither against the checkpoint, so reading a master-bearing checkpoint with the run's own
   171	    master-less template would silently return the bf16 compute copy. When the layouts match the
   172	    template is ``state`` itself. A master-bearing checkpoint read by a master-less run migrates
   173	    in process: the run's device fp32 ``params`` template is presented under ``master_params``, so
   174	    the checkpoint's authoritative fp32 master loads directly into it and the bf16 copy goes
   175	    unread; ``take_master_as_params`` then moves it back, and the next save writes the new layout.
   176	    """
   177	    has_master = checkpoint_stores_master(candidate)
   178	    if has_master == (run_mode != MasterParamMode.DEVICE):
   179	        return state
   180	    if not has_master:
   181	        raise ValueError(
   182	            f"checkpoint {candidate} stores no master parameters, but this run trains with {run_mode}. "
   183	            "Synthesizing a master from stored weights is not a conversion this supports."
   184	        )
   185	    logger.info("Checkpoint %s stores a pinned-host fp32 master; restoring it as the parameters.", candidate)
   186	    return dataclasses.replace(state, params=None, master_params=state.params)
   187	
   188	
   189	def take_master_as_params(state: "GrugTrainState") -> "GrugTrainState":
   190	    """Move a master restored through ``template_for_candidate_layout`` into ``params``."""
   191	    if state.master_params is None:
   192	        return state
   193	    return dataclasses.replace(state, params=state.master_params, master_params=None)
   194	
   195	
   196	def _apply_hero_ep_runtime_defaults(
   197	    *,
   198	    inline_watch_enabled: bool,
   199	    moe_implementation: MoeImplementation | None,
   200	    remat_mode: RematMode,
   201	    processes_per_task: int = 1,
   202	) -> None:
   203	    env_defaults = dict(HERO_EP_RUNTIME_ENV)
   204	    if processes_per_task > 1:
   205	        # With one process per GPU, the per-process CUPTI sessions collide with each
   206	        # other and with CoreWeave's DCGM, so PGLE cannot profile and its recompile
   207	        # machinery only adds failure modes. Default it off; an explicit env wins.
   208	        env_defaults["JAX_ENABLE_PGLE"] = "false"
   209	    for name, value in env_defaults.items():
   210	        os.environ.setdefault(name, value)
   211	    xla_flags = os.environ.get("XLA_FLAGS", "").split()
   212	    ragged = moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION
   213	    if ragged:
   214	        overlap_limit = RAGGED_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   215	    elif inline_watch_enabled:
   216	        overlap_limit = INLINE_WATCH_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   217	    else:
   218	        overlap_limit = DEFAULT_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   219	    # The scheduler's longer buffer live ranges fit on the ragged transport only once the layer
   220	    # carry leaves HBM. Without that offload its first-step NCCL allocations fail.
   221	    latency_hiding = not ragged or remat_mode == "offload_carry"
   222	    flag_defaults = (
   223	        f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={overlap_limit}",
   224	        f"--xla_gpu_enable_latency_hiding_scheduler={'true' if latency_hiding else 'false'}",
   225	        # The scheduler sizes the single `jit_train_step` temp arena against this percentage of
   226	        # its memory budget, roughly `133.6 GiB x percentage`. The pool holds 138.2 GiB and
   227	        # persistent state occupies 18.1 GiB of it, so an arena above 120.2 GiB cannot be served
   228	        # from pool free space and forces a fresh mapping against the ~17 GiB of physical memory
   229	        # outside the pool. The default 95 asks for 125.7 GiB and fails that way. 85 sizes the
   230	        # arena at 113.6 GiB, leaving enough slack for per-node variation in fragmentation. A
   231	        # lower percentage costs throughput, because a smaller arena makes `HloRematerialization`
   232	        # recompute more of the step.
   233	        "--xla_gpu_memory_limit_slop_factor=85",
   234	        XLA_DISABLE_GPU_COMMAND_BUFFER_FLAG,
   235	    )
   236	    explicit_names = {flag.partition("=")[0] for flag in xla_flags}
   237	    xla_flags.extend(flag for flag in flag_defaults if flag.partition("=")[0] not in explicit_names)
   238	    if remat_mode == "offload_carry":
   239	        # Not overridable, unlike the defaults above, including the scheduler flag it pairs with.
   240	        # Both flags can be inherited from another recipe, but only this one fails silently: a
   241	        # wrong overlap limit corrupts training, while an inherited scheduler setting either
   242	        # aborts in NCCL's first-step allocation and names itself, or runs the slower posture.
   243	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] != XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG]
   244	        xla_flags.append(f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={OFFLOAD_CARRY_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT}")
   245	    if ragged:
   246	        # Unlike the defaults above, these are not overridable. Selecting the host-launched
   247	        # one-shot kernel needs both flags cleared together plus a splits-per-peer count this
   248	        # branch no longer carries, so honoring a partial override would run a configuration
   249	        # nothing here measures. Drop any conflicting entry rather than relying on which
   250	        # occurrence XLA's parser keeps.
   251	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] not in _RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAG_NAMES]
   252	        xla_flags.extend(RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAGS)
   253	    os.environ["XLA_FLAGS"] = " ".join(xla_flags)
   254	
   255	
   256	def verify_ragged_pjrt() -> None:
   257	    """Raise unless this process runs Marin's patched GPU PJRT plugin."""
   258	    try:
   259	        installed = importlib.metadata.version(PJRT_DISTRIBUTION)
   260	    except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError as missing:
   261	        raise RuntimeError(
   262	            f"{PJRT_DISTRIBUTION} is not installed, so this process has no GPU PJRT plugin at all."
   263	        ) from missing
   264	    expected_prefix = f"{jax.__version__}+marin."
   265	    if not installed.startswith(expected_prefix):
   266	        raise RuntimeError(
   267	            f"{RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION} needs Marin's patched {PJRT_DISTRIBUTION} "
   268	            f"({expected_prefix}*), found {installed}. The patched wheel is aarch64-only and the "
   269	            "expert MLP is SM100-specialized; run the ragged transport on GB200."
   270	        )
   271	
   272	
   273	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   274	class GrugTrainerConfig:
   275	    """Runtime knobs for grug training."""
   860	
   861	        watch_stats = None
   862	        if watch_config is not None:
   863	            watch_stats = compute_watch_stats(
   864	                watch_targets=watch_targets,
   865	                include_norms=watch_config.include_norms,
   866	                include_per_parameter_norms=watch_config.include_per_parameter_norms,
   867	                include_histogram=watch_config.include_histograms,
   868	                split_scan_layers=watch_config.split_scan_layers,
   869	                params=qb_params,
   870	                grads=grads,
   871	                updates=updates,
   872	                opt_state=opt_state_in,
   873	                model_tree_type=type(state.params),
   874	            )
   875	
   876	        if offload_opt_state:
   877	            opt_state = _tree_to_memory_kind(opt_state, "pinned_host")
   878	
   879	        next_state = dataclasses.replace(
   880	            state,
   881	            step=state.step + one,
   882	            params=params,
   883	            master_params=master_params,
   884	            opt_state=opt_state,
   885	            ema_params=ema_params,
   886	            pending_qb_betas=metrics["qb_beta_per_layer"],
   887	        )
   888	
   889	        return next_state, metrics, watch_stats
   890	
   891	    return train_step
   892	
   893	
   894	def _run_grug_local(config: GrugRunConfig) -> None:
   895	    """Entry point for the grug template training loop."""
   896	    if config.model.moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION:
   897	        verify_ragged_pjrt()
   898	    if config.tensorstore_cache_bytes is not None:
   899	        set_jagged_array_read_cache_bytes(config.tensorstore_cache_bytes)
   900	
   901	    trainer = config.trainer.trainer
   902	    trainer.initialize()
   903	    levanter.tracker.log_configuration(config)
   904	
   905	    run_id = trainer.id
   906	    if run_id is None:
   907	        raise ValueError("trainer.id was not initialized")
   908	
   909	    optimizer = config.optimizer.build(trainer.num_train_steps)
   910	    watch_config = trainer.watch

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Submit one hero EP64 arm for the 8753-mfu loop (research/mcwitt/8753-mfu-loop, #8753 head base).
     3	#
     4	# Protocol (fixed for every iteration so arms stay comparable; see DESIGN.md):
     5	#   - one NVL72 rack on cw-us-east-08a, PRODUCTION priority allowed with job RUNTIME <1h
     6	#     (user 2026-08-28): ARM_TIMEOUT 28800s covers Kueue queue wait plus run (occupancy is
     7	#     intrinsically bounded by the absolute NUM_STEPS stop); watchdog.sh COMPILE_CEILING=1800 +
     8	#     STEP_BUDGET=1200 enforce the runtime cap from admission
     9	#   - restore from the live hero's step-30000 checkpoint, mixture data (trained-router routing);
    10	#     NUM_STEPS is the ABSOLUTE stop step (checkpoint step + window), never a relative count
    11	#   - no checkpoints, no eval; profiler off on SCORED arms (a profile tail on an otherwise-scored
    12	#     draw is allowed: PROFILE_STEPS>0 with PROFILE_START_STEP as an ABSOLUTE step past the
    13	#     scoring window, e.g. 30021)
    14	#   - no MARIN_PJRT_WHEEL overlay: the branch's uv.lock pins jax_cuda13_pjrt 0.11.1+marin.c9526e8c0272
    15	#   - JAX_COMPILATION_CACHE_DIR rotated per run id (clique-init deadlock dodge); a RESUBMITTED
    16	#     arm must use a fresh RID (and VERSION): reusing one merges W&B histories, reuses the
    17	#     leader-populated compile cache (clique-deadlock recipe), and can vacuously reuse artifacts
    18	#
    19	# usage: RID=<run-id> VERSION=<calver> [REPO=<worktree>] [EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS="..."] arm.sh
    20	set -euo pipefail
    21	
    22	: "${WANDB_API_KEY:?set WANDB_API_KEY}"
    23	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    24	VERSION="${VERSION:?set VERSION -- bump it per arm or the artifact layer reuses the last run}"
    25	
    26	# The watchdog addresses jobs at /mwittmann/...; make the submission land there no matter what
    27	# the caller's shell has. (-e below only sets the remote env.)
    28	export IRIS_USER=mwittmann
    29	
    30	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    31	REPO="${REPO:-$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)}"
    32	
    33	# Iris bundles the working tree, not HEAD: uncommitted edits (including another session writing
    34	# to a shared checkout) would ship silently and break arm attribution. Only the loop dir's DATA
    35	# files (logs, tsv, review transcripts) are exempt; uncommitted protocol scripts count as dirty,
    36	# since arms.tsv records HEAD as the arm's provenance.
    37	dirty="$(git -C "$REPO" status --porcelain | grep -Ev "autoresearch/.*\.(log|out|txt|tsv)$" || true)"
    38	if [ -z "${ALLOW_DIRTY:-}" ] && [ -n "$dirty" ]; then
    39	  echo "refusing to submit from a dirty tree (set ALLOW_DIRTY=1 to override):" >&2
    40	  echo "$dirty" >&2
    41	  exit 1
    42	fi
    43	
    44	# JAX_* env leaks into train tasks via dispatch forwarding, and this fork enables pipelined host
    45	# offloading at optimization level O1+ regardless of its flag -- a leaked level would silently
    46	# turn a control into an H10 treatment.
    47	if [ -n "${JAX_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL:-}" ]; then
    48	  echo "JAX_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL is set (${JAX_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL}); unset it -- it changes compiler passes behind the arms' backs" >&2
    49	  exit 1
    50	fi
    51	
    52	# A flag-based treatment must be deliberate: an ARM_XLA_FLAGS value leaking from the supervisor
    53	# shell into a "control" draw silently runs the treatment and records nothing -- the null delta
    54	# then reads as "dead lever". Controls run with TREATMENT unset and ARM_XLA_FLAGS empty.
    55	if [ -n "${ARM_XLA_FLAGS:-}" ] && [ -z "${TREATMENT:-}" ]; then
    56	  echo "ARM_XLA_FLAGS is set but TREATMENT=1 is not; refusing (is this a contaminated control?)" >&2
    57	  exit 1
    58	fi
    59	
    60	# One RID/VERSION per submission, ever: a reused RID merges W&B histories, resurrects the
    61	# leader-populated compile cache (clique-deadlock recipe), and can vacuously reuse artifacts.
    62	if [ -f "${LOOP_DIR}/arms.tsv" ] && awk -F'\t' -v rid="$RID" -v ver="$VERSION" \
    63	    'NR>1 && ($1==rid || $3==ver) {found=1} END {exit !found}' "${LOOP_DIR}/arms.tsv"; then
    64	  echo "RID ${RID} or VERSION ${VERSION} already appears in arms.tsv; pick fresh ones" >&2
    65	  exit 1
    66	fi
    67	
    68	PRIORITY="${PRIORITY:-production}"
    69	# Covers Kueue queue wait PLUS the run: same-band gangs queue FIFO behind whatever holds the
    70	# rack, and the coordinator's timeout clock runs while its child sits in the gate. Occupancy
    71	# after admission is intrinsically bounded by NUM_STEPS (~30 steps past restore); the watchdog
    72	# enforces the <1h-runtime rule from admission.
    73	ARM_TIMEOUT="${ARM_TIMEOUT:-28800}"
    74	NUM_STEPS="${NUM_STEPS:-30030}"
    75	TRAINING_DATA="${TRAINING_DATA:-mixture}"
    76	MASTER_PARAMS="${MASTER_PARAMS:-device}"
    77	MOE_IMPL="${MOE_IMPL:-ragged_all_to_all}"
    78	RESTORE_FROM="${RESTORE_FROM:-s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin/grug/hero-12d8b6f0-dee637/2026.08.19.2/checkpoints/step-30000}"
    79	RESTORE_ARGS=()
    80	if [ -n "$RESTORE_FROM" ]; then
    81	  RESTORE_ARGS=(--restore-from "$RESTORE_FROM")
    82	fi
    83	# The learning-rate schedule's length; fixed across arms so losses stay comparable.
    84	SCHEDULE_STEPS=4470000
    85	
    86	read -r -a EXTRA <<<"${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-}"
    87	
    88	cd "$REPO"
    89	uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml job run \
    90	  --no-wait --enable-extra-resources \
    91	  --target-cluster cw-us-east-08a --priority "${PRIORITY}" \
    92	  --cpu 2 --memory 8GB --disk 32GB --timeout "${ARM_TIMEOUT}" \
    93	  --job-name "${RID}-coord" \
    94	  -e IRIS_USER mwittmann \
    95	  -e WANDB_API_KEY "${WANDB_API_KEY}" \
    96	  -e WANDB_PROJECT marin_moe \
    97	  -e MARIN_PREFIX s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin \
    98	  -e IRIS_PORT_JAX 32703 \
    99	  -e AWS_MAX_ATTEMPTS 25 -e AWS_RETRY_MODE adaptive \
   100	  -e JAX_COMPILATION_CACHE_DIR "s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin/tmp/ttl=30d/jaxcache/${RID}" \
   101	  -e XLA_FLAGS "${ARM_XLA_FLAGS:-}" \
   102	  -e XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_MEM_FRACTION "${MEM_FRACTION:-0.75}" \
   103	  -e TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL 0 \
   104	  -e TF_CPP_VMODULE "hlo_rematerialization=1,execution_stream_assignment=1,collective_pipeliner=1" \
   105	  -- python -m experiments.grug.moe_hero_ep.launch_diagnostics \
   106	     --run-id "${RID}" \
   107	     --dp-racks 1 --num-steps "${NUM_STEPS}" --schedule-steps "${SCHEDULE_STEPS}" \
   108	     --capacity-factor 1.15 \
   109	     --moe-implementation "${MOE_IMPL}" \
   110	     --processes-per-task 4 \
   111	     --master-params "${MASTER_PARAMS}" \
   112	     --training-data "${TRAINING_DATA}" \
   113	     --profile-steps "${PROFILE_STEPS:-0}" --profile-start-step "${PROFILE_START_STEP:-30021}" \
   114	     --watch-interval 0 --eval-every 0 \
   115	     --no-save-checkpoints \
   116	     "${RESTORE_ARGS[@]}" \
   117	     "${EXTRA[@]}" \
   118	     --version "${VERSION}" --run >"${LOOP_DIR}/${RID}-submit.log" 2>&1 \
   119	  || { echo "submit FAILED, tail of ${RID}-submit.log:" >&2; tail -20 "${LOOP_DIR}/${RID}-submit.log" >&2; exit 1; }
   120	
   121	printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' \
   122	  "${RID}" "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse --short HEAD)" "${VERSION}" "${MOE_IMPL}" "${TRAINING_DATA}" \
   123	  "${MASTER_PARAMS}" "${SCHEDULE_STEPS}" "${ARM_TIMEOUT}" "${RESTORE_FROM:-none}" \
   124	  "${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-none}" "${ARM_XLA_FLAGS:-none}" "${MEM_FRACTION:-0.75}" >> "${LOOP_DIR}/arms.tsv"
   125	
   126	echo "submitted ${RID} at ${PRIORITY} priority (timeout ${ARM_TIMEOUT}s, commit $(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse --short HEAD), data ${TRAINING_DATA}, extra: ${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-none}, xla_flags: ${ARM_XLA_FLAGS:-none}, memfrac: ${MEM_FRACTION:-0.75})"
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Out-of-process runtime cap, early release, and anomaly alerting for one arm.
     3	#
     4	# Exits (each prints one WATCHDOG marker line; the harness alerts on process exit, so exiting
     5	# fast IS the alert):
     6	#   1. Step budget: cancel STEP_BUDGET seconds after the first step this watch observes. Compile
     7	#      time varies run to run; an arm that pays a slow compile still gets its full measurement
     8	#      window, but an arm whose change makes *steps* slow gets no extra rack time.
     9	#   2. Compile ceiling: cancel if no step has appeared COMPILE_CEILING seconds after submission.
    10	#   3. Early release: cancel as soon as W&B shows the last step the scoring window needs.
    11	#   4. Preemption or task failure: `job describe` publishes failures= and preemptions= counters.
    12	#      Either going nonzero invalidates the measurement (a rescheduled gang recompiles and the
    13	#      step timings straddle the gap), so cancel immediately instead of letting the retry burn
    14	#      the ceiling.
    15	#   5. Coordinator death before the train job exists (e.g. a config guard raising at dispatch):
    16	#      previously this burned the whole compile ceiling; now it exits within one poll.
    17	# Iris's own --timeout on the coord job is the outer backstop; it must cover
    18	# COMPILE_CEILING + STEP_BUDGET. This watchdog runs in a separate process so a wedged CLI or a
    19	# lost session cannot defeat it.
    20	#
    21	# State polls are cheap (one describe RPC), so they run every POLL=20s; the W&B step query is
    22	# heavier and runs every third poll.
    23	#
    24	# usage: RID=<run-id> [STEP_BUDGET=900] [COMPILE_CEILING=1200] [SCORE_MAX_STEP=19] watchdog.sh
    25	set -uo pipefail
    26	
    27	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    28	STEP_BUDGET="${STEP_BUDGET:-1200}"
    29	COMPILE_CEILING="${COMPILE_CEILING:-1800}"
    30	SCORE_MAX_STEP="${SCORE_MAX_STEP:-19}"
    31	POLL="${POLL:-20}"
    32	STEP_POLL_EVERY="${STEP_POLL_EVERY:-3}"
    33	# A campaign arm restores from step-30000; a first step below this means restore silently fell
    34	# back to scratch (or the wrong checkpoint) and the whole measurement is invalid.
    35	EXPECTED_MIN_FIRST_STEP="${EXPECTED_MIN_FIRST_STEP:-30000}"
    36	
    37	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    38	REPO="$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)"
    39	IRIS=(uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml)
    40	COORD="/mwittmann/${RID}-coord"
    41	TRAIN="${COORD}/grug-train-${RID}"
    42	start=$(date +%s)
    43	# A production submission may queue behind another job, so the compile ceiling starts when the
    44	# train job is first observed running, not at submission. QUEUE_CEILING bounds the pending phase
    45	# (iris reports a Kueue-gated gang as "running", so this is the only queue bound that works).
    46	QUEUE_CEILING="${QUEUE_CEILING:-25200}"
    47	deadline=$(( start + QUEUE_CEILING ))
    48	run_seen=-1
    49	first_step=-1
    50	steps=-1
    51	tick=0
    52	
    53	cancel_arm() {  # cancel_arm <reason>
    54	  echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) cancelling ${COORD}: $1"
    55	  # Cancel the train job by its own path as well as through the coordinator. Iris's --timeout may
    56	  # already have taken the coordinator down, and a cancel routed only through a dead parent is
    57	  # exactly how a 16-node child gets orphaned on the rack.
    58	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$TRAIN" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    59	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$COORD" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    60	  # A cancel that silently failed leaves a 16-node gang holding the rack while the supervisor
    61	  # submits the next arm. Verify the train job actually reached a terminal state; if it does not,
    62	  # say so loudly and exit nonzero so the supervisor stops instead of stacking arms.
    63	  for _ in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do
    64	    read -r vstate _ _ <<<"$(state_line_of "$TRAIN")" || true
    65	    case "${vstate:-gone}" in
    66	      succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed|gone) return 0;;
    67	    esac
    68	    sleep 10
    69	  done
    70	  echo "WATCHDOG CANCEL-UNVERIFIED train=${vstate:-?} -- do NOT submit the next arm until the rack is confirmed free"
    71	  exit 2
    72	}
    73	
    74	state_line_of() {  # state_line_of <job path> -> "state failures preemptions" (empty if absent)
    75	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 120 "${IRIS[@]}" job describe "$1" 2>/dev/null \
    76	      | sed -n 's/^State: \([a-z_]*\).*failures=\([0-9]*\).*preemptions=\([0-9]*\).*/\1 \2 \3/p' | head -1 )
    77	}
    78	
    79	max_step() {
    80	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 180 uv run --with wandb python - "$RID" <<'PY' 2>/dev/null
    81	import sys, wandb
    82	try:
    83	    run = wandb.Api().run(f"marin-community/marin_moe/{sys.argv[1]}")
    84	except Exception:
    85	    print(-2); raise SystemExit
    86	steps = [row.get("_step", -1) for row in run.scan_history(keys=["_step", "throughput/mfu"])]
    87	print(max(steps) if steps else -1)
    88	PY
    89	  )
    90	}
    91	
    92	while :; do
    93	  now=$(date +%s)
    94	  if [ "$now" -ge "$deadline" ]; then
    95	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
    96	      cancel_arm "step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s past first step reached"
    97	      echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap step-budget"
    98	    else
    99	      if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -ge 0 ]; then
   100	        cancel_arm "no step within the ${COMPILE_CEILING}s compile ceiling"
   101	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap compile-ceiling"
   102	      else
   103	        cancel_arm "still queued after ${QUEUE_CEILING}s"
   104	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap queue-ceiling"
   105	      fi
   106	    fi
   107	    exit 0
   108	  fi
   109	
   110	  read -r train_state train_failures train_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$TRAIN")" || true
   111	  if [ -z "${train_state:-}" ]; then
   112	    # No train job yet: the coordinator is either still dispatching or died before dispatch.
   113	    read -r coord_state coord_failures coord_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$COORD")" || true
   114	    case "${coord_state:-}" in
   115	      succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   116	        cancel_arm "coordinator ${coord_state} before the train job existed"
   117	        echo "WATCHDOG coord-terminal state=${coord_state} failures=${coord_failures:-?}"
   118	        exit 0;;
   119	    esac
   120	  fi
   121	  if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${first_step:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${train_state:-}" = "running" ]; then
   122	    # Iris can report a gang "running" while another job still holds the rack; the W&B run is
   123	    # only created once distributed init actually completes, so gate the compile clock on it.
   124	    # The first_step guard keeps this from re-arming the longer compile deadline over an
   125	    # already-started step budget when the run-existence probe failed transiently earlier.
   126	    probe="$(max_step)"
   127	    if [ "${probe:--2}" -ge -1 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   128	      run_seen=$now
   129	      deadline=$(( now + COMPILE_CEILING ))
   130	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) gang up (wandb run exists), compile ceiling ${COMPILE_CEILING}s starts now"
   131	    fi
   132	  fi
   133	  if [ "${train_preemptions:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   134	    cancel_arm "train job preempted (preemptions=${train_preemptions})"
   135	    echo "WATCHDOG preempted train=${train_state} preemptions=${train_preemptions} step=${steps}"
   136	    exit 0
   137	  fi
   138	  if [ "${train_failures:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   139	    cancel_arm "train task failed (failures=${train_failures})"
   140	    echo "WATCHDOG task-failed train=${train_state} failures=${train_failures} step=${steps}"
   141	    exit 0
   142	  fi
   143	  case "${train_state:-}" in
   144	    succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   145	      echo "WATCHDOG terminal train=${train_state} step=${steps}"; exit 0;;
   146	  esac
   147	
   148	  if [ $(( tick % STEP_POLL_EVERY )) -eq 0 ]; then
   149	    steps="$(max_step)"
   150	    echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) t=$(( now - start ))s train=${train_state:-none} step=${steps}"
   151	    # A restored arm resumes at the checkpoint's step, so progress is counted from the first step
   152	    # this watch actually observed rather than from zero.
   153	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${steps:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   154	      if [ "$steps" -lt "$EXPECTED_MIN_FIRST_STEP" ]; then
   155	        # Restore silently fell back to scratch (or the wrong checkpoint): the run is logging
   156	        # steps below the checkpoint step. Nothing it measures is valid; kill it immediately.
   157	        cancel_arm "first logged step ${steps} < expected ${EXPECTED_MIN_FIRST_STEP} (restore fell back)"
   158	        echo "WATCHDOG wrong-first-step step=${steps} expected>=${EXPECTED_MIN_FIRST_STEP}"
   159	        exit 1
   160	      fi
   161	      first_step="$steps"
   162	      deadline=$(( now + STEP_BUDGET ))
   163	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) first observed step=${first_step}, step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s starts now"
   164	    fi
   165	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null && [ $(( steps - first_step )) -ge "$SCORE_MAX_STEP" ]; then
   166	      cancel_arm "scored through step ${steps} (${SCORE_MAX_STEP} past first)"
   167	      echo "WATCHDOG scored step=${steps} first=${first_step}"
   168	      exit 0
   169	    fi
   170	  fi
   171	  tick=$(( tick + 1 ))
   172	  sleep "$POLL"
   173	done

     1	# Copyright The Levanter Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Terminate training after a step or the surrounding pipeline stops progressing."""
     5	
     6	import logging
     7	import os
     8	import threading
     9	from dataclasses import dataclass
    10	from datetime import timedelta
    11	from time import monotonic
    12	from typing import Any, Callable
    13	
    14	from levanter.callbacks._core import Callback, ProgressEvent, StepInfo
    15	
    16	
    17	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18	
    19	_WATCHDOG_POLL_INTERVAL = 60.0
    20	_DEFAULT_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD = timedelta(hours=1)
    21	STALLED_TRAINING_EXIT_CODE = 124
    22	
    23	
    24	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25	class ProgressTimeout:
    26	    """Progress deadline that triggered watchdog termination."""
    27	
    28	    event: ProgressEvent
    29	    elapsed: float
    30	    timeout: float
    31	
    32	
    33	class ProgressWatchdog(Callback[Any]):
    34	    """Watch explicit training lifecycle events with step and process deadlines."""
    35	
    36	    def __init__(
    37	        self,
    38	        *,
    39	        step_timeout: timedelta | None,
    40	        process_timeout: timedelta | None,
    41	        startup_timeout: timedelta | None = None,
    42	        startup_grace_period: timedelta = _DEFAULT_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD,
    43	        diagnostic: Callable[[ProgressTimeout], None] | None = None,
    44	        diagnostic_timeout: timedelta | None = None,
    45	        poll_interval: float = _WATCHDOG_POLL_INTERVAL,
    46	    ) -> None:
    47	        if step_timeout is None and process_timeout is None and startup_timeout is None:
    48	            raise ValueError("at least one watchdog timeout must be set")
    49	        if startup_timeout is not None and startup_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
    50	            raise ValueError("startup_timeout must be positive")
    51	        if step_timeout is not None and step_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
    52	            raise ValueError("step_timeout must be positive")
    53	        if process_timeout is not None and process_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
    54	            raise ValueError("process_timeout must be positive")
    55	        if startup_grace_period.total_seconds() < 0:
    56	            raise ValueError("startup_grace_period must be non-negative")
    57	        if diagnostic is not None and diagnostic_timeout is None:
    58	            raise ValueError("diagnostic_timeout is required when diagnostic is set")
    59	        if diagnostic_timeout is not None and diagnostic_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
    60	            raise ValueError("diagnostic_timeout must be positive")
    61	        if poll_interval <= 0:
    62	            raise ValueError("poll_interval must be positive")
    63	
    64	        self._startup_timeout = startup_timeout.total_seconds() if startup_timeout is not None else None
    65	        self._step_timeout = step_timeout.total_seconds() if step_timeout is not None else None
    66	        self._process_timeout = process_timeout.total_seconds() if process_timeout is not None else None
    67	        self._startup_grace_period = startup_grace_period.total_seconds()
    68	        self._diagnostic = diagnostic
    69	        self._diagnostic_timeout = diagnostic_timeout.total_seconds() if diagnostic_timeout is not None else None
    70	        self._poll_interval = poll_interval
    71	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    72	        self._created_at = monotonic()
    73	        self._completed_training_step = False
    74	        self._training_started_at: float | None = None
    75	        self._active_step_started_at: float | None = None
    76	        self._last_progress: tuple[ProgressEvent, float] | None = None
    77	        self._stop = threading.Event()
    78	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name="levanter-progress-watchdog", daemon=True)
    79	        self._thread.start()
    80	
    81	    def on_step(self, info: StepInfo[Any], force: bool = False) -> None:
    82	        del info, force
    83	
    84	    def on_event(self, event: ProgressEvent) -> None:
    85	        if event is ProgressEvent.TRAINING_FINISHED:
    86	            self.stop()
    87	            return
    88	
    89	        now = monotonic()
    90	        with self._lock:
    91	            if self._stop.is_set():
    92	                return
    93	
    94	            if event is ProgressEvent.TRAIN_STEP_STARTED and self._training_started_at is None:
    95	                self._training_started_at = now
    96	
    97	            if event is ProgressEvent.TRAIN_STEP_STARTED and self._completed_training_step:
    98	                self._active_step_started_at = now
    99	            else:
   100	                self._active_step_started_at = None
   101	
   102	            if event is ProgressEvent.TRAIN_STEP_FINISHED:
   103	                self._completed_training_step = True
   104	
   105	            if self._completed_training_step:
   106	                self._last_progress = (event, now)
   107	
   108	    def stop(self) -> None:
   109	        self._stop.set()
   110	        self._thread.join(timeout=self._poll_interval * 2)
   111	
   112	    def _run(self) -> None:
   113	        while not self._stop.wait(self._poll_interval):
   114	            now = monotonic()
   115	            with self._lock:
   116	                completed_training_step = self._completed_training_step
   117	                training_started_at = self._training_started_at
   118	                active_step_started_at = self._active_step_started_at
   119	                last_progress = self._last_progress
   120	
   121	            if not completed_training_step:
   122	                # No step has completed, so the process is still restoring, building caches, or
   123	                # compiling its first step. Elapsed time is all that bounds this: the step deadline
   124	                # stays unarmed until a step completes, and the process deadline has no progress
   125	                # event to measure from.
   126	                if self._startup_timeout is not None and now - self._created_at >= self._startup_timeout:
   127	                    self._terminate(ProgressEvent.PROCESS_STARTED, now - self._created_at, self._startup_timeout)
   128	                    return
   129	                continue
   130	
   131	            if training_started_at is None or now - training_started_at < self._startup_grace_period:
   132	                continue
   133	
   134	            if (
   135	                active_step_started_at is not None
   136	                and self._step_timeout is not None
   137	                and now - active_step_started_at >= self._step_timeout
   138	            ):
   139	                self._terminate(ProgressEvent.TRAIN_STEP_STARTED, now - active_step_started_at, self._step_timeout)
   140	                return
   141	
   142	            if last_progress is not None and self._process_timeout is not None:
   143	                event, event_time = last_progress
   144	                if now - event_time >= self._process_timeout:
   145	                    self._terminate(event, now - event_time, self._process_timeout)
   146	                    return
   147	
   148	    def _terminate(self, event: ProgressEvent, elapsed: float, timeout: float) -> None:
   149	        self._stop.set()
   150	        try:
   151	            logger.critical(
   152	                "No progress after %s for %.1f seconds (timeout %.1f); terminating with exit code %d",
   153	                event.value,
   154	                elapsed,
   155	                timeout,
   156	                STALLED_TRAINING_EXIT_CODE,
   157	            )
   158	            if self._diagnostic is not None:
   159	                assert self._diagnostic_timeout is not None
   160	                diagnostic = threading.Thread(
   161	                    target=self._run_diagnostic,
   162	                    args=(ProgressTimeout(event, elapsed, timeout),),
   163	                    name="levanter-progress-diagnostic",
   164	                    daemon=True,
   165	                )
   166	                diagnostic.start()
   167	                diagnostic.join(self._diagnostic_timeout)
   168	                if diagnostic.is_alive():
   169	                    logger.error("Progress diagnostic exceeded its %.1f second budget", self._diagnostic_timeout)
   170	        finally:
   171	            os._exit(STALLED_TRAINING_EXIT_CODE)
   172	
   173	    def _run_diagnostic(self, timeout: ProgressTimeout) -> None:
   174	        try:
   175	            assert self._diagnostic is not None
   176	            self._diagnostic(timeout)
   177	        except Exception:
   178	            logger.exception("Progress diagnostic failed")
   179	
   180	
   181	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   182	class ProgressWatchdogConfig:
   183	    """Configure the startup grace period and training progress deadlines."""
   184	
   185	    step_timeout: timedelta | None = None
   186	    process_timeout: timedelta | None = None
   187	    startup_timeout: timedelta | None = None
   188	    startup_grace_period: timedelta = _DEFAULT_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD
   189	    diagnostic_timeout: timedelta | None = None
   190	
   191	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   192	        if self.step_timeout is not None and self.step_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
   193	            raise ValueError("step_timeout must be positive")
   194	        if self.startup_timeout is not None and self.startup_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
   195	            raise ValueError("startup_timeout must be positive")
   196	        if self.process_timeout is not None and self.process_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
   197	            raise ValueError("process_timeout must be positive")
   198	        if self.startup_grace_period.total_seconds() < 0:
   199	            raise ValueError("startup_grace_period must be non-negative")
   200	        if self.diagnostic_timeout is not None and self.diagnostic_timeout.total_seconds() <= 0:
   201	            raise ValueError("diagnostic_timeout must be positive")
   202	
   203	    def create(
   204	        self,
   205	        *,
   206	        process_index: int = 0,
   207	        diagnostic: Callable[[ProgressTimeout], None] | None = None,
   208	    ) -> ProgressWatchdog | None:
   209	        if self.step_timeout is None and self.process_timeout is None and self.startup_timeout is None:
   210	            return None
   211	        if self.diagnostic_timeout is not None and process_index != 0:
   212	            return None
   213	        if self.diagnostic_timeout is not None and diagnostic is None:
   214	            raise ValueError("diagnostic is required when diagnostic_timeout is set")
   215	        return ProgressWatchdog(
   216	            step_timeout=self.step_timeout,
   217	            process_timeout=self.process_timeout,
   218	            startup_timeout=self.startup_timeout,
   219	            startup_grace_period=self.startup_grace_period,
   220	            diagnostic=diagnostic if self.diagnostic_timeout is not None else None,
   221	            diagnostic_timeout=self.diagnostic_timeout,
   222	        )

The env knob itself is inherited by every local child because the supervisor copies its full environment before adding rank variables. The mismatch case is fail-stop rather than a tolerable numerical skew; I’m checking the pinned XLA barrier/storage code to distinguish an indefinite wait from an address/transport fault and to avoid overstating corruption risk.

   189	  // Launch grid for the device kernel, and the number of per-CTA
   190	  // barrier/signal slots reserved when creating the device communicator (the
   191	  // kernel indexes its cooperative barrier by blockIdx.x, so registration
   192	  // must cover the launched grid). All ranks launch the same grid, which the
   193	  // cross-rank cooperative barriers require: every CTA must be resident
   194	  // simultaneously, so the size is kGridSmMultiplier CTAs of 128 threads per
   195	  // SM. Callers pass the SM count from
   196	  // se::DeviceDescription::core_count(); all participating ranks are expected
   197	  // to be homogeneous so every rank arrives at the same value.
   198	  static int32_t DeviceKernelCtaCount(int core_count) {
   199	    return std::max<int32_t>(kGridSmMultiplier * core_count,
   200	                             kMinDeviceKernelCtaCount);
   201	  }
   202	
   203	  GpuDeviceCommunicator::Requirements DeviceKernelLsaDevCommRequirements(
   204	      int core_count) const {
   205	    GpuDeviceCommunicator::Requirements requirements;
   206	    requirements.lsa_barrier_count = DeviceKernelCtaCount(core_count);
   207	    return requirements;
   208	  }
   209	
   210	  GpuDeviceCommunicator::Requirements DeviceKernelDevCommRequirements(
   211	      int core_count) const {
   212	    GpuDeviceCommunicator::Requirements requirements;
   213	    const int32_t c = DeviceKernelCtaCount(core_count);
   214	    requirements.barrier_count = c;
   215	    requirements.lsa_barrier_count = c;
   216	    requirements.rail_gin_barrier_count = c;
   217	    requirements.gin_signal_count = c;
   218	    requirements.gin_connection_full = true;
   219	    return requirements;
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  // Returns true if one shot kernel is supported
   223	  bool IsOneShotKernelSupported() const;
   224	
   225	  static absl::StatusOr<std::unique_ptr<RaggedAllToAllThunk>> FromProto(
   226	      ThunkInfo thunk_info, const RaggedAllToAllThunkProto& thunk_proto,
   227	      absl::Span<const BufferAllocation> buffer_allocations);
   228	
   229	  absl::StatusOr<ThunkProto> ToProto() const override;
   230	
   231	 protected:
   232	  // No rendezvous needed when using one-shot kernel in local mode instead of
   233	  // NCCL.
   234	  bool RequiresRendezvous() const override {
   235	    return !is_one_shot_kernel_enabled();
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  absl::Status PrepareCollective(const PrepareParams& params,
   239	                                 const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key) override;
   240	
   241	  absl::Status RunCollective(const ExecuteParams& params,
   242	                             const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key, se::Stream& stream,
   243	                             Communicator& comm) override;
   244	
   245	 private:
   246	  bool is_local(int device_count) const;
   247	
   248	  const RaggedAllToAllConfig config_;
   249	
   250	  // Floor on the launch grid so small shapes still get some parallelism.
   251	  // The upper bound is derived from the executor's SM count at Prepare /
   252	  // Initialize / Run time via DeviceKernelCtaCount().
   253	  static constexpr int32_t kMinDeviceKernelCtaCount = 8;
   254	  // Resident CTAs per SM for the device kernel's cooperative grid. With
   255	  // 128-thread CTAs this is 1024 threads/SM; the in-kernel LSA barriers
   256	  // require the whole grid resident, so the product must stay within the
   257	  // architecture's thread- and CTA-residency limits.
   258	  static constexpr int32_t kGridSmMultiplier =
   259	      stream_executor::gpu::kRaggedAllToAllDeviceKernelCtasPerSm;
   260	
   261	  mutable absl::Mutex mutex_;
   262	  absl::flat_hash_map<se::StreamExecutor*,
   263	                      std::unique_ptr<RaggedAllToAllStreamState>>
   264	      per_stream_states_ ABSL_GUARDED_BY(mutex_);
   265	
   266	  absl::StatusOr<RaggedAllToAllStreamState*> InitializeOnce(
   267	      const InitializeParams& params);
   268	};

   270	// Executes the rendezvous to exchange buffer addresses and barrier signal
   271	// buffers.

   278	// Executes an optimized "One-Shot" Ragged All-to-All collective.
   279	//
   280	// Unlike the standard implementation, this approach consolidates the
   281	// coordination and data movement into a single execution path (typically a
   282	// custom kernel or specialized P2P sequence) to reduce host-device
   283	// synchronization overhead.
   284	//
   285	// It utilizes `MultiGpuBarrierKernel` to enforce device-side synchronization.
   286	// This ensures input/output buffers are safe to access without requiring
   287	// Event-based coordination, enabling compatibility with CUDA Graphs.
   288	absl::Status RunOneShotRaggedAllToAll(
   289	    const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key, se::Stream& stream, RankId rank,
   290	    const se::DeviceAddressBase& barrier_signal_buffer,
   291	    const se::DeviceAddressBase& barrier_signal_value,
   292	    int64_t num_total_updates, int64_t num_input_rows, int64_t num_row_elements,
   293	    absl::Span<DeviceBufferPair const> buffers,
   294	    const std::vector<RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue>& participants);
   295	
   296	// It utilizes `MultiGpuBarrierWithNcclKernel` to enforce device-side
   297	// synchronization. This ensures input/output buffers are safe to access without
   298	// requiring Event-based coordination, enabling compatibility with CUDA Graphs.
   299	absl::Status RunOneShotRaggedAllToAllWithNccl(
   300	    const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key, se::Stream& stream, RankId rank,
   301	    std::shared_ptr<xla::SymmetricMemory> barrier_signal_symmetric_memory,
   302	    const se::DeviceAddressBase& barrier_signal_value,
   303	    SymmetricMemory* output_sym_mem, size_t output_sym_offset,
   304	    int64_t num_total_updates, int64_t num_input_rows, int64_t num_row_elements,
   305	    absl::Span<DeviceBufferPair const> buffers);

686-   686	absl::Status RaggedAllToAllThunk::Initialize(const InitializeParams& params) {
687-   687	  ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(CollectiveThunk::Initialize(params));
688-   688

692-   692	  // This can happen in two scenarios:
693-   693	  //   1. This is the first time the thunk is initialized.
694-   694	  //   2. Original NCCL communicator was destroyed and symmetric memory handler
695-   695	  //      was released. NCCL communicator can be destroyed in comm splitting
696-   696	  //      process, but generally it should not change between executions, so
697-   697	  //      it's safe to cache the symmetric handler.
698-   698	  if (state->lsa_size.has_value() &&
699-   699	      state->lsa_size.value() == state->clique_key.num_devices() &&
700:   700	      config_.use_multi_gpu_barrier_with_nccl_in_one_shot_kernel) {

702-   702	                                     state->clique_key, state->rank));
703-   703	
704:   704	    if (state->barrier_signal_symmetric_memory.Expired()) {
705-   705	      ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
706-   706	          auto symmetric_memory,
707:   707	          comm->CreateSymmetricMemory(state->barrier_signal_buffer->address()));
708-   708	
709:   709	      ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(state->barrier_signal_symmetric_memory,
710-   710	                       params.collective_cliques->Tie(
711-   711	                           state->clique_key, std::move(symmetric_memory)));
712-   712	    }
713-   713	  } else if (is_local(params.local_device_count)) {
714:   714	    // Rendezvous - Exchange output pointers and barrier signal buffers.
715-   715	    ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
716-   716	        std::vector<DeviceBufferPair> device_buffers,

724:   724	            device_buffers, state->barrier_signal_buffer->address()));
725-   725	  }

   925	absl::Status RaggedAllToAllThunk::RunCollective(const ExecuteParams& params,
   926	                                                const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key,
   927	                                                se::Stream& stream,
   928	                                                Communicator& comm) {
   929	  ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(std::vector<DeviceBufferPair> device_buffers,
   930	                   ConvertToDeviceBuffers(params.buffer_allocations, buffers(),
   931	                                          config_.config.operand_element_type));
   932	
   933	  ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(bool peer_access_enabled,
   934	                   params.collective_cliques->peer_access_enabled(clique_key));
   935	
   936	  RaggedAllToAllStreamState* state = nullptr;
   937	  {
   938	    absl::MutexLock lock(mutex_);
   939	    state = per_stream_states_[stream.parent()].get();
   940	  }
   941	
   942	  auto* gpu_comm = tsl::down_cast<GpuCommunicator*>(&comm);
   943	  if (UsesDeviceKernel() && gpu_comm->SupportsDeviceComm() &&
   944	      params.collective_memory != nullptr && state->lsa_size.has_value()) {
   945	    auto [input_sym, input_offset] =
   946	        params.collective_memory->FindSymmetricMemory(
   947	            clique_key, device_buffers[0].source_buffer);
   948	    auto [output_sym, output_offset] =
   949	        params.collective_memory->FindSymmetricMemory(
   950	            clique_key, device_buffers[1].destination_buffer);
   951	
   952	    if (input_sym != nullptr && output_sym != nullptr) {
   953	      ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(int32_t num_ranks, comm.NumRanks());
   954	      const int core_count =
   955	          stream.parent()->GetDeviceDescription().core_count();
   956

   958	      const bool has_remote_peers = state->lsa_size.value() < num_ranks;
   959	      if (has_remote_peers && !gpu_comm->SupportsGin()) {
   960	        XLA_VLOG_DEVICE(3, state->device_ordinal)
   961	            << "Device kernel skipped: lsa_size=" << lsa_size
   962	            << " num_ranks=" << num_ranks << " requires GIN";

   965	        ABSL_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
   966	            GpuDeviceCommunicator * dev_comm,
   967	            params.collective_cliques->GetDeviceComm(

   969	                has_remote_peers
   970	                    ? DeviceKernelDevCommRequirements(core_count)
   971	                    : DeviceKernelLsaDevCommRequirements(core_count)));
   972

   974	            config_.num_total_updates / num_ranks;
   975	        const int32_t cta_count = DeviceKernelCtaCount(core_count);
   976	        const PrimitiveType element_type = device_buffers[0].element_type;
   977	
   978	        XLA_VLOG_DEVICE(3, state->device_ordinal)
   979	            << "Device kernel: lsa_size=" << lsa_size
   980	            << " num_ranks=" << num_ranks << " gin=" << gin
   981	            << " cta_count=" << cta_count
   982	            << " num_updates_per_replica=" << num_updates_per_replica
   983	            << " num_row_elements=" << config_.num_row_elements
   984	            << " element_type="
   985	            << primitive_util::LowercasePrimitiveTypeName(element_type);
   986	
   987	        return RunDeviceRaggedAllToAllKernel(

   989	            device_buffers[2].source_buffer, device_buffers[3].source_buffer,
   990	            device_buffers[4].source_buffer, num_ranks, num_updates_per_replica,
   991	            config_.num_row_elements, cta_count,
   992	            static_cast<int64_t>(input_offset),
   993	            static_cast<int64_t>(output_offset));
   994	      }
   995	    }
   996	  }
   997	
   998	  FabricHomogeneity homogeneity =
   999	      CheckFabricHomogeneity(stream.parent(), clique_key);
  1000	  // The fabric is "safe" unless we have confirmed a mismatch.
  1001	  // This allows H100/Borg (kUnknown) to still use one-shot kernels.
  1002	  // FabricInfo queries are unavailable with old driver (e.g. 535.xx).
  1003	  bool fabric_safe = (homogeneity != FabricHomogeneity::kHeterogeneous);
  1004

  1006	    if (use_multi_gpu_barrier_with_nccl_in_one_shot_kernel() &&

  1029	          config_.num_row_elements, device_buffers);

  1031

  1033	        peer_access_enabled &&
  1034	        is_local(params.collective_params->local_device_count)) {
  1035	      return RunOneShotRaggedAllToAll(
  1036	          clique_key, stream, state->rank,
  1037	          state->barrier_signal_buffer
  1038	              ->address(),  // Buff peers write signals to
  1039	          state->barrier_signal_value
  1040	              ->address(),  // Local monotonic step counter
  1041	          config_.num_total_updates, config_.num_input_rows,
  1042	          config_.num_row_elements, device_buffers, *state->participants);
  1043	    }
  1044	  }
  1045	
  1046	  // Get buffer allocs to load sizes and offsets of ragged tensors from device
  1047	  // memory.
  1048	  absl::InlinedVector<int64_t*, 8> ragged_metadata_allocs;
  1049	  ragged_metadata_allocs.reserve(kNumRaggedMetadataOperands);
  1050	  for (int64_t i = 0; i < kNumRaggedMetadataOperands; ++i) {
  1051	    ragged_metadata_allocs.push_back(reinterpret_cast<int64_t*>(
  1052	        state->host_buffer_allocs[i]->address().opaque()));
  1053	  }
  1054	
  1055	  SymmetricMemory* output_sym_mem = nullptr;
  1056	  size_t output_base_offset = 0;
  1057	  if (use_symmetric_memory() && params.collective_memory) {
  1058	    auto [sym, offset] = params.collective_memory->FindSymmetricMemory(
  1059	        clique_key, device_buffers[1].destination_buffer);
  1060	    output_sym_mem = sym;

  1064	        << "returned sym=" << (sym ? "non-null" : "NULL")

  1066	  }
  1067	
  1068	  if (!config_.allow_fallback_to_nccl) {
  1069	    return InvalidArgument(
  1070	        "RaggedAllToAll fallback to NCCL is not allowed. This is likely "
  1071	        "unintentional and caused by a misconfiguration or bug. On MNNVL "
  1072	        "systems (e.g., GB200/GB300), make sure to enable "
  1073	        "`xla_gpu_experimental_ragged_all_to_all_use_barrier_with_nccl`. "

  1089	// Executes the rendezvous to exchange buffer addresses and barrier signal
  1090	// buffers.
  1091	absl::StatusOr<std::shared_ptr<std::vector<RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue>>>
  1092	RendezvousResources(int device_ordinal, RankId rank,
  1093	                    const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key,
  1094	                    const se::DeviceAddressBase& output_buffer,
  1095	                    const se::DeviceAddressBase& barrier_signal_buffer) {
  1096	  int64_t num_ranks = clique_key.num_local_participants();
  1097	
  1098	  RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue rendezvous_value;
  1099	  rendezvous_value.rank = rank;
  1100	  rendezvous_value.output_buffer = output_buffer;
  1101	  rendezvous_value.barrier_signal_buffer = barrier_signal_buffer;
  1102	
  1103	  auto rendezvous_fn =
  1104	      [](absl::Span<const RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue* const> values) {
  1105	        std::vector<RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue> values_copy;
  1106	        for (const auto& value : values) {
  1107	          values_copy.push_back(*value);
  1108	        }
  1109	        // Sort to make sure that values are in the same order as the devices
  1110	        // are ordered in the communicator.
  1111	        absl::c_sort(values_copy);
  1112	        return values_copy;
  1113	      };
  1114	
  1115	  auto rendezvous_name = absl::StrFormat(
  1116	      "[%d] [rank=%v] One-shot ragged-all-to-all rendezvous, clique: %v",
  1117	      device_ordinal, rank, clique_key);
  1118	
  1119	  return Rendezvous<std::vector<RaggedAllToAllRendezvousValue>>(
  1120	      rendezvous_name, clique_key, rendezvous_value, num_ranks, rendezvous_fn);
  1121	}

  1123	absl::Status RaggedAllToAllThunk::PrepareCollective(
  1124	    const PrepareParams& params, const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key) {
  1125	  if (config_.use_multi_gpu_barrier_with_nccl_in_one_shot_kernel ||
  1126	      use_symmetric_memory()) {
  1127	    // Request symmetric memory for the output buffer only.
  1128	    const Buffer& output_buffer = buffers()[1];
  1129	    ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(
  1130	        params.collective_memory_requests->RequestSymmetricAllocationSlice(
  1131	            clique_key, output_buffer.destination_buffer.slice));
  1132	  }
  1133	
  1134	  if (UsesDeviceKernel()) {
  1135	    const Buffer& input_buf = buffers()[0];
  1136	    ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(
  1137	        params.collective_memory_requests->RequestSymmetricAllocation(
  1138	            clique_key, input_buf.source_buffer.slice.index()));
  1139	
  1140	    ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(device_groups().status());
  1141	    CollectiveCliqueRequests::CliqueRequirements gin_reqs;
  1142	    gin_reqs.dev_comm = DeviceKernelDevCommRequirements(
  1143	        params.executor->GetDeviceDescription().core_count());
  1144	    ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(params.collective_clique_requests->RequestClique(
  1145	        clique_key, *device_groups(), gin_reqs));
  1146	  }
  1147	
  1148	  return absl::OkStatus();
  1149	}

  1151	struct CharFormatter {
