  1157	
  1158	// Executes the RaggedAllToAll collective using a "One-Shot" kernel with
  1159	// explicit device-side synchronization.
  1160	// The execution flow is:
  1161	// Prerequisite: Rendezvous - Exchange output buffers and barrier signal
  1162	// buffers with peers.
  1163	// 1. Pre-Kernel Barrier: Wait until all peers are ready to receive data.
  1164	// 2. Execution: Run the RaggedAllToAll kernel (direct P2P writes).
  1165	// 3. Post-Kernel Barrier: Wait until all peers have finished writing.
  1166	absl::Status RunOneShotRaggedAllToAllWithNccl(
  1167	    const GpuCliqueKey& clique_key, se::Stream& stream, RankId rank,
  1168	    std::shared_ptr<xla::SymmetricMemory> barrier_signal_symmetric_memory,
  1169	    const se::DeviceAddressBase& barrier_signal_value,
  1170	    SymmetricMemory* output_sym_mem, size_t output_sym_offset,
  1171	    int64_t num_total_updates, int64_t num_input_rows, int64_t num_row_elements,
  1172	    absl::Span<DeviceBufferPair const> buffers) {
  1173	  int device_ordinal = stream.parent()->device_ordinal();
  1174	  const int64_t num_ranks = clique_key.num_devices();
  1175	
  1176	  XLA_VLOG_DEVICE(3, device_ordinal)
  1177	      << "RaggedAllToAll (One-Shot NCCL) STARTED. Rank: " << rank.value()
  1178	      << ", Total Updates: " << num_total_updates;
  1179	
  1180	  PrimitiveType element_type = buffers[0].element_type;
  1181	  se::DeviceAddressBase input_buffer = buffers[0].source_buffer;
  1182	  se::DeviceAddressBase output_buffer = buffers[1].destination_buffer;
  1183	  XLA_VLOG_DEVICE(4, device_ordinal)
  1184	      << "Performing one-shot ragged-all-to-all "
  1185	         "with NCCL barrier. input buffer: ("
  1186	      << input_buffer.opaque() << ", size=" << input_buffer.size()
  1187	      << ") output buffer: (" << output_buffer.opaque()
  1188	      << ", size=" << output_buffer.size() << ")"
  1189	      << " output sym memory (handle=" << output_sym_mem
  1190	      << ", address=" << output_sym_mem->addr().opaque()
  1191	      << ", size=" << output_sym_mem->addr().size()
  1192	      << ", sym_offset=" << output_sym_offset << ")"
  1193	      << " barrier signal symmetric memory (handle="
  1194	      << barrier_signal_symmetric_memory.get()
  1195	      << ", address=" << barrier_signal_symmetric_memory->addr().opaque()
  1196	      << ", size=" << barrier_signal_symmetric_memory->addr().size() << ")";
  1197	
  1198	  // 1. Barrier (Pre-Kernel)
  1199	  // Global synchronization before P2P writes.
  1200	  // Ensures that all peers have reached this point and their output buffers
  1201	  // are ready to receive data. This prevents the kernel from attempting to
  1202	  // write to a peer's memory before that peer has completed the rendezvous
  1203	  // setup.
  1204	  ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(xla::gpu::LaunchMultiGpuBarrierWithNccl(
  1205	      &stream, num_ranks, rank, barrier_signal_symmetric_memory.get(),
  1206	      barrier_signal_value));
  1207	
  1208	  // 2. Execution of RunRaggedAllToAllKernel
  1209	  const int64_t num_updates_per_replica = num_total_updates / num_ranks;
  1210	
  1211	  if (VLOG_IS_ON(5)) {
  1212	    ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(CheckRaggedAllToAllBounds(stream, num_total_updates,
  1213	                                              num_row_elements, output_sym_mem,
  1214	                                              output_sym_offset, buffers));
  1215	  }
  1216	
  1217	  ABSL_RETURN_IF_ERROR(RunRaggedAllToAllWithSymmetricMemoryKernel(
  1218	      &stream, element_type, input_buffer, output_sym_mem, output_sym_offset,
  1219	      buffers[2].source_buffer, buffers[3].source_buffer,
  1220	      buffers[4].source_buffer, num_ranks, num_updates_per_replica,

   544	def make_pjrt_c_api_client(
   545	    plugin_name: str,
   546	    options: OptionsDict | Callable[[], OptionsDict] | None = None,
   547	) -> xla_client.Client:
   548	  """Creates a PjRt client for the given plugin.
   549	
   550	  Args:
   551	    plugin_name: the name of the plugin.
   552	    options: Optional. It is used when creating a PJRT plugin client. Can be a
   553	      callable, in which case it will be invoked upon plugin initialization
   554	      time, and will be expected to return an option dictionary.
   555	  """
   556	  if not xla_client.pjrt_plugin_initialized(plugin_name):
   557	    xla_client.initialize_pjrt_plugin(plugin_name)
   558	  updated_options: dict[str, Any] = {}
   559	  if options is not None:
   560	    updated_options.update(options() if callable(options) else options)
   561	  updated_options.update(_options_from_jax_configs(plugin_name))
   562	  if distributed.global_state.client is None:
   563	    return xla_client.make_c_api_client(plugin_name, updated_options, None)
   564	
   565	  distribute_options = {
   566	      'node_id': distributed.global_state.process_id,
   567	      'num_nodes': distributed.global_state.num_processes,
   568	  }
   569	  if (partition_index := distributed.global_state.partition_index) is not None:
   570	    distribute_options['partition_index'] = partition_index
   571	  if options is not None:
   572	    distribute_options.update(updated_options)
   573	  return xla_client.make_c_api_client(
   574	      plugin_name,
   575	      distribute_options,
   576	      distributed.global_state.client,
   577	      _make_transfer_server_factory(),
   578	      FORCE_DCN_CROSS_HOST_TRANSFERS.value,
   579	      SORT_DEVICES_BY_PROCESS_INDEX.value,
   580	  )

   583	def register_plugin(
   584	    plugin_name: str,
   585	    *,
   586	    priority: int = 400,
   587	    library_path: str | None = None,
   588	    options: OptionsDict | Callable[[], OptionsDict] | None = None,
   589	    c_api: Any | None = None,
   590	    factory: BackendFactory | None = None,
   591	    make_topology: TopologyFactory | None = None,
   592	) -> Any:
   593	  """Registers a backend factory for the PJRT plugin.
   594	
   595	  Args:
   596	    plugin_name: the name of the plugin.
   597	    priority: the priority this plugin should be registered in jax backends.
   598	      Default to be 400.
   599	    library_path: Optional. The full path to the .so file of the plugin. The
   600	      plugin needs to provide either the library_path or the c_api.
   601	    options: Optional. It is used when creating a PJRT plugin client. Can be a
   602	      callable, in which case it will be invoked upon plugin initialization
   603	      time, and will be expected to return an option dictionary.
   604	    c_api: Optional. The plugin can provide a PJRT C API to be registered.
   605	    factory: Optional. A factory function that creates a PJRT client. If not
   606	      provided, a default factory will be used.
   607	  """
   608	
   609	  if library_path and c_api:
   610	    logger.error(
   611	        "Both library_path and c_api are provided when registering PJRT plugin"
   612	        " %s",
   613	        plugin_name,
   614	    )
   615	    return
   616	  if not library_path and not c_api:
   617	    logger.error(
   618	        "Neither library_path nor c_api provided when registering PJRT plugin"
   619	        " %s",
   620	        plugin_name,
   621	    )
   622	    return
   623	
   624	  if factory is not None and options is not None:
   625	    raise ValueError(
   626	        "Cannot provide both 'factory' and 'options' when registering PJRT"
   627	        " plugin. When providing a custom factory, the factory's must handle"
   628	        " its own options."
   629	    )
   630	  if factory is None:
   631	    factory = partial(make_pjrt_c_api_client, plugin_name, options=options)
   632	
   633	  logger.debug(
   634	      'registering PJRT plugin %s from %s', plugin_name, library_path
   635	  )
   636	  if library_path is not None:
   637	    c_api = xla_client.load_pjrt_plugin_dynamically(plugin_name, library_path)
   638	    _profiler.register_plugin_profiler(c_api)
   639	  else:
   640	    assert c_api is not None
   641	    xla_client.load_pjrt_plugin_with_c_api(plugin_name, c_api)
   642	
   643	  make_topology = make_topology or partial(xla_client.make_c_api_device_topology, c_api)
   644	  experimental = plugin_name not in _nonexperimental_plugins
   645	  register_backend_factory(plugin_name, factory, priority=priority,
   646	                           fail_quietly=False, experimental=experimental,
   647	                           make_topology=make_topology, c_api=c_api)
   648	  return c_api
   649	
   650	
   651	def register_pjrt_plugin_factories_from_env() -> None:
   652	  """Registers backend factories for PJRT plugins.
   653	
   654	  A backend factory will be registered for every PJRT plugin in the input
   655	  string, in the format of 'name1:path1,name2:path2' ('name1;path1,name2;path2'
   656	  for windows). The path can be a path to the plugin library or a path to the
   657	  plugin configuration json file. The json file needs to have a "library_path"
   658	  field for the plugin library path. It can have an optional "create_option"
   659	  field for the options used when creating a PJRT plugin client. The value of
   660	  "create_option" is key-value pairs. Please see xla_client._NameValueMapping
   661	  for the supported types of values.
   662	
   663	  TPU PJRT plugin will be loaded and registered separately in make_tpu_client.
   664	  """
   665	  pjrt_plugins = _get_pjrt_plugin_names_and_library_paths(
   666	      os.getenv('PJRT_NAMES_AND_LIBRARY_PATHS', '')
   667	  )
   668	  for plugin_name, path in pjrt_plugins.items():
   669	    if path.endswith('.json'):
   670	      library_path, options = _get_pjrt_plugin_config(path)
   671	    else:
   672	      library_path = path
   673	      options = None
   674	    logger.debug(
   675	        'registering PJRT plugin %s from %s', plugin_name, library_path
   676	    )
   677	    register_plugin(plugin_name, library_path=library_path, options=options)
   678	
   679	
   680	def _discover_and_register_pjrt_plugins():
   681	  global _plugins_registered
   682	
   683	  # Needs a separate lock because register_backend_factory (called from
   684	  # register_plugin) requires to hold _backend_lock.
   685	  with _plugin_lock:
   686	    if not _plugins_registered:
   687	      # Plugins in the namespace package `jax_plugins` or have an entry-point
   688	      # under the `jax_plugins` group will be imported.
   689	      discover_pjrt_plugins()
   690	      # Registers plugins names and paths set in env var
   691	      # PJRT_NAMES_AND_LIBRARY_PATHS, in the format of 'name1:path1,name2:path2'
   692	      # ('name1;path1,name2;path2' for windows).
   693	      register_pjrt_plugin_factories_from_env()
   694	      with _plugin_callback_lock:
   695	        for factory in _backend_factories.values():
   696	          if factory.c_api is not None:
   697	            for callback in _plugin_callbacks:
   698	              callback(c_api=factory.c_api)
   699	      _plugins_registered = True
   700	
   701	
   702	_platform_aliases = {
   703	  "cuda": "gpu",
   704	  "rocm": "gpu",
   705	  "oneapi": "gpu",
   706	}
   707	
   708	_alias_to_platforms: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   709	for _platform, _alias in _platform_aliases.items():
   710	  _alias_to_platforms.setdefault(_alias, []).append(_platform)
   711	
   712	
   713	def known_platforms() -> set[str]:
   714	  platforms = set()
   715	  platforms |= set(_nonexperimental_plugins)
   716	  platforms |= set(_backend_factories.keys())
   717	  platforms |= set(_platform_aliases.values())
   718	  platforms |= set(_platform_aliases.keys())
   719	  return platforms
   720	
   721	
   722	def is_known_platform(platform: str) -> bool:
   723	  # A platform is valid if there is a registered factory for it. It does not
   724	  # matter if we were unable to initialize that platform; we only care that
   725	  # we've heard of it and it isn't, e.g., a typo.
   726	  return platform in known_platforms()
   727	
   728	
   729	def canonicalize_platform(platform: str) -> str:
   730	  """Replaces platform aliases with their concrete equivalent.
   731	
   732	  In particular, replaces "gpu" with either "cuda", "oneapi" or "rocm", depending on which
   733	  hardware is actually present. We want to distinguish "cuda", "oneapi" and "rocm" for
   734	  purposes such as MLIR lowering rules, but in many cases we don't want to
   735	  force users to care.
   736	  """
   737	  platforms = _alias_to_platforms.get(platform, None)
   738	  if platforms is None:
   739	    return platform
   740	
   741	  b = backends()
   742	  for p in platforms:
   743	    if p in b.keys():
   744	      return p
   745	  raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown backend: '{platform}' requested, but no "
   746	                     f"platforms that are instances of {platform} are present. "
   747	                     "Platforms present are: " + ",".join(b.keys()))
   748	
   749	
   750	def expand_platform_alias(platform: str) -> list[str]:
   751	  """Expands, e.g., "gpu" to ["cuda", "rocm", "oneapi"].
   752	
   753	  This is used for convenience reasons: we expect cuda and rocm to act similarly
   754	  in many respects since they share most of the same code.
   755	  """
   756	  return _alias_to_platforms.get(platform, [platform])
   757	
   758	
   759	def backends_are_initialized() -> bool:
   760	  "Returns true if backends have already been initialized."
   761	  with _backend_lock:
   762	    return len(_backends) != 0
   763	
   764	
   765	def register_plugin_callbacks(callback):
   766	  """Registers a callback to be called with c_api after plugins discovery.
   767	
   768	  The callback will be called on all discovered PJRT C API plugins. If
   769	  `register_plugin_callbacks` is called before the plugins are discovered, the
   770	  callback will be called right after the plugins are discovered. Otherwise, the
   771	  callback will be called immediately when `register_plugin_callbacks` is
   772	  called.
   773	
   774	  Args:
   775	    callback: the callback to be called with c_api.
   776	  """
   777	  with _plugin_callback_lock:
   778	    if _plugins_registered:
   779	      for factory in _backend_factories.values():
   780	        if factory.c_api is not None:
   781	          callback(c_api=factory.c_api)
   782	    else:
   783	      _plugin_callbacks.append(callback)
   784	
   785	
   786	def backends() -> dict[str, xla_client.Client]:
   787	  global _backends
   788	  global _backend_errors
   789	  global _default_backend
   790	  global _at_fork_handler_installed
   791	
   792	  _discover_and_register_pjrt_plugins()
   793	
   794	  with _backend_lock:
   795	    if _backends:
   796	      return _backends
   797	
   798	    # os.register_at_fork only exists on Unix.
   799	    if not _at_fork_handler_installed and hasattr(os, "register_at_fork"):
   800	      os.register_at_fork(before=_at_fork)
   801	      _at_fork_handler_installed = True
   802	
   803	    if jax_platforms := config.jax_platforms.value:
   804	      platforms = []
   805	      # Allow platform aliases in the list of platforms.
   806	      for platform in jax_platforms.split(","):
   807	        platforms.extend(expand_platform_alias(platform))
   808	      priorities = range(len(platforms), 0, -1)
   809	      # If the user specified a list of platforms explicitly, always fail
   810	      # loudly.
   811	      fail_quietly_list = [False] * len(platforms)
   812	      platform_registrations = list(
   813	        zip(platforms, priorities, fail_quietly_list))
   814	    else:
   815	      platform_registrations = [
   816	          (platform, registration.priority, registration.fail_quietly)
   817	          for platform, registration
   818	          in _backend_factories.items()
   819	      ]
   820	    default_priority = -1000
   200	):
   201	  """Returns a TPU client. Defaults to allowing 32 in-flight computations."""
   202	  if not _jax.pjrt_plugin_loaded('tpu'):
   203	    c_api = xla_client.load_pjrt_plugin_dynamically(
   204	        "tpu", library_path or "libtpu.so"
   205	    )
   206	    _profiler.register_plugin_profiler(c_api)
   207	    assert _jax.pjrt_plugin_loaded('tpu')
   208	  if not _jax.pjrt_plugin_initialized('tpu'):
   209	    _jax.initialize_pjrt_plugin('tpu')
   210	  if options is None:
   211	    options = {}
   212	  return _jax.get_c_api_client(
   213	      "tpu",
   214	      options,
   215	      distributed.global_state.client,
   216	      _make_transfer_server_factory(),
   217	      FORCE_DCN_CROSS_HOST_TRANSFERS.value,
   218	      SORT_DEVICES_BY_PROCESS_INDEX.value,
   219	  )
   220	
   221	
   222	def tpu_client_timer_callback(timer_secs: float) -> xla_client.Client | None:
   223	  def _log_warning():
   224	    warnings.warn(
   225	      f'TPU backend initialization is taking more than {timer_secs} seconds. '
   226	      'Did you run your code on all TPU hosts? '
   227	      'See https://docs.jax.dev/en/latest/multi_process.html '
   228	      'for more information.')
   229	
   230	  # Will log a warning after `timer_secs`.
   231	  t = threading.Timer(timer_secs, _log_warning)
   232	  t.start()
   233	
   234	  try:
   235	    client = make_tpu_client(
   236	        get_tpu_library_path(),
   237	        _options_from_jax_configs("tpu"))
   238	  finally:
   239	    t.cancel()
   240	
   241	  return client
   242	
   243	
   244	# Backends
   245	#
   246	# We have no particular opinion about how "backends" relate to "devices". For
   247	# example, there could be multiple backends that provide the same kind of
   248	# device.
   249	
   250	BackendFactory = Callable[[], xla_client.Client | None]
   251	TopologyFactory = Callable[..., xla_client.DeviceTopology | None]
   252	
   253	@dataclasses.dataclass(slots=True)
   254	class BackendRegistration:
   255	  factory: BackendFactory
   256	
   257	  # Priority of this backend when choosing a default backend. Higher = more
   258	  # preferred.
   259	  priority: int
   260	
   261	  # If this backend fails to initialize, should we log a user-visible warning?
   262	  # For plugins (e.g., TPU) we usually want a visible failure, because why
   263	  # install a plugin if you don't intend it to be used?
   264	  fail_quietly: bool = False
   265	
   266	  # Is this plugin experimental? If a plugin is deemed experimental, we issue
   267	  # a warning when it is initialized. This is mostly to set user expectations
   268	  # correctly: we don't want users to think that JAX is buggy because of a
   269	  # a buggy plugin.
   270	  experimental: bool = False
   271	
   272	  # The C API (`PJRT_Api*`) if this backend is a plugin.
   273	  c_api: Any | None = None
   274	
   275	_backend_factories: dict[str, BackendRegistration] = {}
   276	_default_backend: xla_client.Client | None = None
   277	_backends : dict[str, xla_client.Client] = {}
   278	_backend_errors : dict[str, str] = {}
   279	_backend_lock = threading.Lock()
   280	_plugins_registered: bool = False
   281	_plugin_lock = threading.Lock()
   282	_topology_factories: dict[str, TopologyFactory] = {}
   283	_plugin_callbacks: list[Any] = []
   284	_plugin_callback_lock = threading.Lock()
   285	
   286	# The set of known non-experimental plugins.
   287	#
   288	# If a plugin passes the JAX test suite, it can be added to the allowlist below.
   289	# Send a PR if you would like to be added.
   290	#
   291	# It is fine for a plugin not to implement every feature that JAX uses, provided
   292	# that a reasonable feature set is implemented and the plugin fails gracefully
   293	# for unimplemented features. Wrong outputs are not acceptable.
   294	_nonexperimental_plugins: set[str] = {'cuda', 'rocm'}
   295	
   296	# The set of known experimental plugins that have registrations in JAX codebase.
   297	_experimental_plugins: set[str] = {"oneapi"}
   298	
   299	def register_backend_factory(name: str, factory: BackendFactory, *,
   300	                             priority: int = 0,
   301	                             fail_quietly: bool = True,
   302	                             experimental: bool = False,
   303	                             make_topology: TopologyFactory | None = None,
   304	                             c_api: Any | None = None) -> None:
   305	  with _backend_lock:
   306	    if name in _backends:
   307	      raise RuntimeError(f"Backend {name} already initialized")
   308	  _backend_factories[name] = BackendRegistration(
   309	    factory, priority, fail_quietly, experimental, c_api)
   310	  if make_topology is not None:
   311	    _topology_factories[name] = make_topology
   312	
   313	
   314	def make_cpu_client(
   315	    collectives: _jax.CpuCollectives | None = None,
   316	) -> xla_client.Client:
   317	  """Creates a CPU client with the requested collectives implementation.
   318	
   319	  The implementation of CPU collectives used by the client is determined by the
   320	  flag `--jax_cpu_collectives_implementation` - unless `collectives` is
   321	  provided, in which case the flag is overridden and `collectives` is used.
   322	
   323	  Args:
   324	    collectives: An optional CPU collectives implementation, used by the client
   325	      if provided.
   326	
   327	  Raises:
   328	    RuntimeError: If `--jax_cpu_collectives_implementation` is unknown.
   329	
   330	  Returns:
   331	    The created CPU client.
   332	  """
   333	  # TODO(skyewm): use distributed.is_initialized() after
   334	  # https://github.com/jax-ml/jax/pull/26172 goes in.
   335	  if collectives is None and distributed.global_state.client is not None:
   336	    collectives_impl = config.cpu_collectives_implementation.value
   337	    if collectives_impl == 'gloo':
   338	      collectives = _jax.make_gloo_tcp_collectives(
   339	        distributed_client=distributed.global_state.client,
   340	      )
   341	    elif collectives_impl == 'mpi':
   342	      collectives = _jax.make_mpi_collectives()
   343	      collectives.Init()
   344	      atexit.register(collectives.Finalize)
   345	    else:
   346	      # Already validated by config module
   347	      assert collectives_impl is None
   348	
   349	  num_devices = num_cpu_devices.value if num_cpu_devices.value >= 0 else None
   350	  return xla_client.make_cpu_client(
   351	      asynchronous=_CPU_ENABLE_ASYNC_DISPATCH.value,
   352	      distributed_client=distributed.global_state.client,
   353	      node_id=distributed.global_state.process_id,
   354	      num_nodes=distributed.global_state.num_processes,
   355	      collectives=collectives,
   356	      num_devices=num_devices,
   357	      get_local_topology_timeout_minutes=cpu_get_local_topology_timeout_minutes.value,
   358	      get_global_topology_timeout_minutes=cpu_get_global_topology_timeout_minutes.value,
   359	      transfer_server_factory=_make_transfer_server_factory(),
   360	  )
   361	
   362	
   363	register_backend_factory(
   364	    "cpu", make_cpu_client, priority=0, fail_quietly=False
   365	)
   366	
   367	def get_num_nodes_from_gpu_topology(topology: str) -> int:
   368	    try:
   369	      slices_str, hosts_per_slice_str, _ = topology.split("x", 2)
   370	      return int(slices_str) * int(hosts_per_slice_str)
   371	    except (IndexError, ValueError):
   372	      raise ValueError('Mock topology must be of the form '
   373	                       '"<number-of-slices> x <number-of-hosts-per-slice> x '
   374	                       '<number-of-devices-per-host>".')
   375	
   376	# TODO(phawkins,skyewm): switch TPU plugin to use the PJRT plugin mechanism,
   377	# and then fail loudly on initialization failure.
   378	register_backend_factory(
   379	  'tpu', partial(tpu_client_timer_callback, timer_secs=60.0), priority=300,
   380	  fail_quietly=True)
   381	
   382	
   383	def _get_pjrt_plugin_names_and_library_paths(
   384	    plugins_from_env: str,
   385	) -> dict[str, str]:
   386	  """Gets the names and library paths of PJRT plugins to load from env var.
   387	
   388	  Args:
   389	    plugins_from_env: plugin name and paths from env var. It is in the format
   390	      of 'name1:path1,name2:path2' ('name1;path1,name2;path2' for windows).
   391	
   392	  Returns:
   393	    A dict of {plugin_name: library path} for the PJRT plugins to load.
   394	  """
   395	  if not plugins_from_env:
   396	    return {}
   397	
   398	  pjrt_plugins = {}
   399	  for plugin in plugins_from_env.split(','):
   400	    try:
   401	      name, library_path = plugin.split(os.path.pathsep)
   402	      pjrt_plugins[name] = library_path
   403	    except ValueError:
   404	      logger.warning(
   405	          'invalid value %s in env var PJRT_NAMES_AND_LIBRARY_PATHS %s',
   406	          plugin,
   407	          plugins_from_env,
   408	      )
   409	  return pjrt_plugins
   410	
   411	
   412	def _get_pjrt_plugin_config(
   413	    json_path: str,
   414	) -> tuple[
   415	    str, Mapping[str, str | int | list[int] | float | bool] | None
   416	]:
   417	  """Gets PJRT plugin configuration from a json file.
   418	
   419	  The json file needs to have a "library_path" field for the plugin library
   420	  path. It can have an optional "create_option" field for the options used when
   421	  creating a PJRT plugin client. The value of "create_option" is key-value
   422	  pairs. Please see xla_client._NameValueMapping for the supported types of
   423	  values.
   424	  """
   425	  with open(json_path) as f:
   426	    config = json.load(f)
   427	  if 'library_path' not in config.keys():
   428	    raise ValueError(
   429	        'PJRT plugin config file should contain "library_path" field.'
   430	    )
   431	  return (config['library_path'], config.get('create_options'))
   432	
   433	def discover_pjrt_plugins() -> None:
   434	  """Discovers plugins in the namespace package `jax_plugins` and import them.
   435	
   436	  There are two methods used to discover plugin modules. They are intended
   437	  to be used together by implementers in order to cover all packaging and
   438	  development cases:
   439	
   440	  1. Define a globally unique module under the `jax_plugins` namespace
   441	     package (i.e. just create a `jax_plugins` directory and define your
   442	     module below it).
   443	  2. If building a package via pyproject.toml or setup.py, advertise your
   444	     plugin module name by including an entry-point under the `jax_plugins`
   445	     group which points to your full module name.
   446	
   447	  During Jax startup, Jax will load each module discovered in such a way and
   448	  call its `initialize()` function. It is expected that this function should
   449	  register its concrete plugin name/implementations via call(s) to
   450	  `jax._src.xla_bridge.register_plugin(name, priority=, library_paty=,
   451	  options=)`. Since `initialize()` functions are called for all installed
   452	  plugins, they should avoid doing expensive, non-registration related work.
   453	
   454	  TODO: We should provide a variant of `register_plugin` which allows the
   455	  library_path and options to be resolved via a callback. This would enable
   456	  light-weight plugin registration in cases where options need to be derived
   457	  from heavy-weight system initialization.
   458	  """
   459	  plugin_modules = set()
   460	  # Scan installed modules under |jax_plugins|. Note that not all packaging
   461	  # scenarios are amenable to such scanning, so we also use the entry-point
   462	  # method to seed the list.
   463	  if jax_plugins:
   464	    for _, name, _ in pkgutil.iter_modules(
   465	        jax_plugins.__path__, jax_plugins.__name__ + '.'
   466	    ):
   467	      logger.debug("Discovered path based JAX plugin: %s", name)
   468	      plugin_modules.add(name)
   469	  else:
   470	    logger.debug("No jax_plugins namespace packages available")
   471	
   472	  # Augment with advertised entrypoints.
   473	  from importlib.metadata import entry_points
   474	
   475	  for entry_point in entry_points(group="jax_plugins"):
   476	    logger.debug("Discovered entry-point based JAX plugin: %s",
   477	                 entry_point.value)
   478	    plugin_modules.add(entry_point.value)
   479	
   480	  # Now load and initialize them all.
   481	  for plugin_module_name in plugin_modules:
   482	    logger.debug("Loading plugin module %s", plugin_module_name)
   483	    plugin_module = None
   484	    try:
   485	      plugin_module = importlib.import_module(plugin_module_name)
   486	    except ModuleNotFoundError:
   487	      logger.warning("Jax plugin configuration error: Plugin module %s "
   488	                     "does not exist", plugin_module_name)
   489	    except ImportError:
   490	      logger.exception("Jax plugin configuration error: Plugin module %s "
   491	                       "could not be loaded")
   492	
   493	    if plugin_module:
   494	      try:
   495	        plugin_module.initialize()
   496	      except:
   497	        logger.exception("Jax plugin configuration error: Exception when "
   498	                         "calling %s.initialize()", plugin_module_name)
   499	
   500

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Build jax-cuda13-pjrt from this XLA checkout.
     3	#
     4	# The changed XLA sources (xla/backends/gpu/runtime/ragged_all_to_all*,
     5	# xla/stream_executor/gpu/ragged_all_to_all_device_kernel_lib.cu.h) compile into the GPU PJRT
     6	# plugin, so the PJRT wheel is the only artifact that carries Marin's delta. jax, jaxlib and
     7	# jax-cuda13-plugin are consumed stock from PyPI at JAX_VERSION.
     8	#
     9	# The built wheel must satisfy the stock plugin's exact `jax-cuda13-pjrt==<JAX_VERSION>` pin.
    10	# Under ML_WHEEL_TYPE=release, jax appends ML_WHEEL_VERSION_SUFFIX verbatim, giving
    11	# `<JAX_VERSION>+marin.<sha12>`. PEP 440 ignores a local segment when the specifier has none, so
    12	# that version resolves against `==<JAX_VERSION>`.
    13	#
    14	# usage: XLA_SOURCE=<path> JAX_COMMIT=<sha> JAX_VERSION=<version> \
    15	#        MARIN_XLA_COMMIT=<sha> MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION=<version> OUT_DIR=<path> \
    16	#        [CUDA_COMPUTE_CAPABILITIES=sm_100] [BAZEL_JOBS=4] build_pjrt.sh
    17	set -euo pipefail
    18	
    19	XLA_SOURCE="${XLA_SOURCE:?set XLA_SOURCE (path to this XLA checkout)}"
    20	JAX_COMMIT="${JAX_COMMIT:?set JAX_COMMIT (jax revision to build against)}"
    21	JAX_VERSION="${JAX_VERSION:?set JAX_VERSION (the stock sibling generation)}"
    22	MARIN_XLA_COMMIT="${MARIN_XLA_COMMIT:?set MARIN_XLA_COMMIT (the fork tip being built)}"
    23	MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION="${MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION:?set MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION (the version the preflight computed)}"
    24	OUT_DIR="${OUT_DIR:?set OUT_DIR}"
    25	CUDA_COMPUTE_CAPABILITIES="${CUDA_COMPUTE_CAPABILITIES:-sm_100}"
    26	BAZEL_JOBS="${BAZEL_JOBS:-4}"
    27	PYTHON_VERSION="${PYTHON_VERSION:-3.12}"
    28	CUDA_MAJOR_VERSION="${CUDA_MAJOR_VERSION:-13}"
    29	
    30	XLA_SOURCE="$(cd "$XLA_SOURCE" && pwd)"
    31	mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR"
    32	OUT_DIR="$(cd "$OUT_DIR" && pwd)"
    33	
    34	# The local segment records the exact XLA tip the wheel was built from, so an installed wheel
    35	# names its own provenance without a manifest lookup. Two files compose the version, and under
    36	# ML_WHEEL_TYPE=release both append this suffix verbatim: third_party/py/python_wheel.bzl, which
    37	# names the wheel, and jax's build/build.py, which globs it out of bazel-bin afterwards. The suffix
    38	# therefore has to carry its own '+'.
    39	SHORT_XLA="${MARIN_XLA_COMMIT:0:12}"
    40	WHEEL_VERSION_SUFFIX="+marin.${SHORT_XLA}"
    41	
    42	# Compose it here the way both files will, and refuse a build that disagrees with the preflight.
    43	# The version is otherwise only materialized by the last action of the compile.
    44	COMPOSED="${JAX_VERSION}${WHEEL_VERSION_SUFFIX}"
    45	if [ "$COMPOSED" != "$MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION" ]; then
    46	  echo "this build would make ${COMPOSED}, but the preflight computed ${MARIN_EXPECT_VERSION}" >&2
    47	  exit 1
    48	fi
    49	
    50	work="${WORK_DIR:-$(mktemp -d)}"
    51	mkdir -p "$work"
    52	jax_dir="$work/jax"
    53	
    54	if [ ! -d "$jax_dir/.git" ]; then
    55	  git clone --filter=blob:none https://github.com/jax-ml/jax.git "$jax_dir"
    56	fi
    57	git -C "$jax_dir" fetch --no-tags origin "$JAX_COMMIT"
    58	git -C "$jax_dir" checkout --detach "$JAX_COMMIT"
    59	
    60	echo "xla:  $(git -C "$XLA_SOURCE" rev-parse HEAD)"
    61	echo "jax:  $(git -C "$jax_dir" rev-parse HEAD)"
    62	
    63	# Assert the delta is actually present. A silently-absent patch produces a wheel that looks
    64	# healthy and benchmarks as a null result, which is indistinguishable from the change not helping.
    65	if ! grep -q "kGridSmMultiplier" "$XLA_SOURCE/xla/backends/gpu/runtime/ragged_all_to_all_thunk.h"; then
    66	  echo "XLA checkout does not carry the ragged a2a device-kernel grid delta." >&2
    67	  exit 1
    68	fi
    69	
    70	cd "$jax_dir"
    71	python3 build/build.py build \
    72	  --wheels=jax-cuda-pjrt \
    73	  --cuda_major_version="$CUDA_MAJOR_VERSION" \
    74	  --python_version="$PYTHON_VERSION" \
    75	  --cuda_compute_capabilities="$CUDA_COMPUTE_CAPABILITIES" \
    76	  --local_xla_path="$XLA_SOURCE" \
    77	  --bazel_options=--jobs="$BAZEL_JOBS" \
    78	  --bazel_options=--repo_env=ML_WHEEL_TYPE=release \
    79	  --bazel_options=--repo_env=ML_WHEEL_VERSION_SUFFIX="$WHEEL_VERSION_SUFFIX" \
    80	  --bazel_options=--define=ynn_enable_arm64_neonfp8=false \
    81	  --verbose

    87	  ls -la dist/ >&2
    88	  exit 1

    92	built="$OUT_DIR/$(basename "${wheels[0]}")"

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     3	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     4	
     5	"""Check that the XLA base and the jax revision agree, before the build runs.
     6	
     7	This fork carries an XLA change on the upstream commit that a published jax revision pins. Only the
     8	GPU PJRT plugin contains the change, so the build makes one wheel. Its stock siblings come from
     9	PyPI at the same release.
    10	"""
    11	
    12	import argparse
    13	import json
    14	import sys
    15	import urllib.error
    16	import urllib.request
    17	from pathlib import Path
    18	
    19	MODULE_BAZEL_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/jax-ml/jax/{commit}/MODULE.bazel"
    20	XLA_PREFIX = 'strip_prefix = "xla-'
    21	PYPI_RELEASE_URL = "https://pypi.org/pypi/{distribution}/{version}/json"
    22	SIBLINGS = ("jax", "jaxlib", "jax-cuda13-plugin", "jax-cuda13-pjrt")
    23	
    24	
    25	def note(message: str) -> None:
    26	    """Progress goes to stderr. Stdout carries only the key=value pairs the workflow reads."""
    27	    print(message, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
    28	
    29	
    30	def wheel_version(config: dict, fork_commit: str) -> str:
    31	    """Return the version the build must make.
    32	
    33	    ``jax-cuda13-plugin`` requires ``jax-cuda13-pjrt==<jax_version>``. PEP 440 ignores a local
    34	    segment when the specifier has none. A local segment therefore keeps the wheel installable, and
    35	    also records the XLA commit that the build used.
    36	    """
    37	    return f"{config['jax_version']}+marin.{fork_commit[:12]}"
    38	
    39	
    40	def jax_pinned_xla(jax_commit: str) -> str:
    41	    """Return the XLA commit that a jax revision pins, read from its MODULE.bazel."""
    42	    with urllib.request.urlopen(MODULE_BAZEL_URL.format(commit=jax_commit), timeout=60) as response:
    43	        text = response.read().decode()
    44	    for line in text.splitlines():
    45	        stripped = line.strip()
    46	        if stripped.startswith(XLA_PREFIX):
    47	            return stripped[len(XLA_PREFIX) :].rstrip('",')
    48	    raise SystemExit(f"no XLA strip_prefix found in MODULE.bazel at {jax_commit}")
    49	
    50	
    51	def check_base(config: dict, base: str) -> None:
    52	    """Fail unless the base is the exact XLA commit that ``jax_commit`` pins.
    53	
    54	    jax pins an exact XLA tarball by integrity hash, and builds its dependency graph from XLA module
    55	    extensions. A base that no published jax generation pins can compile, and can then fail at run
    56	    time. The check is symmetric. It fails if the overlay moved, and it fails if the jax revision
    57	    moved.
    58	    """
    59	    pinned = jax_pinned_xla(config["jax_commit"])
    60	    if pinned != base:
    61	        raise SystemExit(
    62	            f"upstream base {base} is not what jax {config['jax_commit'][:12]} pins ({pinned}).\n"
    63	            "Rebase onto the pinned commit, or move jax_commit and jax_version together."
    64	        )
    65	    note(f"base {base[:12]} matches jax {config['jax_commit'][:12]}")
    66	
    67	
    68	def check_siblings_published(config: dict) -> None:
    69	    """Fail unless every stock sibling is released on PyPI at ``jax_version``.
    70	
    71	    A jax revision that was never cut as a release makes a wheel whose siblings nobody can install.
    72	    Without this check that failure appears much later, in a training run.
    73	    """
    74	    version = config["jax_version"]
    75	    missing = []
    76	    for distribution in SIBLINGS:
    77	        url = PYPI_RELEASE_URL.format(distribution=distribution, version=version)
    78	        try:
    79	            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=60) as response:
    80	                note(f"  {response.status}  {distribution} {version}")
    81	        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
    82	            missing.append(f"  {error.code}  {distribution} {version}")
    83	        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError) as error:
    84	            # Do not report a network fault as an absent release. They need different fixes.
    85	            raise SystemExit(f"could not check {url}: {error}") from error
    86	    if missing:
    87	        raise SystemExit(f"jax_version {version} is not fully released on PyPI:\n" + "\n".join(missing))
    88	
    89	
    90	def main() -> None:
    91	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    92	    parser.add_argument("--config", type=Path, required=True)
    93	    parser.add_argument("--fork-commit", required=True)
    94	    parser.add_argument("--base", required=True)
    95	    args = parser.parse_args()
    96	
    97	    config = json.loads(args.config.read_text())
    98	    if config.get("schema_version") != 1:
    99	        raise SystemExit(f"{args.config}: unsupported schema_version {config.get('schema_version')!r}")
   100	
   101	    check_base(config, args.base)
   102	    check_siblings_published(config)
   103	
   104	    print(f"version={wheel_version(config, args.fork_commit)}")
   105	
   106	
   107	if __name__ == "__main__":
   108	    main()

    54	def _get_library_path():
    55	  installed_path = (
    56	      pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'xla_cuda_plugin.so'
    57	  )
    58	  if installed_path.exists():
    59	    return installed_path
    60	
    61	  local_path = os.path.join(
    62	      os.path.dirname(__file__), 'pjrt_c_api_gpu_plugin.so'
    63	  )
    64	  if not os.path.exists(local_path):
    65	    runfiles_dir = os.getenv('RUNFILES_DIR', None)
    66	    if runfiles_dir:
    67	      local_path = os.path.join(
    68	          runfiles_dir, '__main__/jax_plugins/cuda/pjrt_c_api_gpu_plugin.so'
    69	      )
    70

    77	        __package__,
    78	        local_path,

    85	      ' installed or missing src files.',
    86	      installed_path,
    87	      local_path,
    88	      __package__,
    89	  )
    90	  return None

    93	def _load(module, libraries):
    94	  try:
    95	    m = importlib.import_module(f"nvidia.{module}")
    96	  except ImportError:
    97	    m = None
    98	
    99	  for lib in libraries:
   100	    excs = []
   101	    if m is not None:
   102	      path = pathlib.Path(m.__path__[0]) / "lib" / lib
   103	      try:
   104	        ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(path)
   105	        continue
   106	      except OSError as e:
   107	        excs.append(e)
   108	
   109	    # TODO(phawkins): check the non-Python path here and error if not found.
   110	    # # Try again, without the Python module path.
   111	    # try:
   112	    #   ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(lib)
   113	    #   continue
   114	    # except OSError as e:
   115	    #   excs.append(e)
   116	    #

   125	  find these copies."""

   148	def _check_cuda_versions(raise_on_first_error: bool = False,
   149	                         debug: bool = False):
   150	  assert cuda_versions is not None
   151	  results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   152	
   153	  def _make_msg(name: str,
   154	                runtime_version: int,
   155	                build_version: int,
   156	                min_supported: int,
   157	                debug_msg: bool = False):
   158	    if debug_msg:
   159	      return (f"Package: {name}\n"
   160	              f"Version JAX was built against: {build_version}\n"
   161	              f"Minimum supported: {min_supported}\n"
   162	              f"Installed version: {runtime_version}")
   163	    if min_supported:
   164	      req_str = (f"The local installation version must be no lower than "
   165	                 f"{min_supported}.")
   166	    else:
   167	      req_str = ("The local installation must be the same version as "
   168	                 "the version against which JAX was built.")
   169	    msg = (f"Outdated {name} installation found.\n"
   170	           f"Version JAX was built against: {build_version}\n"
   171	           f"Minimum supported: {min_supported}\n"
   172	           f"Installed version: {runtime_version}\n"
   173	           f"{req_str}")
   174	    return msg
   175	
   176	
   177	  def _version_check(name: str,
   178	                     get_version,
   179	                     get_build_version,
   180	                     scale_for_comparison: int = 1,
   181	                     min_supported_version: int = 0) -> int | None:
   182	    """Checks the runtime CUDA component version against the JAX one.
   183	
   184	    Args:
   185	      name: Of the CUDA component.
   186	      get_version: A function to get the local runtime version of the component.
   187	      get_build_version: A function to get the build version of the component.
   188	      scale_for_comparison: For rounding down a version to ignore patch/minor.
   189	      min_supported_version: An absolute minimum version required. Must be
   190	        passed without rounding down.
   191	
   192	    Returns: the runtime version, or None if the component is not found.
   193	
   194	    Raises:
   195	      RuntimeError: If the component is not found, or is of unsupported version,
   196	        and if raising the error is not deferred till later.
   197	    """
   198	
   199	    build_version = get_build_version()
   200	    try:
   201	      version = get_version()
   202	    except Exception as e:
   203	      err_msg = f"Unable to load {name}. Is it installed?"
   204	      if raise_on_first_error:
   205	        raise RuntimeError(err_msg) from e
   206	      err_msg += f"\n{traceback.format_exc()}"
   207	      results.append({"name": name, "installed": False, "msg": err_msg})
   208	      return
   209	
   210	    if not min_supported_version:
   211	      min_supported_version = build_version // scale_for_comparison
   212	    passed = min_supported_version <= version
   213	
   214	    if not passed or debug:
   215	      msg = _make_msg(name=name,
   216	                      runtime_version=version,
   217	                      build_version=build_version,
   218	                      min_supported=min_supported_version,
   219	                      debug_msg=passed)
   220	      if not passed and raise_on_first_error:
   221	        raise RuntimeError(msg)
   222	      else:
   223	        record = {"name": name,
   224	                  "installed": True,
   225	                  "msg": msg,
   226	                  "passed": passed,
   227	                  "build_version": build_version,
   228	                  "version": version,
   229	                  "minimum_supported": min_supported_version}
   230	        results.append(record)
   231	    return version
   232	
   233	  _version_check("CUDA", cuda_versions.cuda_runtime_get_version,
   234	                 cuda_versions.cuda_runtime_build_version,
   235	                 scale_for_comparison=10,
   236	                 min_supported_version=12010)
   237	  cudnn_version = _version_check(
   238	      "cuDNN",
   239	      cuda_versions.cudnn_get_version,
   240	      cuda_versions.cudnn_build_version,
   241	      # NVIDIA promise both backwards and forwards compatibility for cuDNN patch
   242	      # versions:
   243	      # https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/cudnn/backend/latest/developer/forward-compatibility.html#cudnn-api-compatibility
   244	      scale_for_comparison=100,
   245	  )
   246	  _version_check("cuFFT", cuda_versions.cufft_get_version,
   247	                 cuda_versions.cufft_build_version,
   248	                 # Ignore patch versions.
   249	                 scale_for_comparison=100)
   250	  # TODO(phawkins): for some reason this check fails with a cusolver internal
   251	  # error when fetching the version. This may be a path error from our stubs.
   252	  # Figure out what's happening here and re-enable.
   253	  # _version_check("cuSOLVER", cuda_versions.cusolver_get_version,
   254	  #                cuda_versions.cusolver_build_version,
   255	  #                # Ignore patch versions.
   256	  #                scale_for_comparison=100,
   257	  #                min_supported_version=11400)
   258	  _version_check("cuPTI", cuda_versions.cupti_get_version,
   259	                 cuda_versions.cupti_build_version,
   260	                 min_supported_version=18)
   261	  cublas_version = _version_check("cuBLAS", cuda_versions.cublas_get_version,
   262	                 cuda_versions.cublas_build_version,
   263	                 # Ignore patch versions.
   264	                 scale_for_comparison=100,
   265	                 min_supported_version=120100)
   266	  _version_check("cuSPARSE", cuda_versions.cusparse_get_version,
   267	                 cuda_versions.cusparse_build_version,
   268	                 # Ignore patch versions.
   269	                 scale_for_comparison=100,
   270	                 min_supported_version=12100)
   271	
   272	  compute_capabilities = [
   273	      cuda_versions.cuda_compute_capability(d)
   274	      for d in range(cuda_versions.cuda_device_count())
   275	  ]
   276	  # cuBLAS has a known issue whereby many kernels free Tensor Memory multiple
   277	  # times between CUDA versions 12.8 and 13.1 inclusive---making them unsafe
   278	  # to use in scenarios involving several concurrent streams of compute kernels.
   279	  #
   280	  # tcgen05 instructions are available on the sm_100f, and sm_110f
   281	  # architecture families.
   282	  # https://docs.nvidia.com/cuda/parallel-thread-execution/#tcgen05-instructions-tcgen05-alloc-dealloc-relinquish-alloc-permit
   283	  if (cublas_version is not None and 120800 <= cublas_version < 130200 and
   284	      any(100 <= cc < 120 for cc in compute_capabilities)):
   285	    logger.warning(
   286	        f"cuBLAS < 13.2 ({cublas_version} found) has a known issue where "
   287	        "many kernels free TMEM buffers multiple times. Executing a cuBLAS "
   288	        "kernel concurrently with another kernel (e.g. on another stream) can "
   289	        "lead to silent data corruption."
   290	    )
   291	
   292	  # https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/cudnn/backend/latest/release-notes.html#cudnn-9-10-1
   293	  if (cudnn_version is not None and cudnn_version == 91000
   294	      and cuda_versions.cudnn_build_version() != 91000):
   295	    msg = ("cuDNN 9.10.0 had a binary backward-compatibility issue due to reordered enum "
   296	           f"values affecting block-scale datatypes. Found runtime version {cudnn_version} "
   297	           f"and build version {cuda_versions.cudnn_build_version()}. Please upgrade to "
   298	           "9.10.1 or above.")
   299	    if raise_on_first_error:
   300	      raise RuntimeError(msg)
   301	    else:
   302	      results.append({"installed": True, "msg": msg, "passed": False})
   303	  # xb.local_device_count() cannot safely be called at this point
   304	  if xb.CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES.value == "all":
   305	    local_device_count = cuda_versions.cuda_device_count()
   306	  else:
   307	    local_device_count = len(xb.CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES.value.split(","))
   308	  # https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/cudnn/backend/latest/release-notes.html#cudnn-9-10-0
   309	  if (cudnn_version is not None and cudnn_version < 91001
   310	      and cublas_version is not None and cublas_version >= 120900
   311	      and local_device_count > 1):
   312	    msg = (f"cuDNN < 9.10.0 ({cudnn_version} found) had an issue that caused some multi-GPU "
   313	            "matmuls, in which the same finalized execution plan is used across different "
   314	            f"GPUs, to be functionally incorrect when run with cublasLt >= 12.9 ({cublas_version} "
   315	            "found). Please upgrade to 9.10.1 or above.")
   316	    if raise_on_first_error:
   317	      raise RuntimeError(msg)
   318	    else:
   319	      results.append({"installed": True, "msg": msg, "passed": False})
   320

    39	def load_version_module(pkg_path):
    40	  spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    41	    'version', os.path.join(pkg_path, 'version.py'))
    42	  module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    43	  spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    44	  return module
    45

   105	def hero_trainer_config(
   106	    *,
   107	    run_id: str,
   108	    seed: int,
   109	    train_batch_size: int,
   110	    num_train_steps: int,
   111	    profiler: ProfilerConfig,
   112	    tracker: TrackerConfig,
   113	    watch: WatchConfig,
   114	    checkpointer: CheckpointerConfig,
   115	    progress_watchdog: ProgressWatchdogConfig = ProgressWatchdogConfig(),
   116	    load_checkpoint_path: str | list[str] | None = None,
   117	    load_checkpoint: bool | None = None,
   118	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
   119	) -> TrainerConfig:
   120	    """Set the Levanter options that affect the compiled hero step."""
   121	    return TrainerConfig(
   122	        id=run_id,
   123	        seed=seed,
   124	        train_batch_size=train_batch_size,
   125	        num_train_steps=num_train_steps,
   126	        profiler=profiler,
   127	        mp=jmp.get_policy(HERO_MIXED_PRECISION_BY_MASTER_PARAM_MODE[master_param_mode]),
   128	        tracker=tracker,
   129	        watch=watch,
   130	        progress_watchdog=progress_watchdog,
   131	        use_explicit_mesh_axes=True,
   132	        require_accelerator=True,
   133	        allow_nondivisible_batch_size=False,
   134	        load_checkpoint_path=load_checkpoint_path,
   135	        load_checkpoint=load_checkpoint,
   136	        checkpointer=checkpointer,
   137	    )

   139	
   140	def validation_datasets() -> list[ArtifactStep[TokenizedCache]]:
   240	    capacity: int,
   241	    source_rows: int,
   242	    chunk_source: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   243	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   244	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   245	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   246	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   247	) -> Float[Array, "C H"]:
   248	    """Dispatch-direction ``ragged_all_to_all`` into a freshly zeroed receiver buffer.
   249	
   250	    Owning the output-init here (instead of taking a caller-built ``jnp.zeros``) lets both the
   251	    primal and the transposed direction build their inits with `_loop_local_zeros`. jax's
   252	    built-in transpose rule inits the swapped a2a with plain zeros, which XLA hoists out of
   253	    the layer loop and re-copies every iteration; the custom vjp below is that same rule with
   254	    the init construction replaced. Zeros stay load-bearing: receiver rows no peer writes must
   255	    read 0 downstream, and dropped source rows must read a 0 cotangent in the backward.
   256	    """
   257	    del source_rows
   258	    output_init = _loop_local_zeros(capacity, chunk_source.shape[1], chunk_source.dtype, send_sizes, site=1)
   259	    return jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   260	        chunk_source, output_init, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   261	    )
   262	
   263	
   264	def _dispatch_ragged_a2a_fwd(
   265	    capacity: int,
   266	    source_rows: int,
   267	    chunk_source: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   268	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   269	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   270	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   271	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   272	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "C H"], tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array]]:
   273	    result = _dispatch_ragged_a2a(
   274	        capacity, source_rows, chunk_source, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes
   275	    )
   276	    return result, (input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes)
   277	
   278	
   279	def _dispatch_ragged_a2a_bwd(
   280	    capacity: int,
   281	    source_rows: int,
   282	    residuals: tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array],
   283	    cotangent: Float[Array, "C H"],
   284	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "TK H"], None, None, None, None]:
   285	    del capacity
   286	    input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes = residuals
   287	    # The transpose of a ragged all-to-all is the reverse ragged all-to-all: swap the send and
   288	    # recv roles and exchange the offset vectors across shards, exactly as jax's transpose rule
   289	    # does. Source rows that were clipped receive no cotangent and keep the init's zeros.
   290	    output_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(output_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   291	    input_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(input_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   292	    # Tie to this side's send_sizes, not recv_sizes: the return path's passthrough fill ties to
   293	    # its own send_sizes, which is the SAME tensor as this dispatch's recv_sizes, and two fills
   294	    # with identical shape and tie CSE back into one value with a destructive consumer --
   295	    # re-minting exactly the copy this construction removes.
   296	    init = _loop_local_zeros(source_rows, cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, send_sizes, site=2)
   297	    source_ct = jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   298	        cotangent, init, output_offsets_x, recv_sizes, input_offsets_x, send_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   299	    )
   300	    return source_ct, None, None, None, None
   301	
   302	
   303	_dispatch_ragged_a2a.defvjp(_dispatch_ragged_a2a_fwd, _dispatch_ragged_a2a_bwd)
   304	
   305	
   306	@functools.partial(jax.custom_vjp, nondiff_argnums=(0,))
   307	def _return_ragged_a2a(
   308	    capacity: int,
   309	    out_dispatch: Float[Array, "C H"],
   310	    returned: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   311	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   312	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   313	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   314	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   315	) -> Float[Array, "TK H"]:
   316	    """Return-direction ``ragged_all_to_all``: write expert outputs back over ``returned``.
   317	
   318	    Same construction as `_dispatch_ragged_a2a`: the primal is unchanged, and the custom vjp
   319	    replicates jax's transpose rule operation for operation, replacing only the transposed
   320	    a2a's zero init so it is built per-iteration instead of hoisted and copied.
   321	    """
   322	    del capacity
   323	    return jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   324	        out_dispatch, returned, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   325	    )
   326	
   327	
   328	def _return_ragged_a2a_fwd(
   329	    capacity: int,
   330	    out_dispatch: Float[Array, "C H"],
   331	    returned: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   332	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   333	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   334	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   335	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   336	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "TK H"], tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array]]:
   337	    result = _return_ragged_a2a(
   338	        capacity, out_dispatch, returned, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes
   339	    )
   340	    return result, (input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes)
   341	
   342	
   343	def _return_ragged_a2a_bwd(
   344	    capacity: int,
   345	    residuals: tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array],
   346	    cotangent: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   347	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "C H"], Float[Array, "TK H"], None, None, None, None]:
   348	    input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes = residuals
   349	    output_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(output_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   350	    input_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(input_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   351	    init = _loop_local_zeros(capacity, cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, recv_sizes, site=3)
   352	    out_dispatch_ct = jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   353	        cotangent, init, output_offsets_x, recv_sizes, input_offsets_x, send_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   354	    )
   355	    # Rows this a2a overwrote took their value from ``out_dispatch``, so the pass-through
   356	    # cotangent to ``returned`` is zeroed on exactly the written intervals. This mirrors jax's
   357	    # transpose rule op for op (interval marks, cumsum, integer select_n) so the behavior is
   358	    # bit-identical, including on degenerate empty-group offset patterns.
   359	    interval_marks = (
   360	        jnp.zeros(cotangent.shape[0], jnp.int32).at[output_offsets_x].set(1).at[output_offsets_x + recv_sizes].add(-1)
   361	    )
   362	    written = jnp.broadcast_to(jnp.cumsum(interval_marks)[:, None], cotangent.shape)
   363	    passthrough_zero = _loop_local_zeros(cotangent.shape[0], cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, send_sizes, site=4)
   364	    returned_ct = jax.lax.select_n(written, cotangent, passthrough_zero)
   365	    return out_dispatch_ct, returned_ct, None, None, None, None
    82	# Heredoc delimiter for materializing a setup script to disk. Distinctive enough
    83	# that a real setup script will not contain it as a standalone line.
    84	_SETUP_STEP_DELIMITER = "__IRIS_SETUP_STEP__"
    85	
    86	
    87	def render_setup_steps(scripts: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]:
    88	    """Render bash lines that run each setup script as a separate step.
    89	
    90	    Each script is written to its own file and run in a fresh ``bash`` with a
    91	    banner, rather than concatenated, so a failure points at the exact step. The
    92	    caller's ``set -e`` stops the sequence on the first non-zero step.
    93	    """
    94	    lines: list[str] = []
    95	    total = len(scripts)
    96	    for index, script in enumerate(scripts, start=1):
    97	        step_file = f"/tmp/iris-setup-step-{index}.sh"
    98	        lines.append(f"cat > {step_file} <<'{_SETUP_STEP_DELIMITER}'")
    99	        lines.append(script.rstrip("\n"))
   100	        lines.append(_SETUP_STEP_DELIMITER)
   101	        lines.append(f'echo "[iris setup] step {index}/{total}"')
   102	        lines.append(f"bash {step_file}")
   103	    return lines
   104

   110	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid workdir file path (absolute paths are not allowed): {path}")
   111	    normalized = posixpath.normpath(candidate)
   112	    if normalized in {"", "."}:

    17	def build_runtime_entrypoint(
    18	    entrypoint: Entrypoint,
    19	    env_config: job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig,
    20	) -> job_pb2.RuntimeEntrypoint:
    21	    """Build a RuntimeEntrypoint from a user Entrypoint + env config.
    22	
    23	    Assembles ``setup_commands`` as the user's resolved scripts followed by iris's
    24	    runtime-deps script. The run_command is the user's original command, kept
    25	    separate so runtimes that don't need setup can skip it cleanly.
    26	    """
    27	    rt = job_pb2.RuntimeEntrypoint()
    28	    # Drop whitespace-only scripts; an empty user list means no setup at all, so the
    29	    # build phase (iris script included) is skipped and the command runs as-is.
    30	    user_scripts = [s for s in env_config.setup_scripts if s.strip()]
    31	    if user_scripts:
    32	        iris_script = iris_runtime_setup_script()
    33	        rt.setup_commands[:] = [*user_scripts, iris_script]
    34	    rt.run_command.argv[:] = entrypoint.command
    35	    for k, v in entrypoint.workdir_files.items():
    36	        rt.workdir_files[k] = v
    37	    for k, v in entrypoint.workdir_file_refs.items():
    38	        rt.workdir_file_refs[k] = v

   188	def iris_runtime_setup_script() -> str:
   189	    """Render the script that installs iris's own runtime deps into ``$IRIS_VENV``.
   190	
   191	    Installs cloudpickle (callable entrypoints) and py-spy/memray (the profiler)
   192	    so iris features work without the user declaring them. Best-effort: skipped
   193	    unless a venv exists (a bring-your-own image is left untouched) and a failed
   194	    install only warns, so it never fails the job.
   195	    """
   196	    pkgs = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in _IRIS_RUNTIME_DEPS)
   197	    pip_cmd = " ".join(["uv pip install", "--link-mode symlink", pkgs])
   198	    return (
   199	        'cd "$IRIS_WORKDIR" 2>/dev/null || true\n'
   200	        'if [ -d "$IRIS_VENV" ]; then\n'
   201	        "  echo 'installing iris runtime deps'\n"
   202	        f"  {pip_cmd} || echo '[iris setup] runtime deps install failed; continuing'\n"
   203	        "fi\n"
   204	    )
   737	    Setup:
   738	    - ``setup_scripts=None`` builds the default uv-sync script. ``sync_packages``
   739	      scopes that sync to specific workspace members (default: all members).
   740	    - ``setup_scripts`` set to a list runs those scripts verbatim before the
   741	      command, with the task's ``IRIS_*`` env available; ``[]`` means no setup (the
   742	      image is used as-is). Build the default and tweak it via
   743	      ``iris.cluster.setup_scripts.default_setup_script``.
   744	
   745	    Whenever any setup runs (default or custom), iris appends its own
   746	    ``iris_runtime_setup_script`` so cloudpickle/profiler support is always
   747	    present; it is skipped only for the no-setup (``[]``) case.
   748	
   749	    Note: To specify workspace for bundle creation, use IrisClient.remote(workspace=...).
   750	    """
   751	
   752	    pip_packages: Sequence[str] | None = None
   753	    env_vars: dict[str, str] | None = None
   754	    extras: Sequence[str] | None = None
   755	    setup_scripts: Sequence[str] | None = None
   756	    sync_packages: Sequence[str] | None = None
   757	
   758	    def to_proto(self) -> job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig:
   759	        """Convert to wire format, resolving the user setup scripts.
   760	
   761	        ``setup_scripts=None`` builds the default uv-sync script from
   762	        extras/pip/sync_packages; a list is used verbatim; ``[]`` is no setup. The
   763	        wire carries only this user list.
   764	        """
   765	        default_env_vars = {
   766	            "HF_DATASETS_TRUST_REMOTE_CODE": "1",
   767	            "TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM": "false",
   768	            "HF_TOKEN": os.getenv("HF_TOKEN"),
   769	            "WANDB_API_KEY": os.getenv("WANDB_API_KEY"),
   770	            # Launch provenance: a task running from a git-less bundle inherits the
   771	            # submitter's identity via Provenance.capture(); transitive, since a task
   772	            # re-submitting captures this same env value.
   773	            LAUNCH_PROVENANCE_ENV: launch_provenance().to_json(),
   774	        }
   775	        if wants_gpu_extra(self.extras or ()):
   776	            default_env_vars.update(
   777	                {
   778	                    NCCL_RAS_ENABLE_ENV: "1",
   779	                    "NCCL_DEBUG": "INFO",
   780	                    "NCCL_DEBUG_SUBSYS": "INIT,BOOTSTRAP,ENV,NET,GRAPH,TUNING,RAS",
   781	                    "NCCL_DEBUG_TIMESTAMP": "[%F %T.%3f]",
   782	                }
   783	            )
   784	
   785	        merged_env_vars = {k: v for k, v in {**default_env_vars, **(self.env_vars or {})}.items() if v is not None}
   786	
   787	        if self.setup_scripts is None:
   788	            py_version = f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}"
   789	            extras = list(self.extras or [])
   790	            setup_scripts = [
   791	                default_setup_script(
   792	                    extras=extras,
   793	                    pip_packages=list(self.pip_packages or []),
   794	                    python_version=py_version,
   795	                    packages=list(self.sync_packages or []) or None,
   796	                )
   797	            ]
   798	            # GPU jobs need the venv's CUDA toolchain (ptxas/nvlink/libdevice)
   799	            # exposed for JAX/Pallas Mosaic; the script no-ops without it.
   800	            if wants_gpu_extra(extras):
   801	                setup_scripts.append(cuda_toolchain_setup_script())
   802	        else:
   803	            setup_scripts = [s for s in self.setup_scripts if s.strip()]
   804	
   805	        return job_pb2.EnvironmentConfig(env_vars=merged_env_vars, setup_scripts=setup_scripts)
   530	def _build_task_script(run_req: job_pb2.RunTaskRequest) -> str:
   531	    """Build a shell script that runs the setup steps then the run_command."""
   532	    lines = ["set -e", "ulimit -c 0", "mkdir -p /app", "cd /app"]
   533	    lines.extend(render_setup_steps(run_req.entrypoint.setup_commands))
   534	    # Activate the venv the setup script populated. Conditional on it existing so
   535	    # a custom or no-setup script that brings its own environment runs as-is.
   536	    lines.append('[ -f "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate" ] && source "$IRIS_VENV/bin/activate"')
   537	    if run_req.entrypoint.run_command.argv:
   538	        lines.append("exec " + shlex.join(run_req.entrypoint.run_command.argv))
   539	    return "\n".join(lines)
   540	
    19	Each child inherits the supervisor's environment plus its rank::
    20	
    21	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_COUNT    = num_tasks * nproc           (global world size)
    22	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX    = task_index * nproc + local  (global rank)
    23	    IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS = the child's D device ids     ("0", or "2,3")
    24	
    25	``iris.runtime.jax_init.initialize_jax`` reads these (their names are the contract
    26	this module defines) and joins the JAX mesh. The names are iris-private (not the
    27	JAX_*/framework namespace) so a job that already sets JAX rank vars never trips
    28	the supervised path.
    29	
    30	The supervisor owns child lifecycle: it forwards SIGINT/SIGTERM to every child,
    31	tears the group down and exits non-zero if any child fails, and tags each
    32	child's output with its local rank, keeping stdout and stderr apart so iris
    33	still classifies each line by its stream. It deliberately does not import jax — the
    34	children initialize CUDA only after the supervisor has already spawned them, so
    35	no CUDA context exists in the supervisor's address space.
   108	def _spawn_children(
   109	    nproc: int, devices_per_proc: int, task_index: int, num_tasks: int, child_argv: list[str]
   110	) -> tuple[list[subprocess.Popen], list[threading.Thread]]:
   111	    """Spawn all children, or SIGKILL any that started and re-raise on failure.
   112	
   113	    A partial spawn (rank 0 up, rank 3 fails on ``EAGAIN`` / a missing binary /
   114	    thread-creation failure) would otherwise leave the started ranks orphaned,
   115	    holding GPUs after the supervisor exits.
   116	    """
   117	    children: list[subprocess.Popen] = []
   118	    pumps: list[threading.Thread] = []
   119	    write_lock = threading.Lock()
   120	    try:
   121	        for local_rank in range(nproc):
   122	            rank_env = _child_rank_env(local_rank, nproc, devices_per_proc, task_index, num_tasks)
   123	            child_env = {**os.environ, **rank_env}
   124	            logger.info(
   125	                "launch local rank %d: %s=%s %s=%s",
   126	                local_rank,
   127	                IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV,
   128	                rank_env[IRIS_MULTIGPU_PROCESS_INDEX_ENV],
   129	                IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV,
   130	                rank_env[IRIS_MULTIGPU_LOCAL_DEVICE_IDS_ENV],
   131	            )
   132	            child = subprocess.Popen(
   133	                child_argv,
   134	                env=child_env,
   135	                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   136	                stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   137	                text=True,
   138	                bufsize=1,
   139	            )
   140	            children.append(child)
   141	            tag = rank_log_tag(local_rank)
   142	            for source, sink in ((child.stdout, sys.stdout), (child.stderr, sys.stderr)):
   143	                pump = threading.Thread(target=_pump_output, args=(tag, source, sink, write_lock), daemon=True)
   144	                pump.start()
   145	                pumps.append(pump)
   208	    first_failure: int | None = None
   209	    pending = set(range(nproc))
   210	    while pending:
   211	        # Escalate to SIGKILL if a requested teardown overran its grace period,
   212	        # so a child that traps or ignores SIGTERM cannot wedge the supervisor.
   213	        if kill_deadline is not None and kill_deadline.expired():
   214	            survivors = [r for r in pending if children[r].poll() is None]
   215	            if survivors:
   216	                logger.error("SIGTERM grace expired; SIGKILL %d straggler(s): %s", len(survivors), survivors)
   217	                _signal_children(children, survivors, signal.SIGKILL)
   218	            kill_deadline = None  # SIGKILL is final; do not re-fire each tick
   219	        for local_rank in list(pending):
   220	            child = children[local_rank]
   221	            try:
   222	                code = child.wait(timeout=_REAP_POLL_INTERVAL)
   223	            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   224	                continue
   225	            pending.discard(local_rank)
   226	            logger.info("local rank %d exited with code %d", local_rank, code)
   227	            if code != 0 and first_failure is None:
   228	                first_failure = code
   229	            # A failure tears the whole group down: a collective job cannot make
   230	            # progress once one rank is gone. A clean exit does not — peers run to
   231	            # their own completion.
   232	            if code != 0 and not terminating.is_set():
   233	                terminating.set()
   234	                logger.error("local rank %d failed (code %d); terminating %d peer(s)", local_rank, code, len(pending))
   235	                _signal_children(children, pending, signal.SIGTERM)
   236	                kill_deadline = Deadline.from_now(_TERMINATE_GRACE)
   237	
   238	    for pump in pumps:
   239	        pump.join(timeout=5.0)
   240	
   241	    # An external signal wins over child exit codes: the children we forwarded it
   242	    # to report negative (signal) codes that are an artifact of the teardown, not
   243	    # a task failure. A child failing on its own never sets shutdown_signum, so
   244	    # its genuine non-zero code surfaces.
   245	    if shutdown_signum is not None:
   246	        return 128 + shutdown_signum
   247	    if first_failure is not None:
   248	        return first_failure
   249	    return 0
